# PART 07 — TONE 7: SUNDAY AND WEEKDAY OFFICES (SATURDAY VESPERS THROUGH SATURDAY MATINS)

*[Engraved Headpiece: An ornamental baroque frieze depicting the Most Pure Theotokos holding the Pre-eternal Christ Child, surrounded by holy prophets, angels, and foliate cartouches, marking the beginning of Tone 7]*[^7954]

*On Saturday Evening.*
*At Little Vespers.*
*On "Lord, I have cried," we set 4 verses, and sing 3 Resurrection Stichera, repeating the first.*
*Tone 7:*

*Sticheron 1 (Resurrectional):* Come, let us rejoice in the Lord, Who shattered the dominion of death and enlightened the human race, crying aloud with the Bodiless Powers: O our Creator and Savior, glory to Thee! *(Twice)*[^7955]

*Sticheron 2 (Resurrectional):* Thou didst endure the Cross and burial for our sake, O Savior, and by death, as God, Thou didst put death to death; therefore we worship Thy third-day Resurrection: O Lord, glory to Thee![^7956]

*Sticheron 3 (Resurrectional):* Beholding the Resurrection of the Creator, the Apostles were astonished, singing the angelic praise: this is the glory of the Church, this is the richness of the Kingdom! O Thou Who didst suffer for our sake: O Lord, glory to Thee![^7957]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion (Dogmatikon), Tone 7:* Truly dread and ineffable is the mystery wrought in thee, O Immaculate One! The Word Who is the Cause of all, above cause and word, being incarnate by the Holy Spirit, thou didst bear, taking flesh of thee, remaining unchangeably in His own nature; for when both came together subsistently in a single Hypostasis, twofold in nature He proceeds: perfect God and perfect man, manifest in both perfections by active properties; for having suffered upon the Cross in the flesh, He remained impassible in His Godhead; dying as man, as God He lived again on the third day, casting down the dominion of death, and delivering mankind from corruption. Him, as the Deliverer and Savior of our race, O Mother of God, do thou entreat, to send down upon us the great mercy of His tender compassions.[^7958]

*O Gladsome Light. Prokeimenon: "The Lord is King," three times. Vouchsafe, O Lord.*[^7959]

*At the Aposticha, Resurrection Sticheron, Tone 7:* Risen from the tomb, O Savior of the world, Thou didst raise up men together with Thy flesh: O Lord, glory to Thee![^7960]

*Other Stichera, to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Other Stichera, to the Most Holy Theotokos, Tone 7.*
*Tone 7, Podoben: "Having disregarded temporal things" (Неради́вше ѡ҆ вре́менныхъ):*

*Verse: I will remember thy name in every generation and generation [LXX Ps. 44:18].*[^7961]

*Sticheron 1:* Thou wast the East of the noetic Sun, O Virgin, in the setting of our nature; but as thou hast boldness, entreat Him, O all-hymned Mother of God: to free our souls from immeasurable transgressions.[^7962]

*Verse: Hear, O daughter, and see, and incline thine ear [LXX Ps. 44:11].*[^7963]

*Sticheron 2:* Clearly didst thou bud forth, O Virgin, as a rod from the root of Jesse, which destroyed the plant of deceit from the root; but as thou hast boldness, unceasingly pray, O All-hymned One, to uproot the passions of my heart, and to plant therein His fear, and to save me.[^7964]

*Verse: The rich among the people shall entreat thy countenance [LXX Ps. 44:13].*[^7965]

*Sticheron 3:* O all-holy Gate of God! Deliver me from the gates of hades, and show me the path of repentance, turning away from which the gates lead unto life; O guide of the lost, preserve the race of faithful men, and save our souls.[^7966]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:* From thee, O all-holy Virgin Mother of God, was Christ our God ineffably born, truly being the pre-eternal God and a new man: being on the one hand eternal, and on the other having become man for our sake; for by Himself He saves the property of each nature: on the one hand shining forth in miracles, and on the other confirming by passions. Therefore being one and the same, He both dies as man and rises as God; entreat Him, O pure unwedded One, that our souls may be saved.[^7967]

*Then, Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart: Trisagion, and after Our Father: Troparion: "Thou didst shatter death by Thy Cross." Theotokion: "As the treasure of our resurrection." Litany and Dismissal.*[^7968]

*[Engraved Vignette: Small ornamental foliate tailpiece] On Saturday at Great Vespers.*
*On "Lord, I have cried," we set 10 verses, and sing Resurrection Stichera, Tone 7.*
*(Composition of John of Damascus):*[^7969]

*Sticheron 1 (Resurrectional):* Come, let us rejoice in the Lord, Who shattered the dominion of death and enlightened the human race, crying aloud with the Bodiless Powers: O our Creator and Savior, glory to Thee![^7970]

*Sticheron 2 (Resurrectional):* Thou didst endure the Cross and burial for our sake, O Savior, and by death, as God, Thou didst put death to death; therefore we worship Thy third-day Resurrection: O Lord, glory to Thee![^7971]

*Sticheron 3 (Resurrectional):* Beholding the Resurrection of the Creator, the Apostles were astonished, singing the angelic praise: this is the glory of the Church, this is the richness of the Kingdom! O Thou Who didst suffer for our sake: O Lord, glory to Thee![^7972]

*Other Stichera, Eastern (by Anatolius), in the same Tone:*

*Sticheron 4:* Even though Thou wast seized, O Christ, by lawless men, yet Thou art my God, and I am not ashamed; Thou wast struck upon the shoulders, and I deny Thee not; Thou wast nailed upon the Cross, and I conceal it not; in Thy Resurrection do I boast, for Thy death is my life. O Almighty Lord and Lover of mankind, glory to Thee![^7973]

*Sticheron 5:* Fulfilling David's prophecy, Christ revealed His majesty in Zion unto the disciples, showing Himself praised and ever glorified, with the Father and the Holy Spirit: first indeed bodiless as the Word, and afterwards incarnate for our sake, and put to death as man, and risen with power as the Lover of mankind.[^7974]

*Sticheron 6:* Thou didst descend into hades, O Christ, even as Thou didst will; Thou didst overthrow death, as God and Master; and Thou didst rise on the third day, raising Adam with Thyself from the bonds of hades and corruption...[^7975]

...crying aloud and saying: Glory to Thy Resurrection, O only Lover of mankind![^7976]

*Sticheron 7:* Thou wast laid in the tomb as one asleep, O Lord, and Thou didst rise on the third day as mighty in strength, raising Adam with Thyself from mortal corruption, as Thou art Almighty.[^7977]

*Other Stichera, to the Most Holy Theotokos (by Paul of Amorium), Tone 7.*[^7978]
*Tone 7, Podoben: "When from the Tree" (Є҆гда̀ ѿ дре́ва):*

*Sticheron 8 (Theotokion 1):* Thou art the joy of Angels, thou art the glory of men, thou art the hope of the faithful, O all-hymned Lady, our protectress; and unto thee do we flee in every need, that by thy supplications we all who hymn thee, O Bride of God, may be delivered from the soul-destroying arrows of the enemy and from all afflictions.[^7979]

*Sticheron 9 (Theotokion 2):* Thou art my hope, O Mother of God, thou art my protectress, and a wall and a refuge, O all-hymned Lady, who didst bear God in the flesh without man, the Savior of the world; do thou by thy supplications deliver us from the temptations, dangers, and distresses that encompass us, O only refuge of Christians.[^7980]

*Sticheron 10 (Theotokion 3):* Subdue the surgings of my flesh, quench the flame of my passions, take away from me, O Mother of God, the wicked burning of my desire; and turn my stubborn habit, O Virgin, from every demonic impulse, that in quietness of heart and in dispassion of my soul I may hymn thee, the all-hymned.[^7981]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion (Dogmatikon):* Thou wast known as a Mother above nature, O Mother of God, yet thou didst remain a virgin above word and reason; and the wonder of thy childbirth no tongue can tell; for since thy conceiving was all-glorious, O Pure One, incomprehensible is the manner of thy giving birth; for where God wills, the order of nature is overcome. Therefore, knowing thee to be the Mother of God, we all pray thee fervently: entreat Him that our souls may be saved.[^7982]

*O Gladsome Light. Prokeimenon of the day, and the rest as usual.*[^7983]

*At the Aposticha, Resurrection Stichera, Tone 7:*
*Sticheron 1:* Risen from the tomb, O Savior of the world, Thou didst raise up men together with Thy flesh: O Lord, glory to Thee![^7984]

*Other Stichera (Alphabetical):*
*Verse: The Lord is King, He is robed in majesty [LXX Ps. 92:1].*[^7985]

*Sticheron 2:* Come, let us worship Him Who rose from the dead and enlightened all things; for from the tyranny of hades He delivered us by His third-day Resurrection, granting us life and great mercy.[^7986]

*Verse: For He hath established the world, so that it cannot be moved [LXX Ps. 92:1].*[^7987]

*Sticheron 3:* Having descended into hades, O Christ, Thou didst lead captivity captive; and having risen on the third day, Thou didst raise us with Thyself, who glorify Thine all-powerful rising, O Lord and Lover of mankind.[^7988]

*Verse: Holiness becomes Thy house, O Lord, unto length of days [LXX Ps. 92:5].*[^7989]

*Sticheron 4:* Thou didst appear awesome, O Lord, lying in the tomb as one asleep; but having risen on the third day as mighty, Thou didst raise Adam with Thyself, who cried: Glory to Thy Resurrection, O only Lover of mankind![^7990]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:* Beneath thy protection, O Lady, do all we earth-born flee, crying aloud unto thee: O Mother of God, our hope, deliver us from immeasurable transgressions, and save our souls.[^7991]

*Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart: Trisagion, and after Our Father: we sing the Resurrectional Troparion, Tone 7:*
*Resurrectional Troparion, Tone 7:* Thou didst destroy death by Thy Cross, Thou didst open paradise to the thief, Thou didst change the weeping of the Myrrh-bearers, and didst command Thine Apostles to preach: that Thou art risen, O Christ God, granting unto the world great mercy.[^7992]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:* As the treasure of our resurrection, O All-hymned One, lead up from the pit and the depth of transgressions those who hope in thee; for thou hast saved those guilty of sin, having given birth to our Salvation: who before childbirth wast a Virgin, and in childbirth a Virgin, and after childbirth still remainest a Virgin.[^7993]

*[Engraved Vignette: Ornamental foliate divider]*

*On Saturday at Compline.*
*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos, Tone 7.*
*Ode 1. Heirmos:* Unto Him Who shattered warfare with His arm, and submerged the three-tiered chariot riders, let us sing as unto our Deliverer God, for He hath been glorified.[^7994]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*[^7995]

*Troparion 1:* As an adornment with singing, and a God-befitting song of thanksgiving, we bring gifts unto thee: the "Rejoice," now, O Pure One, for from sorrow thou hast bestowed joy upon us.[^7996]

*Troparion 2:* The grace of thy mercy and the strength of thy protection we will not keep silent, O most pure Virgin; for from cruel distresses thou hast saved us.[^7997]

*Troparion 3:* Having been delivered from various temptations and afflictions by thy maternal prayers, O Most Pure One, with one accord and fervently we all sing songs of thanksgiving unto thee.[^7998]

*Both now:* Variously adorned with fringes, as with golden virtues and with the graces of the Spirit, O Most Pure One, having adorned thyself as the Bride of the Father, thou hast truly been shown forth as the Mother of God.[^7999]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* O Lord and Savior, Who in the beginning didst establish the heavens by Thy all-powerful Word, and all the host thereof by the all-effecting and Divine Spirit: establish me upon the unshakable rock of the confession of Thee.[^8000]

*Troparion 1:* Diligently do we offer voices of thanksgiving unto the majesty of divine joy, as unto the cause of our gladness from the beginning, honoring her as our protectress.[^8001]

*Troparion 2:* Having been delivered from distresses through thee, and having obtained joy for thy sake, O unwedded Mother, we all glorify thee as the good bestower and lauded protectress.[^8002]

*Glory:* Receiving the transformation of our transgressions and adversities through thy divine prayer, O Mother of Christ God, we faithfully hymn Thee with thankful voices, as the cause of good things.[^8003]

*Both now:* She who is the fountain of joy ever pours forth streams of immortality, and herself saves all, as an ever-living thanksgiving unto us, the Mother of Christ God.[^8004]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* The virtue of Thine ineffable wisdom, O Christ God, hath covered the heavens by Thy dispensation, O Lover of mankind.[^8005]

*Troparion 1:* Rejoicing, we offer unto thee a worthy song of rejoicing, O pure Virgin, having been delivered from distresses by thy prayers.[^8006]

*Troparion 2:* With thanksgiving, O pure Virgin, we bring forth a hymn unto thee with hands of the soul, leaping for joy with divine songs, having been delivered from much sorrow.[^8007]

*Glory:* Passionate sins have raised up many distresses against us; but by thy divine protection, O Pure One, deliver us.[^8008]

*Both now:* Blessed truly are they that honor thee, O most pure Mother of God, for through thee have we been delivered from sin and sorrow.[^8009]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* Night without light is unto the unbelieving, O Christ, but illumination unto the faithful in the sweetness of Thy words; therefore I rise early at dawn unto Thee, and hymn Thy Divinity.[^8010]

*Troparion 1:* Thou didst give birth, O Virgin, unto Christ the consumer of sin, by Whom the world was saved from adversities and sicknesses;...[^8011]

...therefore, "Rejoice!" we also cry unto thee, having been delivered from sorrows.[^8012]

*Troparion 2:* Encompassed by various adversities, O pure Sovereign Lady, and by sorrow and affliction and grievous circumstance, in our despair we found thee as the hope of gladness.[^8013]

*Glory:* As a treasury of salvation for us thy servants, O Pure One, thou drivest away adversities and keepest us unharmed; therefore, partakers of thy many good things, we thank thee with songs.[^8014]

*Both now:* We thank thee, having been delivered through thee from many sins, and weaknesses and sicknesses, and grievous ailments, O most pure Sovereign Lady; for thou art a steadfast hope unto thy faithful servants.[^8015]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Fallen into the depth of sins, O Good One, as Jonah from the sea monster I cry aloud unto Thee: Lead up my life from corruption, and save me, O Lover of mankind.[^8016]

*Troparion 1:* Angelic tongues cannot worthily sing thy praises, O Pure One; yet we now, undertaking as servants, bring Gabriel's greeting unto thee.[^8017]

*Troparion 2:* Having fallen into the depth of sorrow and tribulation through our sins, we have been delivered through thee from distress and adversities, O Virgin, pure Mother of God.[^8018]

*Glory:* The whole world hath been made debtor, O Pure One, to give thanks, and praise, and piously glorify thy grace; for through thee have we been delivered from perils and sorrows.[^8019]

*Both now:* Both in night and in day, openly and in secret, we who faithfully glorify thee flee beneath thy protection, O most pure Virgin.[^8020]

*Lord, have mercy, three times. Glory... both now...*[^8021]

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 7:* O Lord, we are Thy people, and the sheep of Thy pasture: as a Shepherd turn us who have gone astray; gather us who have been scattered by corruption; have mercy on Thy flock, O Lover of mankind; have compassion on Thy people, through the prayers of the Mother of God, O Thou Who alone art without sin.[^8022]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* Cast into the fiery furnace, the venerable Children turned the fire into dew, crying aloud thus with singing: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God of our fathers.[^8023]

*Troparion 1:* A thankful rejoicing do we bring unto thee, O Mother of God, for truly through thee have we been delivered from every evil undertaking; and with one accord we sing unto thee: Blessed art thou.[^8024]

*Troparion 2:* At evening we took up our abode in weeping of sorrows, and in expectation of evils: but by thy God-befitting shelter, O Virgin, having been deified, we found joy in the morning; for thou hast saved us.[^8025]

*Glory:* Having all acquired thy protection toward God as a divine refuge, in adversities and persecutions and sins we flee unto thee, and through thee receive transformation, O Most Pure One.[^8026]

*Both now:* The grace of thy prayer, O Pure One, we proclaim with lips and with spirit, O Glorious One: for for thy sake are we all delivered from adversities and tempest and grievous sorrows, and the passions of sin.[^8027]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* Him Who is awesome unto the Cherubim, and marvelous unto the Seraphim, and the Creator of the world: ye priests and servants, and spirits of the righteous, sing, bless, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^8028]

*Troparion 1:* Having been delivered from the tempest of sin, and from passions, and from adversities by thy prayers, O good Mother of God, with a thankful voice we cry aloud unto thee: "Rejoice!" for through thee have we passed from sorrow into joy.[^8029]

*Troparion 2:* Despise not those held fast in sicknesses and adversities, O Good One: but giving ear unto our poor supplication, free us from great afflictions, that we may faithfully hymn thy mercy, O Pure One.[^8030]

*Glory:* O cause of the dissolution of transgressions, raise us up now from sorrows, adversities, and passions of men, and from unseemly temptation; and by thy divine supplications, O Mother of God, most gloriously deliver us from them.[^8031]

*Both now:* Thy compassions are ever sent down upon all, O Christ, truly by grace and by the supplications of her that bare Thee; for from Thee, O merciful Savior, do we Christians receive Thy mercy.[^8032]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* Her that is a Mother above nature, and a Virgin according to nature, the only blessed one among women, let us the faithful magnify with songs as the Mother of God.[^8033]

*Troparion 1:* A worthy rejoicing, with a voice of acclamation, do we now offer unto thee, O Virgin, with the Angel...[^8034]

...Gabriel, having been delivered from various temptations, O Mother of God, by thy prayers.[^8035]

*Troparion 2:* Divine joy and gladness and exultation, O Virgin that knewest not man, hath multiplied unto us from God; for lo, we who weep grievously rejoice by thy prayers.[^8036]

*Glory:* I will sacrifice a sacrifice of praise, and with tongue and voice diligently render a song of thanksgiving unto thee, O Virgin, that praying unto thee, I may be delivered through thee in the day of affliction.[^8037]

*Both now:* We piously rejoice together, O Most Pure One, in thy divine Childbearing; for thou hast gushed forth joy for us from adversities and sorrows. Therefore unto thee do we also thankfully accomplish a hymn, faithfully praising thee.[^8038]

*It is truly meet: Trisagion Prayers, Our Father: Troparia and the rest.*[^8039]

*On Sunday Morning at the Midnight Office.*[^8040]
*Canon to the Most Holy and Life-Originating Trinity. (The acrostic whereof is: I praise the Trinity, the monarchical nature.) A composition of Metrophanes.*[^8041]
*Tone 7. Ode 1. Heirmos:* By Thy nod the watery nature, formerly flowing easily, was transformed into dry land, O Lord. Therefore Israel, having journeyed on foot dry-shod, sings unto Thee a song of victory.[^8042]

*Refrain: Most Holy Trinity our God, glory to Thee.*[^8043]

*Troparion 1:* Open Thou the noetic mouth of my heart, and my material lips unto Thy praise, O thrice-radiant single Godhead of all: to sing unto Thee, O Author of Light, a song of thanksgiving.[^8044]

*Troparion 2:* That Thou mightest show forth the superabundant riches of Thy goodness, O Trinity of immeasurable power, Thou didst create man, an earthly image of Thy sovereign Lordship, O Creator.[^8045]

*Troparion 3:* O Unoriginate Mind, Who didst beget the co-ever-existing Word, and didst cause to shine forth the co-unoriginate Spirit: vouchsafe us to worship conformably the One God by essence, in three Hypostases.[^8046]

*Theotokion:* Thou didst appear unto Moses at the bush, O Word of God, as a cleansing fire that consumed not at all, prefiguring Thine incarnation, whereby Thou didst fashion men anew.[^8047]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* O Lord and Savior, Who in the beginning didst establish the heavens by Thy all-powerful Word, and all the host thereof by the all-effecting and Divine Spirit: establish me upon the unshakable rock of the confession of Thee...[^8048]

...and all the host thereof by the all-effecting and Divine Spirit: establish me upon the unshakable rock of the confession of Thee.[^8049]

*Troparion 1:* Hymning Thee, the single, thrice-radiant, and all-effecting Master, we ask deliverance from sins and temptations, O God of immeasurable power: therefore despise not those who praise Thy goodness in faith.[^8050]

*Troparion 2:* A branch hath appeared from the Father, as from an unoriginate root, God the Word, equal in power with the co-sprouting and Divine Spirit; and therefore we the faithful glorify the one Lordship in threefold Persons.[^8051]

*Troparion 3:* Equally glorified and co-growing, the tri-hypostatic nature, undividedly and dividedly, and the monarchical Trinity, all we faithful praise, and bowing down we ask forgiveness of transgressions.[^8052]

*Theotokion:* Unchangeably likened unto men in all things, O Word of God, Thou didst manifestly come forth from the pure Maiden; and Thou hast shown unto all the Godhead thrice-radiant, single in essence, of unchangeable Hypostases.[^8053]

*Lord, have mercy, three times.*[^8054]
*Sessional Hymn, Tone 7.*
*Tone 7, Podoben: "He Who for my sake" (И҆́же менѐ ра́ди):*
Have mercy on Thy servants who have sinned, O Holy Trinity; and receive those who repent unto Thee, O Tender-hearted One, and vouchsafe forgiveness.[^8055]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:* Sweeten our souls embittered by sins, O all-pure Mother of God, and deliver from transgressions those who hymn thee, O Bride of God.[^8056]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Leaving not the Father's bosom, and having descended to earth, O Christ God: I have heard the mystery of Thy dispensation, and I have glorified Thee, O only Lover of mankind.[^8057]

*Troparion 1:* O all-holding, thrice-radiant Monad, origin of Godhead, and Savior of all: protect now Thy singers, and save from sorrow and passions, and from every affliction.[^8058]

*Troparion 2:* Being in perplexity at significant words concerning Thine incomprehensible thrice-radiant Godhead, we hymn Thee, O Lord Lover of mankind, and we glorify Thy power.[^8059]

*Troparion 3:* On earth as in heaven with the bodiless choirs, O Monad and Trinity, undividedly do we divide Thee, and with love we glorify Thee, as exercising lordship over all that exist.[^8060]

*Theotokion:* Not departing from the paternal glory, Thou didst descend unto our lowliness...[^8061]

...willingly, being incarnate, O Super-essential One; and Thou hast raised us up unto divine glory, as Thou art tender-hearted.[^8062]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* Night without light is unto the unbelieving, O Christ, but illumination unto the faithful in the sweetness of Thy words; therefore I rise early at dawn unto Thee, and hymn Thy Divinity.[^8063]

*Troparion 1:* O Nature source of light, Trinity in hypostatic forms, and Monad in counsel, and glory, and honor: establish us in Thy love. *(Twice)*[^8064]

*Troparion 2:* Glorifying Mind, and Word, and Spirit, one divine origin and thrice-sunned nature, we ask to be delivered from temptations and all sorrows.[^8065]

*Theotokion:* Having taken form in human nature, O Word of God, from the holy Virgin, Thou didst teach us to sing of the Trinity in Unity, of the same image and co-enthroned.[^8066]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Sailing in the surge of worldly cares, engulfed with my ship by sins, and cast forth unto the soul-corrupting beast, as Jonah, O Christ, I cry aloud unto Thee: From the deadly depth lead me up.[^8067]

*Troparion 1:* The equally-glorious dominion and monarchical Godhead, existing in three Hypostases, we unchangeably glorify one with another, having distinction only by the existence of each property. *(Twice)*[^8068]

*Troparion 2:* The noetic ranks of Angelic splendors praise Thee, O thrice-sunned Godhead; with whom we also with mortal lips sing of Thee as the Creator of all, One, and faithfully glorify Thee.[^8069]

*Theotokion:* By an incomprehensible word, the Word begotten of the Sun the Father, another Sun before the ages, in the last times shone forth from the Virgin, and proclaimed the One God in three Persons, past finding out.[^8070]

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 7.*
*Tone 7, Podoben: "Brighter than fire" (О҆гнѧ̀ свѣтлѣ́йши):*
O consubstantial Trinity, tri-hypostatic Monad! Have mercy on those whom Thou hast created, O Immortal One! consuming the wickednesses of those who sin, and enlightening the hearts of those who hymn Thy tender compassion: O our God, glory to Thee![^8071]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:* Brighter than fire, more effectual than light is the mercy of thy grace, O Sovereign Lady, consuming the sins of men, and bedewing the thoughts of those who praise...[^8072]

...thy majesties, O all-blameless Mother of God.[^8073]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* Cast into the fiery furnace, the venerable Children turned the fire into dew by singing, crying aloud thus: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God of our fathers.[^8074]

*Troparion 1:* Ever shining with light-beaming flashes, O thrice-radiant God, unapproachable and super-essential Monad: save those who piously believe in Thee, O Master, and bow down before Thee.[^8075]

*Troparion 2:* Submitting unto the saying of the divine Prophets, we glorify Thee alone, the God of all in three characters, crying aloud thus: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God of our fathers.[^8076]

*Troparion 3:* With mortal lips we, together with the immaterial ranks, sing of Thee with songs, O Holy Trinity, in unity of essence, crying aloud: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God of our fathers.[^8077]

*Theotokion:* That He Who fashioned Adam might refashion him again, O All-pure One, He manifestly became man from thee, deifying men, who cry aloud thus: Blessed, O Most Pure One, is the fruit of thy womb.[^8078]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* The bush unconsumed by fire that had communion on Sinai revealed God unto Moses slow of speech and stammering; and zeal for God showed forth the three invincible Children as singers in the fire: All ye works of the Lord, sing the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^8079]

*Troparion 1:* Vouchsafe the hearts of Thy singers to be illuminated with light-effecting rays of the thrice-radiant Sun; and grant now ever to behold Thy beauty, O Trinity, O Monad, as is possible, unto all who with fitting faith sing of Thy majesty unto all ages.[^8080]

*Troparion 2:* O threefold and single unoriginate Lordly Dominion, Thou upholdest all things, and rulest heaven and earth; therefore keep me ever drawn by Thy love, to sing unto Thee: All ye works of the Lord, sing the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^8081]

*Troparion 3:* Make me a temple of Thy thrice-radiant splendor, O Benefactor and Lover of mankind; and show me unapproachable to invisible enemies and to fleshly passions by communion and partaking, O Master, my monarchical God and Lord of glory, to sing of Thee unto all ages...[^8082]

...O my monarchical God and Lord of glory, to sing of Thee unto all ages.[^8083]

*Theotokion:* The God-originating Light, having shone forth from thy womb, O all-pure Mother of God, illuminated the whole world with thrice-sunned light, and showed the earth to be as another heaven, singing: All ye works of the Lord, sing the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^8084]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* Mother of God and Virgin, who gavest birth and still remainest virgin: this is not a work of nature, but of God's condescension; therefore, as her who alone wast accounted worthy of God's wonders, we magnify thee ever.[^8085]

*Troparion 1:* To speak sublime words and worthily sing of Thee, Whom the Seraphim in the highest unceasingly hymn, we mortals are unable; yet taking courage as before the Master of all and most man-befriending God, we magnify Thee.[^8086]

*Troparion 2:* Deliver Thy singers from bodily sicknesses and spiritual passionate attachment, O single undivided Trinity; and vouchsafe us to be preserved unharmed from all worldly temptations.[^8087]

*Troparion 3:* O equal in power, origin of Godhead, thrice-radiant, all-powerful dominion, unchangeable beauty of essential goodness: grant forgiveness of transgressions unto Thy servants, and deliver from temptations and passions.[^8088]

*Theotokion:* Having received mind and soul and fleshly commingling from thy most pure womb, O Mother of God, God the Word truly appeared as man; and He manifestly showed man to be a partaker of the Divine nature.[^8089]

*After this, the refrains of Gregory of Sinai: It is truly meet: and the rest.*[^8090]

*At Matins:*[^8091]
*After the Six Psalms: "God is the Lord," and hath appeared unto us: in Tone 7; and we say the Resurrectional Troparion: "Thou didst destroy death by Thy Cross": twice. Glory: to the Saint, from the Menaion. Both now: Resurrectional Theotokion of the same Tone as the troparion of the Saint. Then the usual stichology of the Psalter.*[^8092]

*After the First Stichology, Resurrectional Sessional Hymns, Tone 7:*
When the tomb was sealed, Thou, O Christ God, the Life, didst shine forth from the tomb; and while the doors were shut, Thou, the Resurrection of all, didst stand before the disciples, through them renewing an upright spirit in us, according to Thy great mercy.[^8093]

*Verse: Arise, O Lord my God, let Thy hand be lifted up; forget not Thy poor unto the end [LXX Ps. 9:33].*[^8094]

By Thy three-day burial having despoiled death, and corrupt...[^8095]

...man having raised up by Thy life-bearing rising, O Christ God, as Thou art the Lover of mankind: Glory to Thee![^8096]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:* As having loving-kindness for our aid, and beholding those that are upon the earth, have compassion upon the helpless people, O blessed Mother of God. Cease not interceding, lest we perish miserably; pray merciful God as Thou art tender-hearted, that our souls may be saved, O most holy Virgin.[^8097]

*After the Second Stichology, Resurrectional Sessional Hymns, Tone 7:*
Life lay in the tomb, and a seal was placed upon the stone; as a sleeping King soldiers guarded Christ, and Angels glorified Him as immortal God, while the women cried aloud: The Lord is risen, granting unto the world great mercy.[^8098]

*Verse: I will give thanks unto Thee, O Lord, with my whole heart; I will tell of all Thy marvelous works [LXX Ps. 9:2].*[^8099]

Unto the tomb ran the women, bearing myrrh with tears; and while soldiers guarded Thee, the King of all, they said among themselves: Who will roll away the stone for us? Risen is the Angel of Great Counsel, having trampled...[^8100]

...down death. O Almighty Lord, glory to Thee![^8101]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:* Unceasingly entreat Christ our God, Who was crucified for our sake, and rose again, and cast down the dominion of death, O Virgin Mother of God, that He may save our souls.[^8102]

*Hypakoe, Tone 7:*
O Thou Who didst take our likeness, and didst endure the Cross in the flesh: save us by Thy Resurrection, O Christ God, as Thou art the Lover of mankind.[^8103]

*Songs of Ascents [Anabathmoi], Tone 7:*
*(Whose verses we sing repeating them).*[^8104]
*Antiphon 1:*
Turning back the captivity of Zion from error, do Thou, O Savior, enliven me also, delivering me from slavish passions.[^8105]

He that sows afflictions of fasting in the south with tears, the same shall reap sheaves of joy of nourishment unto life evermore.[^8106]

*Glory:* In the Holy Spirit is the fountain of divine treasures, from Whom comes wisdom, understanding, fear; unto Him be praise, and glory, honor, and dominion.[^8107]

*Both now: The same.*[^8108]

*Antiphon 2:*
Except the Lord build the house of the soul, we labor in vain; for without Him neither deed nor word is accomplished.[^8109]

As the fruit of the womb, the Saints, moved by the Spirit, sprout forth paternal traditions of adoption as sons.[^8110]

*Glory:* In the Holy Spirit all things have their being; for before all is He God, Lordship over all, unapproachable light, the life of all.[^8111]

*Both now: The same.*

*Antiphon 3:*
They that fear the Lord, having found the paths of life, now and ever are called blessed with incorruptible glory.[^8112]

Beholding thy offspring round about thy table like shoots, rejoice and be glad, bringing them unto Christ the Chief Shepherd.[^8113]

*Glory:* In the Holy Spirit is the depth of gifts, the riches of glory, a great depth of judgments; for He is equally glorified with the Father and the Son, and together served.[^8114]

*Both now: The same.*

*Prokeimenon, Tone 7:*
Arise, O Lord my God, let Thy hand be lifted up; forget not Thy poor unto the end [LXX Ps. 9:33].[^8115]

*Verse: I will give thanks unto Thee, O Lord, with my whole heart [LXX Ps. 9:2].*[^8116]

*Then: Let every breath:*[^8117]
*Verse: Praise God in His Saints [LXX Ps. 150:1]. Resurrectional Gospel. Having beheld the Resurrection of Christ: Psalm 50: and the rest in order.*[^8118]

*Resurrectional Canon.*
*Tone 7. Ode 1. Heirmos:* By Thy nod the watery nature, formerly flowing easily, was transformed into dry land, O Lord. Therefore Israel, having journeyed on foot dry-shod, sings unto Thee a song of victory.[^8119]

*Refrain: Glory to Thee, O Lord, glory to Thy holy Resurrection.*[^8120]

*Troparion 1:* The tyranny of death was condemned by a tree, when Thou wast condemned, O Lord, unto an unrighteous death: whence the prince of darkness, having not prevailed over Thee, was righteously cast out.[^8121]

*Troparion 2:* Hades drew near unto Thee, and not having been able to shatter Thy body with its teeth, was crushed in its jaws: whence, O Savior, having loosed the pangs of death, Thou didst rise on the third day.[^8122]

*Theotokion:* The pangs of our first mother Eve have been loosed; for having escaped pangs, without knowing man thou gavest birth: whence, knowing thee manifestly to be the Mother of God, O Most Pure One, we all glorify thee.[^8123]

*Other Canon, of the Cross and Resurrection.*
*Tone 7. Ode 1. Heirmos:* He Who crushes battles with a high arm covered Pharaoh with his chariots in the deep sea. Let us sing unto Him, for He hath been glorified.[^8124]

*Refrain: Glory to Thee, O Lord, to Thy Cross and Resurrection.*[^8125]

*Troparion 1:* Two life-bearing fountains did the Savior pour forth for us upon the Cross, from His pierced side. Let us sing unto Him, for He hath been glorified.[^8126]

*Troparion 2:* Having taken up His abode in the tomb, and having risen on the third day, Christ granted the expectation of incorruption unto mortals. Let us sing unto Him, for He hath been glorified.[^8127]

*Theotokion:* Thou wast shown forth as the only Virgin even after childbirth: for thou didst bear the Creator incarnate for the world; therefore, "Rejoice!" we all cry unto thee.[^8128]

*Other Canon, to the Most Holy Mother of God.*[^8129]
*Tone 7. Ode 1. Heirmos:*
*By Thy nod to the earthly:*[^8130]
*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Mother of God, save us.*[^8131]
*Troparion 1:* O Virgin who didst bear an abyss of tender-heartedness, illumine my soul with thy light-bearing radiance, that I may worthily sing of the abyss of thy wonders.[^8132]

*Troparion 2:* Seeing us wounded by the arrow of sin, the Word, as Benefactor, had compassion; whence He was ineffably united in the flesh, O Most Pure One, Who is from thee, the Pre-divine One.[^8133]

*Troparion 3:* Corrupt and mortal human nature was held fast by death, O Sovereign Lady: but thou, having conceived the Life, hast led it up from corruption unto life.[^8134]

*Katavasia: I will open my mouth:*[^8135]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* O Lord and Savior, Who in the beginning didst establish the heavens by Thy all-powerful Word, and all the host thereof by the all-effecting and Divine Spirit: establish me upon the unshakable rock of the confession of Thee.[^8136]

*Troparion 1:* Having ascended upon the Tree, Thou of Thine own will didst suffer for our sake, O tender-hearted Savior: and Thou didst endure the wound that mediates reconciliation and salvation for the faithful, whereby we all, O Merciful One, have been reconciled unto Thy Father.[^8137]

*Troparion 2:* Having cleansed me from the wound, who was wounded in soul by the bite of the serpent, O Christ, Thou hast shown forth light unto me who of old lay in darkness and in corruption; for having descended by the Cross into hades, Thou didst raise me up with Thyself.[^8138]

*Theotokion:* By the supplications of Thy Mother who knew not man, grant peace unto the world, O Savior, and grant victory unto the Emperor (Name) over barbarian enemies; and vouchsafe those who glorify Thee partakers of Thine ineffable glory.[^8139]

*Canon of the Cross and Resurrection. Ode 3. Heirmos:*
O Thou Who didst establish the heavens by Thy word, and didst strengthen the foundation of the earth: establish my mind in Thy will, O Lover of mankind.[^8140]

*Troparion 1:* O Thou Who didst endure passions upon the Cross, and didst open Paradise unto the thief, as Benefactor and God: establish my mind in Thy will, O only Lover of mankind.[^8141]

*Troparion 2:* O Thou Who didst rise on the third day from the tomb, and didst shine forth life upon the world, as Giver of life and God: establish my mind in Thy will, O only Lover of mankind.[^8142]

*Theotokion:* O Mary, Virgin Mother, who didst seedlessly conceive God, and didst deliver Eve from the curse: pray God Who was incarnate of thee, to save thy flock.[^8143]

*Canon to the Mother of God. Ode 3. Heirmos:*
*In the beginning the heavens:*[^8144]

*Troparion 1:* The serpent crawling out of Eden, having deceived me with the desire for deification, cast me down unto the earth; but He Who is merciful and tender-hearted by nature, having had compassion, made me divine, having made His abode in thy womb, and become like unto me, O Mother-Virgin.[^8145]

*Troparion 2:* Blessed is the fruit of thy womb, O Virgin Mother of God, joy of all: for thou didst bear joy unto all the world, and gladness truly driving away sinful sorrow, O Bride of God.[^8146]

*Troparion 3:* Thou didst bear eternal Life, and Light, and Peace for us, O Virgin Parent of God, assuaging the ancient warfare of men against the Father and God, through faith and the confession of grace.[^8147]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Leaving not the Father's bosom, and having descended to earth, O Christ God: I have heard the mystery of Thy dispensation, and I have glorified Thee, O only Lover of mankind.[^8148]

*Troparion 1:* Giving His back unto wounds, He Who was incarnate of the Virgin—when the servant had sinned, the innocent Master was beaten, loosing my transgressions.[^8149]

*Troparion 2:* Standing before the judge, the Judge of transgressors, as one condemned became guilty, and was struck by a hand of clay, He Who fashioned man as God, and judges the earth righteously.[^8150]

*Theotokion:* As truly the Mother of God, pray thy Creator and Son, to direct me unto the saving...[^8151]

...haven, O All-blameless One, of His glorious will.[^8152]

*Canon of the Cross and Resurrection. Ode 4. Heirmos:*
Beholding Thy coming in the last times, O Christ, the Prophet cried aloud: I have heard Thy power, O Lord, that Thou art come to save all Thine anointed ones.[^8153]

*Troparion 1:* Knowing not sin, yet for its sake having become, O Lord, what Thou wast not, Thou art fashioned, taking what is foreign, that Thou mightest save the world, and having deceived the tormentor, mightest slay him.[^8154]

*Troparion 2:* Thou wast lifted up upon the Cross, and didst loose the sin of forefather Adam, for whose sake I heard Thy power; that Thou art come to save all Thine anointed ones.[^8155]

*Theotokion:* Born of the Virgin, Thou diest, and dost make alive Adam who had strayed in thought; for death was afraid of Thy power, that Thou art come to save all Thine anointed ones.[^8156]

*Canon to the Mother of God. Ode 4. Heirmos:*
*Leaving not the Father's bosom:*[^8157]

*Troparion 1:* Shown forth unto God before creation as all-elect and fair in radiant brightness, O All-lauded One: by thy outpouring of light enlighten those who hymn thee.[^8158]

*Troparion 2:* Thou didst bear God for men, O Pure One, incarnate of thy pure blood, Who delivers from many transgressions those who love, glorify, and honor thee, O Mother-Virgin.[^8159]

*Troparion 3:* Rational nature ministers sacerdotally unto Him Who shone forth from thee, O All-lauded One: having now learned the ineffable mystery of thy childbirth, O most blessed one.[^8160]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* Night without light is unto the unbelieving, O Christ, but illumination unto the faithful in the sweetness of Thy words; therefore I rise early at dawn unto Thee, and hymn Thy Divinity.[^8161]

*Troparion 1:* For the sake of Thy servants Thou art sold, O Christ, and Thou endurest a blow upon the cheek, mediating freedom unto those who sing unto Thee: Early at dawn I rise unto Thee, and hymn Thy Divinity.[^8162]

*Troparion 2:* By Thy Divine power, O Christ, through the weakness of the flesh Thou didst cast down the strong one, and by Thy Resurrection, O Savior, didst show me forth as conqueror over death.[^8163]

*Theotokion:* Thou didst bear God, O pure Mother, incarnate of thee in a God-befitting manner, O All-lauded One: for thou knewest not male seed, but givest birth from the Holy Spirit.[^8164]

*Canon of the Cross and Resurrection. Ode 5. Heirmos:*
O Lord my God, rising early at dawn from the night unto Thee, I pray: Grant me remission of my transgressions, and direct my ways unto the light of Thy commandments, I pray.[^8165]

*Troparion 1:* When Thou wast numbered with the lawless, and wast lifted up upon Golgotha, the luminaries hid themselves, and the earth was shaken, and the temple veil was rent in twain, making manifest the falling away of the Jews.[^8166]

*Troparion 2:* Thee Who didst destroy all the power of the tyrant by the might of Thy incomprehensible Godhead, and didst raise the dead by Thy Resurrection, we glorify with songs.[^8167]

*Theotokion:* O Mother of the King and God, all-lauded Mother of God, unto those who ever praise thee with songs in faith and love, send down the cleansing of transgressions by thy supplications.[^8168]

*Canon to the Mother of God. Ode 5. Heirmos:*
*Night without light is unto the unbelieving:*[^8169]

*Troparion 1:* Jacob, beholding a ladder established on high, learned the image of thy virginity; for through thee God had communion with men, O all-pure Sovereign Lady.[^8170]

*Troparion 2:* Having now found eternal redemption through thee, O Virgin, we diligently...[^8171]

...cry unto thee, "Rejoice, O Bride of God!" and rejoicing in thy light, O All-lauded One, we sing of thee with songs.[^8172]

*Troparion 3:* Finding thee alone as a lily in the midst of thorns, O Virgin, shining with the brightness of purity and with the light of virginity, O All-blameless One, the Bridegroom received thee as His bride.[^8173]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Sailing in the surge of worldly cares, engulfed with my ship by sins, and cast forth unto the soul-corrupting beast, as Jonah, O Christ, I cry aloud unto Thee: From the deadly depth lead me up.[^8174]

*Troparion 1:* The souls shut up in hades, and the remnants of the righteous, remembered Thee, and prayed for salvation from Thee, which Thou, O Christ, didst grant by the Cross unto those in the nether regions, having come as Thou art tender-hearted.[^8175]

*Troparion 2:* Toward Thy living and not-made-by-hands temple, broken by sufferings, the choir of the Apostles despaired of looking again; but beyond hope having worshipped, they proclaimed Thee risen everywhere.[^8176]

*Theotokion:* An image of thine ineffable and all-blameless childbirth, O Virgin Bride of God, He Who for our sake...[^8177]

...who among men is able to recount? For God the Word, being ineffably united unto thee, became flesh from thee.[^8178]

*Canon of the Cross and Resurrection. Ode 6. Heirmos:*
I cried unto Thee, O Lord, in my affliction; and Thou hast led up my life, O Plenteous in mercy.[^8179]

*Troparion 1:* The Master, being crucified, shook all the earth, and as Good He healed its grievous shatterings.[^8180]

*Troparion 2:* Death was destroyed, and hades was despoiled; for Christ is risen, preparing a path for men by His Resurrection.[^8181]

*Theotokion:* O all-pure Sovereign Lady, sure Hope: preserve those who love and hymn thee as the true Mother of God.[^8182]

*Canon to the Mother of God. Ode 6. Heirmos:*
*Sailing in the surge of worldly cares:*[^8183]

*Troparion 1:* The figures of the Law and the prophetic proclamations clearly foretold thee, who wast to bear the Benefactor of all creation, O Pure One, Who in sundry times and divers manners hath done good unto those who faithfully hymn thee.[^8184]

*Troparion 2:* First-fashioned Adam, who of old was estranged from the divine sweetness of Paradise by the plot of the manslayer, O Unwedded One,...[^8185]

...thou hast led back again: having given birth unto Him Who delivered us from transgression.[^8186]

*Troparion 3:* He Who by divine will and creative power constituted all things from non-being, came forth from thy womb, O Pure One, and enlightened those who were in the shadow of death with the most divine flashes of His Godhead.[^8187]

*Kontakion, Tone 7:*
No longer shall the dominion of death be able to hold men fast; for Christ descended, shattering and destroying its powers. Hades is bound, the Prophets rejoice with one accord: The Savior hath appeared, saying unto those in faith: Come forth, ye faithful, into the resurrection![^8188]

*Ikos:*
The nethermost depths below trembled this day, hades and death, before One of the Trinity; the earth was shaken, and the gatekeepers of hades seeing Thee were terrified; and all creation rejoicing with the Prophets sings unto Thee a song of victory, unto our Redeemer and God, Who hath now destroyed the power of death. Let us exclaim and cry aloud unto Adam, and unto those descended from Adam: The Tree hath brought him back again; come forth, ye faithful, into the resurrection![^8189]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* Of old the Children showed forth the fiery furnace as dropping dew, singing unto the one God and saying: The supremely exalted and supremely glorified God of our fathers.[^8190]

*Troparion 1:* By a tree was Adam put to death, having of his own will wrought disobedience; but by the obedience of Christ he is renewed again: for for my sake is the supremely glorified Son of God crucified.[^8191]

*Troparion 2:* All creation sang of Thee risen from the tomb, O Christ; for Thou, the supremely glorified Light, didst blossom forth as life unto those in hades, and resurrection unto the dead, and light unto those in darkness.[^8192]

*Theotokion:* Rejoice, daughter of corrupt Adam! Rejoice, only Bride of God! Rejoice, through whom corruption was driven out, who didst bear God: Whom pray, O Pure One, that we all may be saved.[^8193]

*Canon of the Cross and Resurrection. Ode 7. Heirmos:*
Cast into the fiery furnace, the venerable Children turned the fire into dew, crying aloud thus with singing: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God of our fathers.[^8194]

*Troparion 1:* Having blunted the sting of sin upon the Tree of the Cross, and destroyed the handwriting of Adam's transgression by the spear of Thy side...[^8195]

...Blessed art Thou, O Lord God of our fathers.[^8196]

*Troparion 2:* O Thou Who wast pierced in the side, and by the sprinklings of Divine Blood didst cleanse the earth that was defiled with the blood of idolatrous madness: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^8197]

*Triadikon:* In orthodox manner we theologically confess the undivided Nature: God the Father unoriginate, the Word of equal authority, and the Spirit, crying: Blessed art Thou, O consubstantial Trinity.[^8198]

*Canon to the Mother of God. Ode 7. Heirmos:*
*Of old the Children showed forth the fiery furnace:*[^8199]

*Troparion 1:* Having thee as a vesture variously adorned and gilded, thy Creator and Lord loved thee, O Virgin: the supremely exalted and supremely glorified God of our fathers.[^8200]

*Troparion 2:* Isaiah, who of old received the coal, is cleansed, O Maiden, having symbolically seen thy childbirth: the supremely exalted and supremely glorified God of our fathers.[^8201]

*Troparion 3:* The divine Prophets, beholding of old the symbolic figures of thy divine childbirth, joyfully singing cried...[^8202]

...aloud: The supremely exalted and supremely glorified God of our fathers.[^8203]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* The bush unconsumed by fire that had communion on Sinai revealed God unto Moses slow of speech and stammering; and zeal for God showed forth the three invincible Children as singers in the fire: All ye works of the Lord, sing the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^8204]

*Troparion 1:* The all-pure noetic Lamb, being slain for the world, brought an end unto the sacrifices offered according to the Law, cleansing it from transgressions as God, who ever cry aloud: All ye works of the Lord, sing the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^8205]

*Troparion 2:* Our flesh, which was not incorruptible before the Passion, assumed by the Creator, was established untouchable by corruption after the Passion and Resurrection, and renews mortals who cry aloud: All ye works of the Lord, sing the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^8206]

*Theotokion:* Thy pure and all-blameless state, O Virgin, cleansed the foul and abominable uncleanness of the world; and thou wast the cause of our reconciliation unto God, O Most Pure One: therefore, O Virgin, we bless thee and supremely exalt thee unto all ages.[^8207]

*Canon of the Cross and Resurrection. Ode 8. Heirmos:*
Him Who alone is the unoriginate King of glory, Whom the heavenly Powers bless, and the ranks of the Angels tremble before: ye priests, sing; ye people, supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^8208]

*Troparion 1:* Him Who endured passions of His own will, and was nailed unto the Cross by His own volition, and destroyed the powers of hades: ye priests, sing; ye people, supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^8209]

*Troparion 2:* Him Who abolished the dominion of death, and rose from the tomb with glory, and saved the human race: ye priests, sing; ye people, supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^8210]

*Theotokion:* The one tender-hearted, pre-eternal Word, born of the Virgin in the last times, and Who loosed the ancient curse: ye priests, sing; ye people, supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^8211]

*Canon to the Mother of God. Ode 8. Heirmos:*
*The bush unconsumed by fire that had communion on Sinai:*[^8212]

*Troparion 1:* With the light of thy childbirth thou hast marvelously enlightened the universe, O Foster-mother of God: for in truth thou bearest God in thine arms, Who enlightens the faithful that ever cry aloud: All ye works of the Lord, sing the Lord,...[^8213]

...and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^8214]

*Troparion 2:* With piety, O Pure One, we sing of thy womb, which contained God ineffably becoming incarnate, Who hath given the illumination of the knowledge of God unto all the faithful, who ever cry aloud: All ye works of the Lord, sing the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^8215]

*Troparion 3:* By the flashes of thy light, O pure Light-bearing Parent of God, thou hast made light-like those who hymn thee: for thou wast revealed as the dwelling-place of light, making bright with light those who cry aloud: All ye works of the Lord, sing the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^8216]

*Canticle of the Mother of God: My soul doth magnify the Lord: with the refrain: More honorable than the Cherubim:*[^8217]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* Mother who knewest not man, O Virgin Mother of God, who didst bear without experience of corruption, and didst lend flesh unto the Word, the Artificer of all, receptacle of the Uncontainable, dwelling-place of Thine uncontainable Creator: thee do we magnify.[^8218]

*Troparion 1:* Ye who ascribe suffering unto the Divinity, be muzzled, all ye alien-minded! For the Lord of glory was crucified in the flesh, but was not crucified according to the Divine nature, as we magnify the One in two natures.[^8219]

*Troparion 2:* Ye who disbelieve the resurrection of bodies, go to the tomb of Christ and learn that the flesh of the Giver of life was put to death and rose again: unto the confirmation of the final resurrection, whereon we hope.[^8220]

*Triadikon:* Venerating not a Trinity of Godheads, but of Hypostases, neither a unity of Persons, but of Godhead: we cut down those who divide It, and we confound those who dare to confuse It, Whom we magnify.[^8221]

*Canon of the Cross and Resurrection. Ode 9. Heirmos:*
Mother of God and Virgin, who gavest birth and yet remainest virgin: this is not a work of nature, but of God's condescension; therefore, as her who alone was accounted worthy of God's wonders, we magnify thee ever.[^8222]

*Troparion 1:* Light from Light, the radiance of the Father's glory shining timelessly, Christ shone forth as in darkness upon human life, and drove away the pursuing darkness: Whom we the faithful unceasingly magnify.[^8223]

*Troparion 2:* Beholding the fleshly passions and the might of the Godhead in Christ, let those who imagine a single composite nature be put to shame; for as man He indeed dies, and as the Creator of all He arises.[^8224]

*Troparion 3:* "Bring myrrh unto the dead, but a hymn unto the Living One; tears unto the dying, but a song unto the Life of all, ye women!" cried the herald of the Resurrection, proclaiming the good tidings of Christ's rising.[^8225]

*Theotokion:* "I know no other God besides Thee," the Church cries unto Thee: "Thou Who didst choose me from among the unbelieving nations as Thy bride, grant salvation unto the faithful, O Word, through the prayers of her who bare Thee, as Thou art tender-hearted."[^8226]

*Canon to the Mother of God. Ode 9. The same Heirmos.*[^8227]

*Troparion 1:* Thou wast shown forth as the mediatress of eternal joy and gladness for us, O Ever-Virgin Maiden: having borne the Redeemer, Who in truth and in divine spirit delivers those who honor Him as God.[^8228]

*Troparion 2:* David thy forefather, O Most Pure One, singing of thee, names thee the ark of divine sanctification, who didst supernaturally contain God Who sits in the Father's bosom: Whom we the faithful unceasingly magnify.[^8229]

*Troparion 3:* Truly thou art higher than all creation, O Maiden: for thou hast bodily borne for us the Creator of all...[^8230]

...Therefore, as Mother of the only Master, thou bearest the pre-eminent victory over all.[^8231]

*After the Katavasia, the Little Litany; then: Holy is the Lord our God. After this, the Morning Exapostilarion.*[^8232]

*At the Praises, Resurrectional Stichera, Tone 7:*
Christ is risen from the dead, having loosed the bonds of death. Proclaim good tidings of great joy, O earth; sing, ye heavens, the glory of God.[^8233]

Having beheld the Resurrection of Christ, let us worship the Holy Lord Jesus, Who alone is sinless.[^8234]

We cease not worshipping Christ's Resurrection; for He hath saved us from our iniquities: Holy is the Lord Jesus, Who hath revealed the Resurrection.[^8235]

What shall we render unto the Lord for all that He hath rendered unto us? For our sake God is among men; for the sake of corrupted nature the Word became flesh, and dwelt among us: unto the ungrateful, the Benefactor; unto captives, the Liberator; unto those sitting in darkness, the Sun of righteousness; upon the Cross, the Impassible One; in hades, the Light; in death, the Life; the resurrection for the sake of the fallen...[^8236]

...unto Whom let us cry aloud: O our God, glory to Thee![^8237]

*Other Stichera, Anatolian. The same Tone:*
Thou didst shatter the gates of hades, O Lord, and abolish the dominion of death by Thy mighty power; and Thou didst raise up with Thyself the dead that slept from the ages in darkness, by Thy divine and glorious Resurrection, as King of all and almighty God.[^8238]

Come, let us rejoice in the Lord, and let us be glad in His Resurrection, for He raised up the dead with Himself from the unloosed bonds of hades, and as God granted eternal life and great mercy unto the world.[^8239]

*Verse: Arise, O Lord my God, let Thy hand be lifted up; forget not Thy poor unto the end [LXX Ps. 9:33].*[^8240]

A radiant Angel sat upon the stone of the life-receiving tomb, and proclaimed the good tidings unto the myrrh-bearing women, saying: The Lord is risen, even as He told you beforehand; tell His disciples that He goes before you into Galilee, and grants eternal life and great mercy unto the world.[^8241]

*Verse: I will give thanks unto Thee, O Lord, with my whole heart; I will tell of all Thy marvelous works [LXX Ps. 9:2].*[^8242]

Why have ye set at naught the chief cornerstone, O most lawless Jews? Behold, He is the One Whom God placed in Zion, Who poured forth water from the rock in the wilderness, and Who pours forth immortality for us from His side. This is the stone cut out from the virginal mountain without male volition: the Son of man coming upon the clouds of heaven unto the Ancient of Days, as Daniel said; and His kingdom is eternal.[^8243]

*Glory: Evangelical Sticheron; and both now:*
*Theotokion: Most blessed art thou:*
*Great Doxology; then the Troparion: "Today salvation is come unto the world."*[^8244]

*At the Liturgy:*
*The Beatitudes, Tone 7:*[^8245]

*Refrain / Troparion 1:* Beautiful was the fruit that put me to death, and good for food; but Christ is the Tree of Life, whereof eating I die not, but cry aloud with the thief: Remember me, O Lord, in Thy kingdom.[^8246]

*Troparion 2:* Having been lifted up upon the Cross, O Tender-hearted One, the handwriting of Adam's ancient sin...[^8247]

...Thou hast blotted out, and hast saved the whole human race from deception; therefore we sing unto Thee, O Benefactor and Lord.[^8248]

*Troparion 3:* Of old the serpent poured venom into the ears of Eve; but Christ upon the Tree of the Cross poured forth unto the world the sweetness of life; therefore we cry aloud: Remember us, O Lord, in Thy kingdom.[^8249]

*Troparion 4:* In the tomb as mortal Thou wast laid, O Life of all, O Christ, and Thou didst shatter the bars of hades; and having risen in glory on the third day as Thou art mighty, Thou didst enlighten all. Glory to Thy rising![^8250]

*Troparion 5:* By Thy rising Thou didst raise up Adam with Thyself, O Giver of life, O Christ, granting the ancient incorruption that was in paradise. Therefore I cry aloud with the thief: Remember me, O Lord, when Thou comest into Thy kingdom.[^8251]

*Troparion 6:* The Lord, having risen on the third day from the dead, granted His peace unto His disciples; and having blessed them, He sent them forth, saying: Bring all into My kingdom.[^8252]

*Glory... Triadikon:* Light is the Father, light is the Son and Word, light is the Holy Spirit; yet the three are one Light; for God is one in three Persons, and in one Nature and Principle, undivided and unconfused, being pre-eternal.[^8253]

*Both now... Theotokion:* Thou didst give birth unto the Son and Word of the Father in the flesh for our sake, even as He Himself knows, O Mother of God. Therefore, O Virgin Mother, having been deified through thee, we cry aloud unto thee: Rejoice, thou Hope of Christians![^8254]

## Monday: The Holy Bodiless Powers[^8255]

*On Sunday Evening.*[^8256]
*On "Lord, I have cried," Penitential Stichera, Tone 7.*[^8257]
*Tone 7, Podoben: "Today Judas watches" (Дне́сь бди́тъ І҆ꙋ́да):*[^8258]

*Sticheron 1:* Lift up thine eyes, O my soul, and look upon the dispensation and tender-heartedness of God: how, having bowed the heavens, He came down to earth, that He might raise thee up from the misery of thy passions and set thee upon the rock of faith. O wonder of the fearful miracle! Glo-...[^8259]

...ry to Thy condescension, O Lover of mankind![^8260]

*Sticheron 2:* Look upon thine exceeding iniquities, and wonder: how does the earth bear thee, how did it not cleave asunder? How do wild beasts not devour thee? And how does the unsetting sun not cease to shine upon thee? Arise, repent, and cry aloud unto the Lord: I have sinned against Thee, I have sinned, have mercy on me![^8261]

*Sticheron 3:* Having hope in Thee, O almighty Lord, we pray Thee: that we may be delivered from all distresses, passions, and turmoil, so that we may pass through our life in peace, and having lived in purity, may find Thee on the day of judgment a clement and merciful Master.[^8262]

*Then the Stichera to the Saint in the Menaion; but if there be no Menaion, we sing:*[^8263]
*Other Stichera, to the Holy Bodiless Angels, Tone 7.*
*Tone 7, Podoben: "No longer are we forbidden" (Не ктомꙋ̀ возбрани́ми):*[^8264]

*Sticheron 1:* Enlightened by the rays of the Trinity, O Archangels, enlighten those who sing of you in faith.[^8265]

*Sticheron 2:* Being shown forth as second lights by participation, perfect lights! Pray that we may stand before the Primary Light.[^8266]

*Sticheron 3:* By the shelter of your wings preserve all the faithful, destroying the darkness of the spirits of wickedness.[^8267]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Let us all cry aloud with songs together with the Angels unto the Mother of God, for she hath given birth unto the Savior of the world; and after childbirth she remained again a Virgin, and delivered the world from deception by her childbirth, and granted unto us inexhaustible nourishment, nursing with milk the Liberator of our souls.[^8268]

*O Gladsome Light: Prokeimenon of the day, and the rest.*[^8269]
*At the Aposticha, Penitential Stichera, Tone 7.*[^8270]

*Sticheron 1:* As the prodigal son I am come, O Tender-hearted One! Receive me falling down before Thee, as one of Thy hired servants, O God, and have mercy on me, O Lover of mankind.[^8271]

*Verse: Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes, unto Thee that dwellest in heaven... So our eyes look unto the Lord our God, until He have mercy on us [LXX Ps. 122:1–2].*[^8272]

*Sticheron 2:* As one that fell among thieves and was wounded, so have I also fallen by reason of many sins, and wounded is my soul. Unto whom shall I flee, guilty that I am? Only unto Thee, the tender-hearted Physician of souls: pour out upon me, O God, Thy great mercy.[^8273]

*Verse: Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us... Our soul is exceedingly filled... [LXX Ps. 122:3–4]*...[^8274]

...with the reproach of them that are at ease, and with the contempt of the proud.[^8275]

*Sticheron 3:* Glory to Thee, O Christ God, praise of the Apostles, and joy of the Martyrs, whose proclamation was the consubstantial Trinity![^8276]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* "Rejoice!" we cry unto thee with the Angel, O Bride of God, calling thee bridal chamber and door, and fiery throne, and unhewn mountain, and bush unburned.[^8277]

*Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart: Trisagion, and the rest as usual.*[^8278]

*On Sunday at Compline.*[^8279]
*Supplicatory Canon to the Most Holy Mother of God, Tone 7.*[^8280]
*Ode 1. Heirmos:* By Thy nod the previously fluid nature of water was changed into an earthly form, O Lord; therefore, Israel, traversing it dry-shod on foot, sang a song of victory unto Thee.[^8281]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Mother of God, save us.*[^8282]

*Troparion 1:* The ranks of Angels truly and beautifully honor thee as the Mother of the God of all, O Most Pure One; yet receive even now from my clay lips a supplicatory song.[^8283]

*Troparion 2:* Put to death the wickedness that yet lives in my flesh, O Most Pure One who didst bear the true Life; and grant unto thy servant deliverance from passions and transgressions, O Pure One.[^8284]

*Glory...* The Sun from the Sun, the timeless Word that incomprehensibly shone forth, shone forth in time; with His rays enlighten our souls, O Virgin, and give them understanding.[^8285]

*Both now...* It is fitting unto thee, O Mother of God, that a word of praise be ever operative; yet we pray thee, O Pure One: save from all worldly sorrow those who flee unto thee.[^8286]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* O Lord and Savior, Who in the beginning didst establish the heavens by Thy all-powerful Word, and all the host thereof by the all-effecting and Divine Spirit: establish me upon the unshakable rock of the confession of Thee.[^8287]

*Troparion 1:* Having discreetly rendered the debts of thy graces in servant-like manner, O Sovereign Lady, I fervently sing of thee, O most pure Mother of the Word and Redeemer of all; yet even now deliver me from worldly adversities.[^8288]

*Troparion 2:* Adam, who of old was slain by transgression, hast thou manifestly accounted worthy of a better life, O Most Pure One, having given birth unto the Life-originating Word, O Sovereign Lady; yet even now deliver me from deadly sin.[^8289]

*Glory...* The law of passions grew fierce in the flesh and now afflicts the spiritual law, and unites my mind unto grievous lusts, O Most Pure One; but grant me the peace of dispassion swiftly by thy prayers.[^8290]

*Both now...* Having ineffably conceived in the womb, O Most Pure One, Christ the Upholder of all, thou didst loose the nature of earthborn mortals from the bars of hades; yet even now tear asunder the closely woven cords that corrupt me.[^8291]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Leaving not the Father's bosom, and having descended to earth, O Christ God: I have heard the mystery of Thy dispensation, and I have glorified Thee, O only Lover of mankind.[^8292]

*Troparion 1:* By night I wrestle with the darkness of grievous things, having darkened my thought: O Door of light, enlighten me with thy rays, and deliver me swiftly from bitterness and sorrow.[^8293]

*Troparion 2:* Constrained by a multitude of sins, and greatly bound by the cords of adversities, I now call upon thy help, O Sovereign Lady, that I may be delivered from every torment.[^8294]

*Glory...* The assemblies of evil and cruel men, and the counsels of envious and crafty men destroy, O All-praised One, and deliver me from all sorrow and affliction of life.[^8295]

*Both now...* Cut off with the sword of thy prayers, O Sovereign Lady, the returns of false words and the actions aimed against me; and swiftly break them in pieces, and deliver me from every sorrow.[^8296]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* Night without light is unto the unbelieving, O Christ, but illumination unto the faithful in the sweetness of Thy words; therefore I rise early at dawn unto Thee, and hymn Thy Divinity.[^8297]

*Troparion 1:* Being an invincible wall unto the faithful, be a shelter also unto thy servant, O Virgin, and deliver me from all distressing sorrow and affliction.[^8298]

*Troparion 2:* I am sorely sick, grievously afflicted on both sides, O Most Pure One: heal by thy prayers the power of soul and body that I have surrendered.[^8299]

*Glory...* Unto the abyss of thy compassions, O Virgin, do I thy servant now flee, that I may be delivered from the counsel that unjustly holds me fast, and from affliction.[^8300]

*Both now...* Highly exalted is the glory of thy prayers everywhere, O most pure Virgin Mother: whereby even now...[^8301]

...deliver me from every assault of this present life.[^8302]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Sailing in the surge of worldly cares, engulfed with my ship by sins, and cast forth unto the soul-corrupting beast, as Jonah, O Christ, I cry aloud unto Thee: From the deadly depth lead me up.[^8303]

*Troparion 1:* The utterances and words of all faithfully praise thee, O Pure One, for the debt of all men was manifestly cancelled by thy childbirth; yet receive even now the prayer of thy singers, and deliver them from temptations and sorrows.[^8304]

*Troparion 2:* I am sick and pierced with the passionate thorns of sin; do thou who hast healing from thyself, O Most Pure One, rescue me from all sickness and sorrow, and deliver me from adversities and griefs.[^8305]

*Glory...* The nature of earthborn mortals hast thou manifestly led up unto heaven, O Mother of God, having become the palace of the King of glory; therefore lead me up from the abyss of many transgressions, adversities, and passions.[^8306]

*Both now...* The Word Who was incarnate beyond mind and word didst thou bear, O pure Virgin, Who delivered us from unreason; therefore with divine words we unceasingly hymn thee, and faithfully glorify thee.[^8307]

*Then: Lord, have mercy (thrice). Glory... Both now...*[^8308]
*Sessional Hymn, Tone 7:* O Sovereign Lady, Thy Son hath been angered against us, thy sinful servants, for we turned Him that is merciful unto wrath; but change Him unto mercy, O Pure One! Have compassion, O Mother of God; pray the tender-hearted One with spontaneous supplications, and deliver the captives.[^8309]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* Cast into the fiery furnace, the venerable Children turned the fire into dew by their singing, crying aloud thus: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God of our fathers.[^8310]

*Troparion 1:* Adorned with the fringes of purity, O most pure Maiden: transform my soul now from defiled wickedness unto divine beauty, O blessed Sovereign Lady, protectress of our race.[^8311]

*Troparion 2:* The wickednesses of demons rage fiercely now, and kindle for me the fire of passions; but all the more by thy rays consume them utterly with thy compassions, O Protectress of our race.[^8312]

*Glory...* Cease not at all to pray thy Son for our sake, O most pure Mother of God, delivering thy servants by thy prayers from human sorrows and afflictions.[^8313]

*Both now...* The transcendent Son received flesh without change from thee, O Virgin, and became man, that He might save those who sing: Blessed art Thou, O Most Pure One, and blessed is the Fruit of thy womb.[^8314]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* The Children in the furnace, being bedewed, cried aloud: Let all creation bless Him Who created all things by His word, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^8315]

*Troparion 1:* Passions of pasturage and manifold afflictions truly surround thy servant, O Sovereign Lady; but deliver me from all temptation thereof.[^8316]

*Troparion 2:* May thy mighty hand come upon us now, O Sovereign Lady, and deliver us from expected human sorrows.[^8317]

*Glory...* Shatter the snares of crafty and evil-conscienced men, O pure Parent of God, and deliver thy servant from all want.[^8318]

*Both now...* O pure Virgin, who art the only divine haven of the faithful: I pray thee, deliver me who am in the bitter storm of the sea of life.[^8319]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* Rejoice, radiant bride, Mother of the Giver of light! Rejoice, thou who didst contain the Uncontainable One in thy womb! Rejoice, thou who art more honorable than the Cherubim themselves, who didst bear the Savior of our souls![^8320]

*Troparion 1:* I have strayed like a beast from the commandments of the Master; by the bridles of thy prayers may I be restrained, O Most Pure One, unto the path of swift ordering.[^8321]

*Troparion 2:* I am dragged down sorely by an irrational stumbling unto the doing of my evil sin; deliver me, O Mother of God, from worse change.[^8322]

*Glory...* Having appeared as the first healing of human nature, thou didst heal the crushing of Adam and Eve; heal me also, O Mother of God, by thy supplications.[^8323]

*Both now...* Grant me a chaste mind, and a pure heart, and a vigilant understanding, that I may keep, O Sovereign Lady, the most pure commandments of my God.[^8324]

*It is truly meet: Trisagion, and the rest.*[^8325]

*On Monday at Matins.*[^8326]
*After the 1st Stichologia, Penitential Sessional Hymns, Tone 7.*[^8327]

*Sessional Hymn 1:* Having the medicine of repentance, O my soul, draw near falling down, and calling aloud with sighing: O Physician of souls and bodies, O Lover of mankind, liberate me from my many transgressions; number me with the harlot, and the thief, and the publican, and grant me, O God, the forgiveness of mine iniquities, and save me.[^8328]

*Verse: O Lord, rebuke me not in Thine anger, nor chasten me in Thy wrath [LXX Ps. 6:2].*[^8329]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* O Lord Who lovest mankind, Who by tears didst cleanse Peter's denial, and by sighing didst forgive the transgressions of the publican: have mercy on me.[^8330]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Thou hast surpassed the heavenly Powers, for thou wast shown forth as a divine temple, O blessed Mother of God, as having borne Christ the Savior of our souls.[^8331]

*After the 2nd Stichologia, Penitential Sessional Hymns, Tone 7.*[^8332]

*Sessional Hymn 1:* I have not rivaled the repentance of the publican, and I have not acquired the tears of the harlot; for from blindness I am at a loss for such amendment: but by Thy tender-heartedness, O Christ God, save me, as Thou art the Lover of mankind.[^8333]

*Verse: O Lord, rebuke me not in Thine anger, nor chasten me in Thy wrath [LXX Ps. 6:2].*[^8334]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* The worldly abyss buffets me with storm, O Lord; the surges of mine iniquities drown me, O Master: but stretch forth Thine hand, even as unto Peter, and save me, O Lover of mankind.[^8335]

*Verse: God is marvelous in His Saints, the God of Israel [LXX Ps. 67:36].*[^8336]

*Martyrion:* O Saints, pray that forgiveness of our transgressions may be given unto us, and that we may be delivered from expected evils and bitter death, we pray.[^8337]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Thou art more honorable than the glorious Cherubim, O most holy Virgin! For they, unable to bear the Divine power, perform their service veiling their faces with their wings; but thou, beholding as an eye-witness the incarnate Word, dost bear Him: Whom unceasingly pray for our souls.[^8338]

*After the 3rd Stichologia, Sessional Hymns, Tone 7.*[^8339]
*Tone 7, Podoben: "Fruit of thy womb" (Пло́дъ чре́ва):*[^8340]

*Sessional Hymn 1:* The storm of various passions and of my transgressions drowns me in the depth...[^8341]

...of despair; but through the prayers of Thine Angels, O Jesus Plenteous in mercy, save me as the prodigal.[^8342]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* Prepare thyself, O my soul, in thy lifetime not to doubt concerning the life to come; for there no one helps, neither riches, nor power, nor friends, nor princes: but only the demonstration of deeds, and God's love for mankind.[^8343]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* O undefiled Virgin Mother of God, pray thy Son with the hosts on high: that forgiveness of transgressions may be granted unto us before the end, who faithfully glorify thee.[^8344]

*Canon of Repentance, to our Lord Jesus Christ and to His Holy Martyrs. The work of Joseph, Tone 7.*[^8345]
*Ode 1. Heirmos:* Unto God Who helped Moses in Egypt to lead out Israel, unto Him alone let us sing, for He hath been glorified.[^8346]

*Refrain: Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee.*[^8347]

*Troparion 1:* Having fallen into the abyss of cruel things, I cry aloud unto Thee: Stretch forth Thine hand unto me, O Tender-hearted One, and save me as Peter, O Lover of mankind.[^8348]

*Troparion 2:* Wash me, who repent, O Christ, even as the harlot, from many transgressions by the nod of Thy mercy, that in faith I may glorify Thee.[^8349]

*Martyrion:* O Christ Who didst enlighten Thy Saints in the furnace of manifold wounds: by their supplication deliver me from the darkening of passions.[^8350]

*Martyrion:* Having cleansed themselves by the endurance of wounds, the Holy Martyrs shone more than the sun, and darkened deception.[^8351]

*Theotokion:* O all-praised Virgin, who art the protectress of the faithful, who art the conversion of sinners unto God: by thy prayers save me.[^8352]

*Other Canon, to the Holy Bodiless Angels. The work of Theophanes, Tone 7.*[^8353]
*Ode 1. Heirmos:* Unto God Who shook off Pharaoh in the Red Sea, let us sing a song of victory, for He hath been glorified.[^8354]

*Troparion 1:* O Christ of unreckonable power, having enlightened my mind with Thy light, inspire me to hymn Thine Angels, as Thou art all-powerful. (Twice)[^8355]

*Troparion 2:* Possessing steadfast radiance proceeding from the Source origin of Godhead, the heavenly choirs honor Christ with songs.[^8356]

*Theotokion:* Knowing thee, O Pure One, manifestly adorned with divine splendors, we faithful all cry unto thee: Rejoice![^8357]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* My heart is established in the Lord, my horn is exalted in my God; my mouth is enlarged over mine enemies; I have rejoiced in Thy salvation [cf. 1 Sam. / 1 Kings 2:1].[^8358]

*Troparion 1:* By night I was held fast by improper sins; enlighten me now with the light of repentance, O Giver of light, O Lover of mankind, that in faith I may glorify Thee.[^8359]

*Troparion 2:* All the paths along which I walked in life cast me down into the precipice of lusts: O Jesus, show me the divine paths of repentance.[^8360]

*Martyrion:* Having finished the contests with valiant character, ye were accounted worthy of heavenly crowns, O all-glorious Martyrs, praying for all.[^8361]

*Martyrion:* Having endured myriad afflictions, O passion-bearers, ye were accounted worthy of myriads of good things, being joined unto the myriads of Bodiless Powers.[^8362]

*Theotokion:* O Virgin who didst bear God the heavenly King: cast down the sin that reigns in me, and having had mercy, save me.[^8363]

*Canon to the Bodiless Powers. Ode 3. Heirmos:* The Church of Christ is established in faith; for unceasingly with songs she cries aloud, singing: Holy art Thou, O Lord, and my spirit hymns Thee![^8364]

*Troparion 1:* Having been the mediators of the Divine manifestation, O angelic choirs, immaterially ye cry aloud unceasingly: Holy art Thou, O Lord, Who savest our souls![^8365]

*Troparion 2:* Imparting the Divine rays unto one another in lawful love, in goodly order ye sing unto Christ: Holy art Thou, O Lord, O only Plenteous in mercy![^8366]

*Troparion 3:* As second lights unto the Primary Ray, let us who live piously emulate their life, singing unto Christ: Holy art Thou, O Lord, Who savest our souls![^8367]

*Theotokion:* The Word Who by His will brought all things from non-being, takes flesh of thee, O Virgin, having become man through tender mercy as the Lover of mankind.[^8368]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* By Thy dispensation, O Christ God, the virtue of Thine ineffable wisdom hath covered the heavens, O Lover of mankind.[^8369]

*Troparion 1:* O Tender-hearted One Who willest that all men should be saved: save me who have transgressed Thy commandments, O Word, and despise me not.[^8370]

*Troparion 2:* I bowed down to irrational passions, and wretched that I am, was made like unto beasts, O Tender-hearted One. O Word of God, having had mercy, save me.[^8371]

*Martyrion:* Roasted and kindled with fire, ye consumed deception, inflamed with the zeal of piety, O Martyrs.[^8372]

*Martyrion:* Ye were shown forth as a noetic paradise, having in your midst the Tree of Life, Christ the Planter of all things, O Blessed Ones.[^8373]

*Theotokion:* O pure Virgin full of God's grace, having bestowed grace upon my darkened mind, completely liberate me from ignorance.[^8374]

*Canon to the Bodiless Powers. Ode 4. Heirmos:* I have heard Thy report, and was afraid; I understood Thy works, and was filled with dread, O Lord [cf. Hab. 3:2].[^8375]

*Troparion 1:* Standing before Christ as chosen ministers, O angelic choirs, pray unto Him to heal the wounds of my soul.[^8376]
