*Troparion 2:* The hosts of the Bodiless Powers, O Master, devoutly standing around Thy throne, clearly and ever cry aloud: Glory to Thy power, O Lord![^8377]

*Troparion 3:* The angelic ranks marvelled, beholding Thee living on earth with flesh, O Christ, with mortal men.[^8378]

*Theotokion:* O All-praised One and most pure Mother of God, who didst ineffably give birth unto God in the flesh: we pray thee, pray that we may be saved.[^8379]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* Rising early at dawn unto Thy glory and praise, O Word, we unceasingly praise the form of Thy Cross, which Thou hast given unto us as a weapon unto help.[^8380]

*Troparion 1:* I have spent my life in laziness, and I tremble before Thine unavoidable judgment seat, whereon I am to be judged, passionate that I am; have compassion on me, O Lord.[^8381]

*Troparion 2:* O Word Who didst enlighten the eyes of the blind: open the pupils of my soul, grievously darkened, to behold the light of Thy commandments.[^8382]

*Martyrion:* Confessing Christ with steadfast mind, ye endured the wounds of every torture, O valiant sufferers; therefore are ye called blessed.[^8383]

*Martyrion:* Having crossed over the surging storm of all tortures, ye attained unto the ha-...[^8384]

...ven of the heavenly kingdom, being filled with true stillness, O Martyrs.[^8385]

*Theotokion:* O God the Word, Who by Thy will didst create all things, wast incarnate of thee beyond word, O Virgin; Him, therefore, pray fervently for all.[^8386]

*Canon to the Bodiless Powers. Ode 5. Heirmos:*[^8387] Unto Thee I rise early at dawn, and unto Thee I cry aloud, O tender-hearted Lord: having enlightened my soul, darkened by sins, with the light of Thy commandments, direct me.[^8388]

*Troparion 1:* With most sacred and eloquent lips the Seraphim hymn the pre-unoriginate and pre-divine Principle, being enlightened by immaterial rays of light.[^8389]

*Troparion 2:* With sacred and multi-radiant wings the Cherubim cover themselves, not daring to gaze upon the divine radiance, the creations of pre-divine wisdom.[^8390]

*Troparion 3:* Intelligently enjoying the most beauteous illumination of the divine and all-rich One, the glorious Thrones are shown forth super-mundanely as beholders of ineffable things.[^8391]

*Theotokion:* The pre-divine Mind is mingled with the human, O Most-pure One, in thy most pure and all-holy womb, having united without confusion and without change according to hypostasis.[^8392]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*[^8393] Jonah cried aloud from the belly of hades: Lead up my life from corruption; and we cry aloud unto Thee: O all-powerful Savior, have mercy on us [cf. Jonah 2:3, 7].[^8394]

*Troparion 1:* The abyss of transgressions hath encompassed me, I have gone down into the depth of perdition: lead me up, O Word, even as once Jonah, from corruption unto life.[^8395]

*Troparion 2:* The surge of thoughts tempests me; direct me unto the haven of true repentance, O Tender-hearted One, preserving my heart in stillness.[^8396]

*Martyrion:* Having protected themselves by faith, the Holy Martyrs turned away from the false flatteries of the torturers, and were not wounded by the darts of the enemy.[^8397]

*Martyrion:* Being lifted up unto God in love, the Martyrs hated worldly love, and were shown forth as friends unto the Creator of all things.[^8398]

*Theotokion:* We hymn thee, O all-hymned Maiden, who didst bear the most holy Word, Whom all the heavenly powers praise with unceasing voices.[^8399]

*Canon to the Bodiless Powers. The same Heirmos:*[^8400] Youthfully irradiated with the splendors of the Lord of principalities, and hymning His ineffable glory, the Dominions are ever manifested. (Twice)[^8401]

*Troparion 2:* Lovingly gazing upon the all-surpassing power, the divine Powers abide in strength, manifestly prevailing.[^8402]

*Theotokion:* Having sprung from a royal tribe, O Virgin, thou didst bear beyond nature the Word, the King of all; and thou didst truly remain a Virgin.[^8403]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*[^8404] Thou didst bedew the burning furnace, O Savior, and didst save the Children who sang and said: Blessed art Thou unto ages, O Lord God of our fathers [cf. Dan. 3:26, 50].[^8405]

*Troparion 1:* Sacrifice a sacrifice of praise unto God, O my soul; make haste and repent while the worldly marketplace endures: now traffic in the merchandise of good additions.[^8406]

*Troparion 2:* The visitation of death draws near, O soul; bring forth fruits worthy of repentance, lest like a barren tree thou be cast into the fire of Gehenna, and weep inconsolably [cf. Matt. 3:8, 10].[^8407]

*Martyrion:* The Holy Martyrs, having quenched the furnace of deception with showers of blood, cried aloud even as the Children: O God of our fathers, blessed art Thou...[^8408]

...O God of our fathers, blessed art Thou.

*Martyrion:* Ye became sons of the Light, having been joined unto the Desired Light, O Martyrs; and ye enlighten all that are in darkness, and drive away the darkness of deception.[^8409]

*Theotokion:* The bush unburnt prefigured thee, O Pure One; for thou, O Virgin, didst bear the unendurable Fire. Therefore I cry aloud unto thee: Consume my material passions.[^8410]

*Canon to the Bodiless Powers. Ode 7. Heirmos:*[^8411] The venerable youths cast into the furnace turned the fire into dew, crying aloud in song thus: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God of our fathers [cf. Dan. 3:50].[^8412]

*Troparion 1:* Unswervingly glorifying Thy throne, O Christ, the intellectual angelic choirs manifestly encircling it cry aloud, saying: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers! (Twice)[^8413]

*Troparion 2:* With twofold zeal the Principalities, immaterially revolving around the One Principle, call aloud with unceasing doxologies: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers![^8414]

*Theotokion:* Unto Christ, one Hypostasis in two natures, O Mother of God, Whom without seed...[^8415]

...thou didst bear, accomplishing the fearful dispensation: O God of our fathers![^8416]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*[^8417] Emulating the Children in the furnace, having received spiritual dew, with faith we call aloud: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord [cf. Dan. 3:57].[^8418]

*Troparion 1:* I received the Word as a shining lamp, but wretched that I am, I inclined unto irrational passions, and ever walk in evil darkness.[^8419]

*Troparion 2:* The Lord is near, even as we believe; be careful, O soul, and despond not; watch and cry aloud vigilantly: O tender-hearted Lover of mankind, save me.[^8420]

*Martyrion:* Having tasted of divine sweetness, ye endured painful bitterness, and now enjoy the most divine communions of the Word, O Martyrs.[^8421]

*Martyrion:* Ye entered into divine rest, ye attained unto the good things for which ye hoped, O all-praised Martyrs! Therefore do we worthily call you blessed.[^8422]

*Theotokion:* Human nature was loosed from the curse by thy giving of birth, O Virgin; for thou didst bear the All-blessed One, Who adorns all things with blessings.[^8423]

*Canon to the Bodiless Powers. Ode 8. Heirmos:*[^8424] The one unoriginate King of glory, Whom the heavenly hosts bless, and before Whom the angelic ranks tremble: praise Him, ye priests, and highly exalt Him, O people, unto all ages [cf. Dan. 3:56–57].[^8425]

*Troparion 1:* Ye Archangels who have transferred all desire unto God, and enjoy the rays proceeding thence: entreat Christ, the King of all, that they who hymn you may be delivered from tribulations. (Twice)[^8426]

*Troparion 2:* O most honorable minds, free from every alteration of material passion, O Angels! save all who with you love to highly exalt Christ unto all ages.[^8427]

*Theotokion:* Having ineffably received in thy womb the unapproachable Light, O Virgin Mother of God, thou didst enlighten those who abide in the darkness of this life, to glorify piously Christ Who ineffably came forth from thee.[^8428]

*Then: My soul magnifies the Lord [LXX Ps. 146:1 / Luke 1:46–55].*[^8429]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*[^8430] The Mother beyond nature, and Virgin by nature, alone blessed among women: with songs, ye faithful, we magnify the Mother of God.[^8431]

*Troparion 1:* Behold, the judgment draws near, and having the deeds of condemnation, I am in de-...[^8432]

...spair; O righteous Judge, Christ God, condemn me not.[^8433]

*Troparion 2:* Even as the faithful Canaanite woman, I cry unto Thee: Have mercy on me! Even as the woman bowed down of old, straighten me, that I may walk upright along Thy paths, O Lover of mankind [cf. Matt. 15:22; Luke 13:11–13].[^8434]

*Martyrion:* Ye stripped off all wickedness along with your garments, and through the vesture of severe tortures ye procured for yourselves the robe of glory, O Sufferers.[^8435]

*Martyrion:* The divine land of the living, Sion the city on high, received you as firstborn, who are illumined by the beauties of your struggles, O Sufferers.[^8436]

*Theotokion:* With the illuminating rays of the Word Who shone forth from thee, O pure Virgin, enlighten me who am covered in the darkness of sins and passions.[^8437]

*Canon to the Bodiless Powers. Ode 9. Heirmos:*[^8438] O all-hymned one, higher than the heavens, who didst conceive the unoriginate Word without seed, and didst bear the incarnate God unto men: therefore do we all magnify thee.[^8439]

*Troparion 1:* Enjoying the splendors of the light poured forth from the Source of Godhead, all the angelic choirs unceasingly hymn the all-glorified God, and glorify Him, ever magnifying Him. (Twice)[^8440]

*Troparion 2:* That I may be delivered from the dominion of passions, O Cherubim and Seraphim, Powers, Authorities, Angels, Archangels, Principalities and Thrones, and Dominions: fervently pray unto Christ![^8441]

*Theotokion:* As a Mother who now hast boldness toward thy Son, O Mother of God: deliver those who hymn thee with love from cruel transgressions, diseases, and tribulations, O All-holy One, that we all may ever magnify thee.[^8442]

*It is truly meet: Litany, Exapostilarion, and the usual Psalms.*[^8443]

*At the Aposticha, Penitential Stichera, Tone 7.*[^8444]

*Sticheron 1:* As the barren fig tree, cut me not down, O Savior, sinner that I am; but grant me delay for many years, watering my soul with tears of repentance, that I may bring forth unto Thee the fruit of almsgiving [cf. Luke 13:6–9].[^8445]

*Verse: We were filled in the morning with Thy mercy, O Lord: - - - And look upon Thy servants, and upon Thy works, and guide their sons [LXX Ps. 89:14, 16].*[^8446]

*Sticheron 2:* As Thou art the righteous Sun, enlighten the hearts of those who hymn Thee: O Lord, glory to Thee![^8447]

*Verse: And let the brightness of the Lord our God be upon us, and the works of our hands... [LXX Ps. 89:17].*[^8448]

...direct Thou upon us; and direct Thou the work of our hands [LXX Ps. 89:17].[^8449]

*Martyrion:* Celebrating the memory of Thy holy passion-bearers, we hymn Thee: O Lord, glory to Thee![^8450]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Make peaceful our life through the prayers of the Mother of God, who cry aloud unto Thee: O merciful Lord, glory to Thee![^8451]

*It is good to give praise: Trisagion. After Our Father: Troparion, Litany, First Hour, and Dismissal.*[^8452]

*On Monday, the Beatitudes, Tone 7.*[^8453]

*Troparion 1:* Fair was the fruit that put me to death, and good for food; but Christ is the Tree of Life, of Which eating I die not, but cry aloud with the thief: Remember me, O Lord, in Thy kingdom [cf. Gen. 3:6; Luke 23:42].[^8454]

*Troparion 2:* Even as the faithful Canaanite woman, I cry in the sickness of my heart: Have mercy on me, O Savior, as Thou art good; for I have a soul suffering distress by all manner of enemy wiles, and ever tempest-tossed [cf. Matt. 15:22].[^8455]

*Troparion 3:* Cherubim and Seraphim, and Thrones, Principalities, and Powers ever, Archangels, armies of Angels, and Dominions, and the assemblies of the all-wise Authorities, glorify Thee, O Benefactor and Lord.[^8456]

*Martyrion:* Being elevated unto God, the Sufferers utterly cast down the wicked exaltations of the enemy; and having been shown forth as victors, they now dwell with joy in the heavens, radiant with incorruptible glory.[^8457]

*Glory... Triadikon:* I worship the unoriginate Father, with the Son and the Spirit, the uncreated Essence and Nature, Who beneficially created all things from non-being, Which is the Trinity and is known as the Unity.[^8458]

*Both now... Theotokion:* I have defiled myself with worldly pleasures; unto thee the undefiled have I fled, O Virgin: from every blemish and transgression deliver my all-wretched soul, that I may call thee blessed, who art ever-blessed.[^8459]

## Tuesday: The Holy Forerunner[^8460]

*On Monday Evening.*[^8461]
*On "Lord, I have cried," Penitential Stichera, Tone 7.*[^8462]
*Tone 7, Podoben: "Today Judas watches" (Дне́сь бди́тъ І҆ꙋ́да):*[^8463]

*Sticheron 1:* O Master and all-merciful God, by whatever judgments Thou knowest, grant me to have Thy fear in my heart; grant me to abhor the deeds of the evil one; grant me to love Thee with all my soul, and to do Thy saving will; for Thou art our God, Who didst say: Ask, and ye shall receive [cf. Matt. 7:7].[^8464]

*Sticheron 2:* I became indeed a laughingstock unto demons, and an abasement unto men, a lamentation unto the righteous, and a weeping unto Angels, a defilement unto the air, and unto the earth and waters; for I befouled my body, and defiled soul and mind with countless deeds, and became an enemy of God. Woe is me, O Lord! I have sinned against Thee, I have sinned, forgive me.[^8465]

*Sticheron 3:* I pray Thee, be long-suffering toward me who am barren, O Master! And cut me not down as a barren tree by the cutting of death, sending me into the fire; but be entreated to make me fruitful, granting me time for repentance as the Lover of mankind, that I may wash away, O Christ my Savior, my many sins.[^8466]

*Then the Stichera from the Menaion for the Saint. Glory... to the Saint. Both now... Theotokion of the Tone. But if there is no Menaion:*[^8467]

*Other Stichera, to the Holy Prophet and Forerunner John, Tone 7.*[^8468]

*Sticheron 1:* O thou who didst live a blameless life in the wilderness: renew my mind, which is desolate with sins, O Forerunner.[^8469]

*Sticheron 2:* Knowing thee to be the most beauteous turtledove, O Prophet, we glorify thee, who didst proclaim the divine spring unto those in the world.[^8470]

*Sticheron 3:* O protector of my life, guardian of my soul, divine Forerunner: deliver me, thy servant, from the deceitful tongue of man.[^8471]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Rejoice, O Pure One, honorable wealth of virginity! Rejoice, O Honorable One, divine adornment! Rejoice, O Virgin, salvation of the faithful! Rejoice, O Sovereign Lady, Bride unwedded![^8472]

*O Gladsome Light: and the rest.*[^8473]

*At the Aposticha, Penitential Stichera, Tone 7.*[^8474]

*Sticheron 1:* As the prodigal son, I have come, O Tender-hearted One...[^8475]

...receive me who fall down, as one of Thy hired servants, O God, and have mercy on me, O Lover of mankind [cf. Luke 15:19].[^8476]

*Verse: Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes, O Thou that dwellest in heaven: - - - So our eyes look unto the Lord our God, until He have mercy on us [LXX Ps. 122:1–2].*[^8477]

*Sticheron 2:* As he that fell among thieves and was wounded, so also have I fallen through many sins, and my soul is wounded; unto whom shall I flee, guilty that I am? Only unto Thee, the tender-hearted Physician of souls: pour out upon me, O God, Thy great mercy [cf. Luke 10:30–35].[^8478]

*Verse: Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us: - - - For our soul hath been exceedingly filled with the reproach of them that are at ease, and with the contempt of the proud [LXX Ps. 122:3–4].*[^8479]

*Martyrion:* Holy Martyrs, who have suffered well and received crowns: pray ye unto the Lord, that our souls may be saved.[^8480]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Rejoice, O cloud of the noetic and ineffable Sun, O Sovereign Lady! Rejoice, all-radiant torch! Rejoice, all-golden lampstand: through thee, O All-holy One, was Eve delivered from the curse. But as thou hast boldness toward thy Son and God Who is easily reconciled: by thy maternal prayer, fail not to pray, O Most-pure One.[^8481]

*Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart: Trisagion. After Our Father: Troparion, Litany, and Dismissal.*[^8482]

*On Monday at Small Compline.*[^8483]
*Supplicatory Canon to the Most Holy Mother of God, Tone 7. Ode 1. Heirmos:*[^8484] Unto Him Who crushed wars by His arm, and drowned the captains of the chariots, let us sing as unto our Redeemer and God, for He hath been glorified [cf. Exod. 15:3–4].[^8485]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Mother of God, save us.*[^8486]

*Troparion 1:* Thee, the cause of our salvation and divine redemption, do we entreat, O Most-pure One: pray that we may be saved.[^8487]

*Troparion 2:* I am wholly in cruel temptations, in afflictions and transgressions; I cry unto thee, O Most-pure One: save me, thy servant.[^8488]

*Glory...* Thee, the pure Virgin and Mother of the Creator, do we entreat: deliver us from every maltreatment of demons.[^8489]

*Both now...* O thou who didst bear the Word in the flesh beyond word, Christ the Author of life and our God: pray that we may be saved.[^8490]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*[^8491] The Church of Christ is established in faith; for unceasingly with songs she cries aloud, singing: Holy art Thou, O Lord, and my spirit hymns Thee![^8492]

*Troparion 1:* Walking along the path of all-destructive malice, I found not the way of my salvation: direct me unto it, O all-blameless Sovereign Lady.[^8493]

*Troparion 2:* Thee alone do I write down as my strength, and establishment, and help, and hope, O All-immaculate One: be thou an intercessor for me in the day of my departure.[^8494]

*Glory...* I ever pray thee, O All-immaculate One: in the hour of my death stand by me, and deliver thy servant from torment.[^8495]

*Both now...* Thou wast born of the Virgin ineffably, Thou wast manifested as our Savior, and didst renew the world, O Lord.[^8496]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*[^8497] I have heard Thy report, and was afraid; I understood Thy works, and was filled with dread, O Lord [cf. Hab. 3:2].[^8498]

*Troparion 1:* Having transgressed the commandments of my Master, I was easily held fast by the enemy: do thou deliver me, O Sovereign Lady.[^8499]

*Troparion 2:* In the hour of death deliver me, O Most-pure One, and save my passionate soul from demons.[^8500]

*Glory...* Loose, O most pure Sovereign Lady, the bonds of my transgressions, and be an advocate unto eternal life for me through thy prayers.[^8501]

*Both now...* We hymn thee as Virgin again after childbearing, and glorify thee, O Most-pure One, as the Mother of our God.[^8502]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*[^8503] Rising early at dawn, O Word, unto Thy glory and praise, we unceasingly praise the form of Thy Cross, which Thou hast given unto us as a weapon unto help.[^8504]

*Troparion 1:* O haven for those tempest-tossed in a cruel storm, door of salvation for those saved by faith, O good Virgin: save me, thy servant.[^8505]

*Troparion 2:* Thy many tender mercies, O Virgin Mother of God, manifest richly upon me, thy servant, delivering me from the fearful and coming judgment.[^8506]

*Glory...* The unstable night of passions holds fast my soul, and dispatches it unto the pit of destruction; by the light of thy prayer, O Mother of God, save me, thy servant.[^8507]

*Both now...* O all-holy Virgin full of grace, who didst bear the Word in time beyond...[^8508]

...word: pray Him to save our souls.[^8509]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*[^8510] Jonah cried aloud from the belly of hades: Lead up my life from corruption; and we cry aloud unto Thee: O all-powerful Savior, have mercy on us [cf. Jonah 2:3, 7].[^8511]

*Troparion 1:* In temptations we have thee as a firm protectress, in tribulations a strong salvation, rest in sorrows, help in distresses, O Ever-virgin.[^8512]

*Troparion 2:* Show thyself unto me as guardian in this life, O Most-pure One; deliver me from demons in the hour of death; and after death give me rest.[^8513]

*Glory...* Thou art the beauty of the honorable and holy Angels, the joy of all men, O Virgin Mother of God: direct me unto life.[^8514]

*Both now...* Heal, O All-immaculate One, my wretched soul, incurably sick with the deceits of life and with the shutting of many doors.[^8515]

*Then: Lord, have mercy (40 times). Glory... Both now...*[^8516]

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 7:*[^8517] For the tender-heartedness of Thy mercy, O Christ God, Thou wast pleased to be incarnate of the Holy Virgin; for her sake preserve our life, as Thou art the Lover of mankind.[^8518]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*[^8519] Thou didst bedew the burning furnace, O Savior, and didst save the Children who sang and said: Blessed art Thou unto ages, O Lord God of our fathers [cf. Dan. 3:26, 50].[^8520]

*Troparion 1:* Considering the multitude of my transgressions, I fall into despair, O all-blameless Virgin! Therefore I cry unto thee: Help me, that I perish not utterly.[^8521]

*Troparion 2:* Knowing thee to be the Mother of Life, O Pure One, I cry unto thee: Deliver me from the death of the soul, and account me worthy of eternal life.[^8522]

*Glory...* Deliver, O All-immaculate One, from afflictions, and passions, and diseases, and maltreatment, and from the sins of this life, and from the unquenchable eternal fire, those who faithfully honor thee with songs.[^8523]

*Both now...* Every tongue glorifies thee, O Pure One, who art the glory and praise of our race, and the guide of the errant, O all-hymned Mother of God.[^8524]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*[^8525] Him Who is fearful unto the Cherubim, and marvelous unto the Seraphim, and the Creator of the world: ye priests, and servants, and spirits of the righteous, praise, bless, and highly exalt Him unto all ages [cf. Dan. 3:56–57].[^8526]

*Troparion 1:* All my life have I spent in laziness, wretch that I am, and now I am at a loss, and have drawn near unto the end: help me, O Sovereign Lady.[^8527]

*Troparion 2:* Thou art the refuge of sinners, the correction of the fallen, O Sovereign Lady: therefore do I flee unto thy protection; save me.[^8528]

*Glory...* As thou hast sleepless prayer, O all-blameless Sovereign Lady of the world: deliver from the coming judgment those who honor thine icon with faith.[^8529]

*Both now...* Every tongue sings thy glory, for thou didst bear the all-hymned God, O Virgin Bride of God! Him unceasingly pray, that the souls of those who hymn thee may be saved.[^8530]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*[^8531] The Mother beyond nature, and Virgin by nature, alone blessed among women: with songs, ye faithful, we magnify the Mother of God.[^8532]

*Troparion 1:* They who flee with faith beneath thy tender mercy are delivered from the grievous afflictions of this life; therefore have I also fled unto thy protection, O Mother of God.[^8533]

*Troparion 2:* O strong protectress, invincible rampart of the sorrowful, O Pure One: save me from the passions of sins, and from the eternal fire.[^8534]

*Glory...* With the bright splendors of the Word Who shone forth from thee, O pure Virgin, enlighten me and save me as thou art good, and snatch me from torments.[^8535]

*Both now...* Thou carriest in thy holy arms Him Who upholds all things: Him entreat, O Pure One, that we who are not held fast by the enemy's malice may be saved.[^8536]

*It is truly meet: Trisagion. After Our Father: Troparia and the rest as usual.*[^8537]

*On Tuesday at Matins.*[^8538]
*After the 1st Stichologia, Penitential Sessional Hymns, Tone 7.*[^8539]

*Sessional Hymn 1:* Having the medicine of repentance, O my soul, draw near falling down, and calling aloud with sighing unto the Physician of souls and bodies: Liberate me, O Lover of mankind, from my many transgressions; number me with the harlot, and the thief, and the publican, and grant me, O God, the forgiveness of mine iniquities, and save me.[^8540]

*Verse: O Lord, rebuke me not in Thine anger, nor chasten me in Thy wrath [LXX Ps. 6:2].*[^8541]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* O Lord and Lover of mankind, Who by tears didst cleanse Peter's denial, and by sighing didst forgive the publican's transgressions: have mercy on me.[^8542]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Rejoice, thou who didst contain in thy womb Him Whom the heavens cannot contain! Rejoice, O Virgin, the proclamation of the prophets, through whom Emmanuel shone forth! Rejoice, O Mother of Christ God![^8543]

*After the 2nd Stichologia, Sessional Hymns, Tone 7.*[^8544]

*Sessional Hymn 1:* I have not emulated the repentance of the publican, nor have I acquired the tears of the harlot; for I fall into despair from the perplexity of such amendment: yet by Thy tender compassion save me, O God, as Thou art the Lover of mankind.[^8545]

*Verse: O Lord, rebuke me not in Thine anger, nor chasten me in Thy wrath [LXX Ps. 6:2].*[^8546]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* O Savior, Who didst accept the tears of the harlot and of Peter, and didst justify the publican who sighed from the depths: have compassion also upon me, who am in despair because of my deeds, and save me.[^8547]

*Verse: Marvelous is God in His saints, the God of Israel [LXX Ps. 67:36].*[^8548]

*For the Martyrs:* Having contended on earth, O Lord, Thy Saints trampled down the enemy and abolished the idolatrous deceit; therefore they also received crowns from Thee, the Master and Lover of mankind, the merciful God, Who grantest great mercy unto the world.[^8549]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* As thou hast compassion for our lowliness, and coverest the people who are in distress upon earth: be compassionate, O blessed Mother of God; abide in prayer that we perish not grievously; as thou art pure, entreat the propitious God, O most holy Virgin, to save our souls.[^8550]

*After the 3rd Stichologia, Sessional Hymns, Tone 7.*[^8551]

*Tone 7, Podoben: "Fruit of thy womb" (Пло́дъ чре́ва твоегѡ̀):*[^8552]

*Sessional Hymn 1:* Even as the harlot I fall down before Thee, O Lover of mankind, though in tears I am altogether at a loss for compunction: by the prayers of the Forerunner, pity me even as her, O Merciful One, and save me.[^8553]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* O Lord, with the hyssop of Thy mercy be compassionate, and wash away the filth of my soul as Thou art good, O Savior; and having cleansed me from the filth of the mire of passions, have mercy on me, O Master: save Thine own creation through the supplications of Thy Forerunner, O Thou Who alone art abundant in mercy.[^8554]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Thou art the fervent protectress and defender of Christians, O all-hymned Mother of God; there-...[^8555]

...fore, together with the Forerunner, pray Thy Son that we may find mercy.[^8556]

*Penitential Canon to our Lord Jesus Christ, and to His Holy Martyrs. By Joseph.*[^8557]
*Tone 7. Ode 1. Heirmos:* Unto Him Who with His arm crushed battles and drowned the chariot-captains, let us sing, as unto our Deliverer and God, for He hath been glorified [cf. Exod. 15:1, 4].[^8558]

*Refrain: Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee.*[^8559]

*Troparion 1:* I ever sin, and fear Thee not, O Christ, Who in long-suffering seekest my repentance: grant me a thought of conversion, as Thou art good, and despise me not.[^8560]

*Troparion 2:* Adding sins unto sins, O Christ, I, wretched one, in no wise cease: O Thou Who alone art Good and sinless, have compassion on me and save me.[^8561]

*For the Martyrs:* Boldly the valiant sufferers cried aloud: Behold the arena filled with contests! Let us run: Christ the Master of the contest sits as president, crowning the victors over the enemy![^8562]

*For the Martyrs:* Ye stripped off the body by manifold wounds, and were clothed with the robe of incorruption, O wise Martyrs, and became sons of the Father of compassions.[^8563]

*Theotokion:* Heal my soul, afflicted by many sins, O Virgin Mother of God, that with voices of thanksgiving I may diligently and ever glorify thee.[^8564]

*Another Canon, to the Honorable and Great Prophet, John the Forerunner. By Joseph.*[^8565]
*Tone 7. Ode 1. Heirmos:* At Thy nod the watery nature, before easily poured forth, was changed into earthly form, O Lord; therefore Israel, having journeyed on foot without wetting his feet, sings unto Thee a song of victory [cf. Exod. 14:21–22].[^8566]

*Troparion 1:* Adorned as the beauty of the Church hast thou been manifested, O blessed Forerunner: by thy prayers ever save her from every tempest of heretics, strong and steadfast.[^8567]

*Troparion 2:* As an immaculate and sacred sacrifice wast thou offered unto the Creator, being slain, O divine Forerunner, like an innocent lamb; therefore with faith I pray thee: deliver me from all malice of the enemy.[^8568]

*Troparion 3:* Heal our bodily and spiritual infirmities, O ever-glorious Forerunner, praying ever to the Word...[^8569]

...Who compassionately took upon Himself the sicknesses and infirmities of all [cf. Matt. 8:17].[^8570]

*Theotokion:* Thou didst bear, O All-holy One, the incomprehensible Word, Who in flesh was hypostatically united unto us; Him ever pray, to save those who with faith ever call thee blessed.[^8571]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* O Thou Who by Thy word didst establish the heavens, and on many waters didst strengthen the earthly foundation: establish my mind in Thy will, O Lover of mankind [cf. Ps. 32:6; 135:6 LXX].[^8572]

*Troparion 1:* O Christ, Who overlookest the sins of men because of Thine abundant love for mankind: O only Savior, overlook my many evils, that I may glorify Thee Who art all-good.[^8573]

*Troparion 2:* That I might fulfill carnal desires, I, shameless one, despised Thy desires, O Christ, and I dread the fiery torment, O Word; from which deliver me.[^8574]

*For the Martyrs:* Having communion with bodily afflictions, the sufferers gazed upon the painless life and respite; which having attained in joy, they ever lighten afflictions for the faithful.[^8575]

*For the Martyrs:* With the rays of your contests, O passion-bearers, ye dispelled the cruel night of deceit, and passed over unto the unwaning light, taking away the darkness of our infirmities.[^8576]

*Theotokion:* The prophet foresaw thee as the gate of God, through which He Himself alone passed, even as He knows, O most pure Virgin; therefore I pray thee: do thou thyself open unto me the gates of repentance [cf. Ezek. 44:2].[^8577]

*Canon to the Forerunner. Ode 3. Heirmos:* O Lord and Savior, Who in the beginning didst establish the heavens by Thy powerful Word, and all their host by the all-effecting and divine Spirit: establish me upon the immovable rock of Thy confession [cf. Ps. 32:6 LXX].[^8578]

*Troparion 1:* With sun-like lightnings thou enlightenest all creation, for thou wast shown forth as a bright star of the spiritual Sun, O Forerunner; Him diligently entreat, to drive away the darkness of passions from our all-afflicted hearts.[^8579]

*Troparion 2:* Standing between the Law and grace, O divine Prophet, thou clearly makest known unto all the cessation of the one, and the pure shining forth of the other, a perfect renewal for those grown old in sins.[^8580]

*Troparion 3:* From demonic assault, from the temptations of life, and from every tribulation deliver us, we cry aloud, O Baptist of Christ, praying...[^8581]

...unto the All-good One; and in the day of judgment free us from torments.[^8582]

*Theotokion:* O blessed Lady, who ineffably didst bear God: together with His divine Baptist, pray unceasingly, O pure Lady, for us who fall into worldly temptations and are held fast by sins.[^8583]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Confirming Thy coming in the flesh, O Christ, the Prophet Habakkuk cried aloud: Glory to Thy power, O Lord [cf. Hab. 3:2].[^8584]

*Troparion 1:* I wandered away from the path leading me unto life, and fell into the pit of evils: O Savior, despise me not.[^8585]

*Troparion 2:* Send down streams of tears unto me, O Master, Word of God, that I may wash away the mire of my many transgressions.[^8586]

*For the Martyrs:* Led like lambs unto the slaughter, the Martyrs slew the battling enemy, being accounted worthy of glory [cf. Isa. 53:7].[^8587]

*For the Martyrs:* Poured forth, the streams of your blood, O all-praised ones, prepared for all the reception of the torrents of delight [cf. LXX Ps. 35:9].[^8588]

*Theotokion:* The Lord was incarnate of thy pure blood, granting repentance unto all men, O Maiden, through thine intercession.[^8589]

*Canon to the Forerunner. Ode 4. Heirmos:* Leaving not the Father's bosom, and descending unto earth, O Christ God, I have heard the mystery of Thy dispensation, and I glorified Thee, O only Lover of mankind.[^8590]

*Troparion 1:* Having overthrown the host of adversaries, and valiantly triumphed over them in brightness, O Baptist: overthrow by thy prayers the sin that reigns within me, I pray thee.[^8591]

*Troparion 2:* Having appeared as a spiritual lamp, O blessed one, thou didst show forth unto men Jesus, the great Sun of righteousness; pray that the hearts of all may be illumined by Him [cf. John 5:35; Mal. 4:2].[^8592]

*Troparion 3:* Conceived and born in iniquities, I live in negligence, and fear the torments of that place; snatch me from them, O Baptist, by entreating God [cf. LXX Ps. 50:7].[^8593]

*Troparion 4:* Bring supplications unto God for us who honor thee, O Baptist, that He may deliver us from every grievous distress and demonic harm, we pray.[^8594]

*Theotokion:* The shadows of the Law manifoldly foreshadowed thee who gavest birth to God; Him entreat, O all-immaculate Lady, from all...[^8595]

...iniquity and bodily passions to deliver me.[^8596]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* O Thou Who didst disperse the night of passions, shine upon me the spiritual light, having driven away the primordial darkness of the abyss, and Who didst cause the first-created light to shine upon the world, O Creator of all [cf. Gen. 1:2–3].[^8597]

*Troparion 1:* Deliver me, O Word, from Thy righteous wrath when Thou judgest the earth; and having cleansed me from many transgressions by repentance, show me forth as a temple of Thy goodness, O only Creator of all.[^8598]

*Troparion 2:* By the heavy gloom of passions I am blinded in mind, and know not what I do, having become insensate in heart: convert me, O Christ, and grant me repentance that cleanses from defilements.[^8599]

*For the Martyrs:* Having run youthfully through the stadium filled with sweat, O sufferers, ye attained unto heavenly brightness, receiving the honors of victory from the life-originating hand; therefore ye rejoice now.[^8600]

*For the Martyrs:* By the strong sinews of your sacred sufferings, O sufferers, ye truly strangled the prince, the evil serpent, and were accounted worthy of the sweetness of paradise; therefore we praise you.[^8601]

*Theotokion:* O bright cloud of the Sun, make to shine upon me the spiritual light of true repentance, and drive away the darkness of wicked thoughts, that with faith I may sing of thee as the salvation of the faithful.[^8602]

*Canon to the Forerunner. Ode 5. Heirmos:* O Lord my God, rising early from the night I pray unto Thee: grant me remission of my transgressions, and direct my paths unto the light of Thy commandments, I pray [cf. Isa. 26:9 LXX].[^8603]

*Troparion 1:* Thou didst sprout forth as the voice of one crying in desolate thoughts the knowledge of God, O most wise one; therefore I pray thee: renew my soul, made desolate by all manner of transgressions [cf. Isa. 40:3; Matt. 3:3].[^8604]

*Troparion 2:* An honorable vessel of the Master wast thou shown forth, O divine Prophet: deliver me from unclean deeds by thy prayers, and entreat the Benefactor that I may obtain eternal honor.[^8605]

*Troparion 3:* Iniquities have drawn near against me by the entrance of negligence; make me better through patterns of repentance, O blessed Forerunner, that I may zealously walk in the ways of the Lord.[^8606]

*Theotokion:* Mortify the earthly mindsets of my flesh, O Mother of God, who didst bear the Life...[^8607]

...who by death utterly destroyed death by divine power, O all-holy and pure one.[^8608]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Having fallen into the depth of sins, O Good One, as Jonah from the whale I cry unto Thee: Lead up my life from corruption, and save me, O Lover of mankind [cf. Jonah 2:3, 7].[^8609]

*Troparion 1:* A new prodigal was I shown forth, having lived vilely upon the earth, and bowed down to the impulse of lusts; convert me, O Christ my God, and save me as Thou art the Lover of mankind [cf. Luke 15:11–32].[^8610]

*Troparion 2:* Sigh, O my soul, that thou mayest be delivered from sighing; weep, that thou mayest not experience unceasing tears there, which have no profit in affliction.[^8611]

*For the Martyrs:* Ye were clearly manifested as stones set in the crown of Christ's Church, and became her beauteous ornament, O honorable Great-martyrs.[^8612]

*For the Martyrs:* Having attained an end worthy of God, O most wise ones, ye inherit endless recompenses; therefore pray, O Martyrs, that our ends may be in repentance.[^8613]

*Theotokion:* From the depth of negligence and immeasurable evils lead me up, O good Lady, who didst bear the abyss of compassion, and grant me a fountain of tears, O Ever-virgin.[^8614]

*Canon to the Forerunner. Ode 6. Heirmos:* Jonah cried aloud from the belly of hades: Lead up my life from corruption; and we cry aloud unto Thee: O all-powerful Savior, have mercy on us [cf. Jonah 2:3, 7].[^8615]

*Troparion 1:* Clearly shining with the rays of virtues, and beaming with radiant martyrdom, thou enlightenest all creation, O close friend of the spiritual Dayspring [cf. Luke 1:78].[^8616]

*Troparion 2:* Thou didst sprout forth from a barren aged woman, O blessed one; therefore I cry unto thee: renew me, who have grown old through many sins, by the beauties of repentance through thy prayers.[^8617]

*Troparion 3:* O lamp of the unwaning Light, divine Prophet! kindle by thy prayers the extinguished lamp of my heart, and make me a partaker of the divine Light.[^8618]

*Theotokion:* Even as rain the Word came down into thy womb: Him entreat, O most pure Virgin, to dry up the torrents of mine immeasurable evils, I pray thee [cf. LXX Ps. 71:6].[^8619]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* The fire touched not, nor distressed, Thy Children in the furnace, O Savior. Then the three, as with one mouth, sang and blessed, saying: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers [cf. Dan. 3:26, 50].[^8620]

*Troparion 1:* I fell into passions of dishonor, I was likened unto the beasts, O Savior, and being darkened I perceive not Thy long-suffering expectation, O Word: grant me time for conversion, and save me [cf. LXX Ps. 48:13].[^8621]

*Troparion 2:* In negligence have I ended my life, doing what is not lawful to do; and lo, now at last I draw near to the gates of hades without gaining understanding: despise me not, O Christ, Who alone art good.[^8622]

*For the Martyrs:* Having desired life eternal, ye died unto the world, O most wise ones; and having utterly slain the enemy, ye flew up unto the heavens, ever praying for us, O sufferers.[^8623]

*For the Martyrs:* Being released from bodily constraints, ye loosed the bonds of deceit, and with firm love bound your souls unto Christ, O Martyrs, Who was bound in the flesh and dissolved the curse.[^8624]

*Theotokion:* The prophet foresaw thee as a new scroll, wherein the Word of the Father was written; therefore, O pure Lady, I pray thee: pray that I may be written in the book of the living, tearing up the handwriting of my many evils [cf. Isa. 8:1; Col. 2:14].[^8625]

*Canon to the Forerunner. Ode 7. Heirmos:* Of old the Children showed the fiery furnace to be dew-dropping, singing unto the one God and saying: Supremely exalted and most glorified is the God of our fathers [cf. Dan. 3:50].[^8626]

*Troparion 1:* Grant me showers of tears by thy prayers, O Forerunner, who didst wash the Abyss of compassion with river streams; and cleanse me from all filthiness, both of flesh and of spirit [cf. 2 Cor. 7:1].[^8627]

*Troparion 2:* Offer supplication, O blessed one, unto our God Who is over all, that He may also have mercy upon me as He is tender-hearted, who have sinned much and now have no uplifting.[^8628]

*Troparion 3:* O barren soul, make haste to repent, lest like the barren fig tree the divine judgment cut thee down from the root; cry aloud unto the Master: O God, having cleansed me, save me [cf. Luke 13:6–9].[^8629]

*Theotokion:* My soul, deadened by the evil wounds of transgressions...[^8630]

...I pray thee, O Lady, who by thy childbearing didst slay hades: quicken me by patterns of repentance.[^8631]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* Unto God Who is unceasingly glorified on high by the Angels, ye heavens of heavens, earth, and mountains, and hills, and the abyss, and all the race of mankind: with songs bless Him as Creator and Deliverer [cf. LXX Ps. 148].[^8632]

*Troparion 1:* Having superabundantly taken pleasure in harmful lusts, I, foolish and injured in mind, surpassed every sinner; O Thou Who hast an exceeding abundance of tender compassion, grant me cleansing of my transgressions.[^8633]

*Troparion 2:* Now the Bridegroom is at the doors! Adorn thy lamp, O soul, watering it with the oil of mercy and with all good deeds; before the door be shut, make haste to enter with Christ into incomparable joys [cf. Matt. 25:1–13].[^8634]

*For the Martyrs:* Fearing not torments, the valiant sufferers cried aloud: Behold the acceptable time! Let us all stand with a steadfast mind; let us acquire through small sufferings the painless life and unfading sweetness [cf. 2 Cor. 6:2].[^8635]

*For the Martyrs:* Ever watered with divine streams, O passion-bearers of the Savior, ye irrigate all the earth through imitation of your contests, and make her fruitful in virtues in Christ unto all ages.[^8636]

*Theotokion:* O Mother of God, sure protection and hope of Christians! In the fearful day stand by me who have sinned much, and deliver me from fearful Gehenna, numbering me with the sheep on the right hand [cf. Matt. 25:33].[^8637]

*Canon to the Forerunner. Ode 8. Heirmos:* The only unoriginate King of glory, Whom the heavenly hosts bless, and before Whom the angelic ranks tremble: praise Him, ye priests, and highly exalt Him, O people, unto all ages [cf. Dan. 3:56–57].[^8638]

*Troparion 1:* Cease not unto all ages, O Prophet, to pray the only Deliverer, Who was bound and granted release unto the bound, that I, who ever glorify thee, may be loosed from the bonds of my many transgressions.[^8639]

*Troparion 2:* I am wounded, O Forerunner, by the weapon of lusts, and in pain of heart I cry unto thee: heal the sicknesses of my soul, praying Christ, the only Physician of souls and bodies.[^8640]

*Troparion 3:* With thy hand, O Forerunner, fail not unceasingly to pray the Word Whom thou didst baptize...[^8641]

...to deliver me from the hand of sin, who have sinned much, and am humble and condemned.[^8642]

*Troparion 4:* Because I foolishly buried in the earth the talent entrusted unto me by God, I await bitter torment: from which snatch me by thy prayers, O Baptist, I faithfully pray thee [cf. Matt. 25:24–30].[^8643]

*Theotokion:* Thou didst remain unconsumed, having received the unendurable Fire into thy womb, O Virgin; therefore snatch me from the unquenchable fire, bedewing me now with the most excellent patterns of continual repentance.[^8644]

*My soul magnifies the Lord [LXX Ps. 146:1 / Luke 1:46–55].*[^8645]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* Who among men can tell the conception of a birth without seed? At the birth of an incorrupt birthgiving, who of the earthborn will not marvel? Therefore, O Mother of God, we, the earthly tribes, magnify thee [cf. Isa. 53:8].[^8646]

*Troparion 1:* That we may inherit future good things, let us weep, let us sigh, let us entreat Christ while we have time, ye faithful, for repentance and supplication.[^8647]

*Troparion 2:* Even as the Canaanite woman I cry unto Thee: Have mercy on me, O Christ; even as the woman bowed down of old, straighten me, O Jesus; and save me, O Savior, even as Peter, when sinking in sins [cf. Matt. 15:22; Luke 13:11–13; Matt. 14:30–31].[^8648]

*For the Martyrs:* Constrained by tribulations, prisons, and torments, the passion-bearing Martyrs passed over unto the broad space of consolation, delivering us from the constraint of transgressions.[^8649]

*For the Martyrs:* The earth indeed hath now covered your bodies, but heaven holds your spirits, O Saints, which ever stand before the throne of glory, rejoicing with the Angels.[^8650]

*Theotokion:* Having clothed Himself in me, the Lord came forth from thee, O most pure Lady: Him therefore pray, O Virgin, to enlighten me with a garment of light, who have now stripped off the most grievous sackcloth of passions.[^8651]

*Canon to the Forerunner. Ode 9. Heirmos:* O Mother who knewest not man, Virgin Mother of God, who didst bear without experience of corruption, and didst lend flesh unto the all-devising Word, receptacle of the Unendurable, dwelling-place of thine uncontainable Creator: thee do we magnify.[^8652]

*Troparion 1:* From a sacred root didst thou sprout forth, O Prophet, and didst uproot all evil roots, by which I am choked and become unprofitable; visit me, O blessed one, and direct me to sprout forth fruits of divine repentance...[^8653]

...The Church knows thee as the most beauteous turtledove and nightingale, O great Forerunner: for thou didst proclaim the song of repentance unto souls that were desolate and dominated by evils; therefore with faith we call thee blessed.[^8654]

*Troparion 2:* Thou hast shown unto all the paths leading unto the entrances of salvation, O glorious Forerunner; strengthen me to walk in them, who wander through all the pathless ways of life, and through deceit have committed wickedness.[^8655]

*Troparion 3:* The fearful day at the doors is drawn near, and having deeds of condemnation, I weep: O Lord, Lord, Who alone art merciful, by the prayers of Thy Forerunner and of all the Saints, show me then uncondemned.[^8656]

*Theotokion:* O thou who didst bear the divine Light, O woman full of the grace of God: enlighten my soul, darkened by transgressions, I pray thee, and show me free from eternal darkness, that I may magnify and glorify thee, the ever-blessed one.[^8657]

*It is truly meet: Litany, Exapostilarion, and the usual Psalms.*[^8658]

*At the Aposticha, Penitential Stichera, Tone 7.*[^8659]

*Sticheron 1:* As the barren fig tree, cut me not down, O Savior, a sinner; but grant me forgiveness for many years, watering my soul with tears of repentance, that I may bring forth unto Thee the fruit of almsgiving [cf. Luke 13:6–9].[^8660]

*Verse: We were filled in the morning with Thy mercy, O Lord: - - - and look upon Thy servants, and upon Thy works, and guide their sons [LXX Ps. 89:14, 16].*[^8661]

*Sticheron 2:* Being the Sun of righteousness, enlighten the hearts of those who sing unto Thee: O Lord, glory to Thee![^8662]

*Verse: And let the brightness of the Lord our God be upon us; and direct Thou the works of our hands upon us, yea, direct Thou the work of our hands [LXX Ps. 89:17].*[^8663]

*Sticheron 3 (For the Martyrs):* In the midst of the tribunal of the lawless, Thy passion-bearers rejoiced and cried aloud: O Lord, glory to Thee![^8664]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* O Christ, Thou hast shone forth as the Light from the Virgin, and hast enlightened the human race: O Lord, glory to Thee![^8665]

*On Tuesday, the Beatitudes, Tone 7.*[^8666]

*Troparion 1:* Beauteous was the fruit and good for food that slew me:...[^8667]

...Christ is the tree of life, whereof eating I die not, but cry aloud with the thief: Remember me, O Lord, in Thy kingdom [cf. Luke 23:42].[^8668]

*Troparion 2:* Heal the incurable wounds of my heart, O Lord, as Thou alone art the Physician of souls and bodies; and make me ever to walk uprightly along saving paths.[^8669]

*To the Forerunner:* O Forerunner of the righteous Sun, Baptist of Christ: kindle the lamp of my soul, which is quenched by much malice, through thy divine prayers, that being saved I may ever call thee blessed.[^8670]

*For the Martyrs:* Having suffered and received crowns, ye put the enemy to shame; and now ye dwell in the heavens, filled with unapproachable light, O all-wise Martyrs, praying for our souls.[^8671]

*Glory... Trinity:* Heal the incurable passions of my soul, O divine Trinity, Who in one Godhead art piously glorified; and snatch me from Gehenna and temptations, and grant me the eternal kingdom.[^8672]

*Both now... Theotokion:* Thou didst unconfinedly contain in thy womb the Uncontainable: Him ever entreat, O pure Mother, to deliver from every constraint and assault of passions thy servants who glorify thee with love.[^8673]

## Wednesday: The Precious and Life-Giving Cross[^8674]

*On Tuesday Evening.*[^8675]
*On "Lord, I have cried," Stichera of the Cross, Tone 7.*[^8676]
*Tone 7, Podoben: "No longer are we forbidden" (Не ктомꙋ̀ возбраня́еми є҆смы̀):*[^8677]

*Sticheron 1:* A tree cast me out of paradise of old; but now a tree hath led me unto paradise, since Thou wast crucified, O Christ.[^8678]

*Sticheron 2:* A fearful wonder! How the Creator, standing before His creation, is condemned and crucified for the salvation of men.[^8679]

*Sticheron 3:* O Cross of Christ, wonder of the holy Angels, and great wound unto the devil and demons: save thy servants![^8680]

*Then the Stichera from the Menaion for the Saint. Glory... to the Saint, if there be one. Both now... Stavrotheotokion of the Tone. But if there is no Menaion, we sing:*[^8681]

*Other Stichera of the Mother of God, Tone 7.*[^8682]
*Tone 7, Podoben: "Today Judas watches" (Дне́сь бди́тъ І҆ꙋ́да):*[^8683]

*Sticheron 1:* Seeing the Lamb stretched out upon the tree between two thieves, the immaculate Ewe-lamb and Virgin cried aloud: Woe is me, my sweetest Child! What is this fearful and most glorious mystery? How hath the lawless council lifted Thee up upon the Cross, and given gall to drink unto Thee Who didst satisfy men with manna?[^8684]

*Sticheron 2:* When the Virgin and Thy Mother beheld Thee nailed upon the Cross, O my Christ, by unlawful judgment of the Jews on the Place of the Skull, she cried aloud: Woe is me, my most beloved Son! What is this fearful sight? How do the senseless children of the Hebrews nail Thee, the Lord of all, unto the Cross?[^8685]

*Sticheron 3:* All we have known thee, O all-holy Sovereign Lady, to be revealed a Virgin after childbearing; and seeing Him Whom thou didst bear without seed having His hands nailed upon the tree, the Most-pure One wept and cried aloud: Willingly, O Long-suffering One, Thou diest, delivering from death all who hymn Thee.[^8686]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:* O all-holy Sovereign Lady Virgin, who didst bear the Lord Who for tender mercy's sake was nailed upon the Cross and poured forth streams of life for the world: pray Him that our souls may be saved; for we the faithful have thee alone as refuge, wall, and defense; therefore we also flee beneath thy protection.[^8687]

*At the Aposticha, Stichera of the Cross, Tone 7.*[^8688]

*Sticheron 1:* No longer are we forbidden the tree of life, having Thy Cross as our hope: O Lord, glory to Thee![^8689]

*Verse: Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes, O Thou that dwellest in heaven: - - - So our eyes look unto the Lord our God, until He have mercy on us [LXX Ps. 122:1–2].*[^8690]

*Sticheron 2:* Being suspended upon the tree, O Immortal One, Thou didst destroy the snares of the devil: O Lord, glory to Thee![^8691]

*Verse: Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us: - - - For our soul hath been exceedingly filled with the reproach of them that are at ease, and with the contempt of the proud [LXX Ps. 122:3–4].*[^8692]

*For the Martyrs:* Having been unmindful of all earthly things, O holy Martyrs, and having manfully preached Christ in the judgment hall, ye received from Him recompense for your torments; but as ye have boldness, entreat Him as the all-powerful God to save our souls who flee unto you, we pray.[^8693]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:* Beholding of old her Son upon the tree, her heart with a sword...[^8694]

...of sorrow did the Most-pure One pierce.[^8695]

*Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart: Trisagion, and the rest.*[^8696]

*On Tuesday at Small Compline.*[^8697]
*Supplicatory Canon to the Most Holy Mother of God, Tone 7.*[^8698]
*Ode 1. Heirmos:* At Thy nod the watery nature, before easily poured forth, was changed into earthly form, O Lord; therefore Israel, having journeyed on foot without wetting his feet, sings unto Thee a song of victory [cf. Exod. 14:21–22].[^8699]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Mother of God, save us.*[^8700]

*Troparion 1:* Still the storm of pleasures and of my passions by the calm of thy divine prayer, O All-immaculate One, that with a quiet heart I may glorify thine ineffable childbearing.[^8701]

*Troparion 2:* All desire and sweetness of salvation came to be by thy childbearing, O Sovereign Lady Mother of God: drive away from thy servants every bitterness arising from the passions of transgressions.[^8702]

*Glory...* Deliver me, O Most-pure One, from demonic deception, and mistreatment, and harm, and temptation, that with faith I may glorify thee who, after God, art my protectress and defense.[^8703]

*Both now...* Thou didst appear unto Moses at the bush, O Word of God, burning as fire, and yet utterly unconsumed, prefiguring Thy birth from the Virgin, for whose sake Thou didst take the form of man [cf. Exod. 3:2].[^8704]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* O Lord and Savior, Who in the beginning didst establish the heavens by Thy powerful Word, and all their host by the all-effecting and divine Spirit: establish me upon the immovable rock of Thy confession [cf. LXX Ps. 32:6].[^8705]

*Troparion 1:* Him Who was lifted up upon the Cross and cast down hades, O Mother of God: diligently entreat Him for those who ever faithfully hymn thee with sacred voices and songs, O hope of the world and defense of the faithful.[^8706]

*Troparion 2:* Higher than the heavens and more glorious than the Cherubim, having conceived thou didst bear God; therefore pray Him to deliver me from transgressions and from the fearful condemnation, through thy merciful supplication and through thy power surpassing word.[^8707]

*Glory...* O Sovereign Lady, who by thy childbearing didst change men from the corruption of death: deliver me from the corruption of passions and sicknesses, and from grievous transgressions, and grant me unaging life by thy di-...[^8708]

...vine intercession.[^8709]

*Both now...* Having unchangeably become like unto men in all things, O Word of God, Thou didst manifestly come forth from the pure Virgin, and didst reveal unto all the thrice-radiant Godhead in one undifferentiated essence and unchangeable hypostases.[^8710]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Leaving not the Father's bosom, and descending unto earth, O Christ God: I have heard the mystery of Thy dispensation, and I glorified Thee, O only Lover of mankind.[^8711]

*Troparion 1:* Extend thy mercy and tender compassion over thy servants, O Pure One, who ever pray thee, delivering them from fearful torments by thy prayers.[^8712]

*Troparion 2:* With pure prayer and fervent faith I flee unto thee, O All-immaculate One: be thou the propitiation for my many transgressions, delivering and saving me.[^8713]

*Glory...* Through thy childbearing, O Most-pure One, thou wast revealed as the mediatress of our joy, O Sovereign Lady: entreat Him, O All-immaculate One, to deliver me from tribulations and account me worthy of eternal life.[^8714]

*Both now...* Being not separated from the Father's bosom, and incarnate of the Virgin, O Christ God: preserve Thy flock belonging to Thy dispensation, which venerates Thy divine icons.[^8715]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* Night without light is unto the unbelieving, O Christ, but enlightenment unto the faithful in the sweetness of Thy words; therefore unto Thee I rise early at dawn, and hymn Thy Godhead.[^8716]

*Troparion 1:* Thee I pray, O pure Virgin, to deliver my soul from dark sin, and to illumine it with the divine ray of repentance of thy mercy.[^8717]

*Troparion 2:* Deliver by thy prayers and supplications those who are in the deep of grievous tribulations and dire passions, O Most-holy and Pure One, and save those who praise thee.[^8718]

*Glory...* O all-holy Mother of God, who didst conceive God in thy virginal womb and didst bear Him: deliver from all eternal condemnation those who hymn thee.[^8719]

*Both now...* Knowing thee to be the Mother of God, O Pure One, and our defense, we set thee forward as the mediatress of our salvation unto the compassionate Lord.[^8720]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* I cried aloud unto Thee, O Lord, when I was in affliction, and Thou didst lead up my life, O Abundant in mercy [cf. Jonah 2:3, 7].[^8721]

*Troparion 1:* Pray Thy Son, O Sovereign Lady, Who was lifted up upon the Cross and delivered men from corruption, that we may be saved.[^8722]

*Troparion 2:* O most pure Sovereign Lady, forsake me not who hope in thee, but make haste with thy help.[^8723]

*Glory...* Deliver from sorrow those who call upon thy divine name, and liberate them from fearful torment.[^8724]

*Both now...* O most pure Sovereign Lady, firm hope: preserve those who with love hymn thee as the true Mother of God.[^8725]

*Then: Lord, have mercy (40 times). Glory... Both now...*[^8726]

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 7:*[^8727] Unceasingly pray Christ God, Who was crucified for our sake and cast down the dominion of death, O Virgin Mother of God, that He may save our souls.[^8728]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* The children showed the burning furnace of old to be pouring forth dew, as they hymned the one God and said: Exalted and most glorious is the God of our fathers [cf. Dan. 3:50].[^8729]

*Troparion 1:* In perplexity, and sorrows, and tribulations, O Most-pure One, I call upon thee fervently from my heart: come quickly and deliver me, and grant me consolation, O Pure One.[^8730]

*Troparion 2:* The surges of many transgressions and passions tempest me, and bring me down unto the depth of perdition: give me thy hand, O Mother of God, and save me, and have mercy on me by thy power.[^8731]

*Glory...* I bring Thy Mother unto Thee in prayer, O Savior, Lover of mankind: accepting her, deliver me from transgressions and the coming judgment, and snatch me from eternal torment.[^8732]

*Both now...* Having acquired a soul deadened by grievous transgressions, I pray thee, O Sovereign Lady, who didst put hades to death by thy childbearing: quicken me by patterns of repentance.[^8733]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* Christ, Who is truly God, and the Cause of the nature of all things, we highly exalt, and we glorify Him unto all ages.[^8734]

*Troparion 1:* Save me, O Most-pure One, from tribulations and corruption and passions; for unto thee do I flee, my hope and divine salvation.[^8735]

*Troparion 2:* In the final hour, taking me away from cruel calumniators, O Sovereign Lady Virgin, do thou thyself guide me unto the divine dwelling.[^8736]

*Glory...* Arise, O my soul! falling down before the Mother of God cry aloud: O hope and salvation of all, deliver me from the eternal fire.[^8737]

*Both now...* Let us sing of the Virgin, alone blessed among women, highly exalting Christ and glorifying Him unto all ages [cf. Luke 1:28].[^8738]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* Mother of God and Virgin, who didst bear and yet remain a Virgin again: this is not a work of nature, but of God's condescension; therefore, as the only one accounted worthy of God's marvelous wonders, thee do we ever magnify.[^8739]

*Troparion 1:* The Mother of God brings supplication unto Thee for me, O Master Christ: receiving her, deliver me from every torment.[^8740]

*Troparion 2:* I ever pray thee, O Sovereign Lady: deliver my humble soul from the coming judgment and torment.[^8741]

*Glory...* Pray my Creator and Maker, O Virgin, to deliver me from the fearful judgment, and to grant me life.[^8742]

*Both now...* O all-immaculate and pure Mother and Virgin: deliver from defilements thy flock which magnifies thee.[^8743]

*It is truly meet: Trisagion. After Our Father: Troparia and the rest.*[^8744]

*On Wednesday at Matins.*[^8745]
*After the 1st Stichologia, Sessional Hymns of the Cross, Tone 7.*[^8746]

*Sessional Hymn 1:* The Church cries aloud unto Thee, O Christ God, worshiping Thee in the pine, the cedar, and the cypress: grant victories unto our Emperor [Name] for the sake of the Mother of God, and have mercy on us [cf. Isa. 60:13].[^8747]

*Verse: Exalt ye the Lord our God, and worship the footstool of His feet; for He is holy [LXX Ps. 98:5].*[^8748]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* O Thou Who for my sake didst endure nailing upon the Cross: accept my vigilant praise, O Christ God, and save me.[^8749]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:* Pray Christ God, Who was crucified for our sake and cast down the dominion of death, O Virgin Mother of God, that He may save our souls.[^8750]

*After the 2nd Stichologia, Sessional Hymns of the Cross, Tone 7.*[^8751]

*Sessional Hymn 1:* The tree brighter than fire and more effectual than flame...[^8752]

...of Thy Cross hast Thou shown forth, O Christ, consuming the sins of men and enlightening the hearts of those who hymn Thy voluntary crucifixion: O Christ God, glory to Thee![^8753]

*Verse: But God is our King before the ages; He hath wrought salvation in the midst of the earth [LXX Ps. 73:12].*[^8754]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* O Thou Who rulest over the bodiless hosts, and knowest the slothfulness of my soul: by Thy Cross save me, O Christ God, as Thou art the Lover of mankind.[^8755]

*Verse: Marvelous is God in His saints, the God of Israel [LXX Ps. 67:36].*[^8756]

*For the Martyrs:* Rejoice, ye righteous! Let the heavenly things be glad! For the Martyrs, having contended upon earth, trampled down the enemy. Let the triumphant Church leap for joy, keeping festival unto the only President of the contest and Author of victory, Christ God, Who grants great mercy unto the world.[^8757]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:* When the Virgin beheld Thee crucified, O Lord, weeping she cried aloud unto Thee: I hymn Thine ineffable long-suffering, O my Son, and Thy supreme and divine condescension unto men.[^8758]

*After the 3rd Stichologia, Sessional Hymns, Tone 7.*[^8759]

*Sessional Hymn 1:* When Thou wast crucified, O Master Christ, the enemy was bound, and death was put to death; and the souls held fast in hades in the nether regions were loosed from their bonds.[^8760]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* O Thou Who for our sake didst accept voluntary crucifixion: crush the enemies that know Thee not as the true God, and save us.[^8761]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:* Ever guarded by the Cross of thy Son, O Virgin, we escape the assaults of demons; therefore, as is meet, hymning thee we glorify thee, O all-hymned Mother of God.[^8762]

*Canon to the Honorable and Life-Giving Cross of the Lord. (Whose acrostic is: The planted Cross hath uprooted deception.) By Lord Joseph.*[^8763]
*Tone 7. Ode 1. Heirmos:* At Thy nod the watery nature, before easily poured forth, was changed into earthly form, O Lord; therefore Israel, having journeyed on foot without wetting his feet, sings unto Thee a song of victory [cf. Exod. 14:21–22].[^8764]

*Refrain: Glory to Thee, O Lord, to Thy Cross and crucifixion.*[^8765]

*Troparion 1:* Bearing reproach, the Master endures crucifixion, taking away the reproaches of men as the Lover of mankind; He is pierced in the ribs with a spear, thereby slaying the warring enemy [cf. John 19:34].[^8766]

*Troparion 2:* Having bent Thy Cross like a bow, O Merciful One, with the arrows of...[^8767]

...the nails Thou didst wound the adversary, O Savior, and didst heal men who of old were wounded by him.[^8768]

*For the Martyrs:* By the blood of the Saints the abominable defilements of blood offered unto idols ceased; and the whole earth was sanctified, ever calling them blessed with praises.[^8769]

*For the Martyrs:* The heavenly hosts sang praise, beholding earthly men in the body contending with the bodiless; therefore the President of the contest crowned them with crowns as victors.[^8770]

*Theotokion:* The handwriting of Adam was torn up, didst thou cry aloud, O most pure Sovereign Lady, when Thou, O Son, wast pierced with the spear; therefore I hymn Thy suffering, which hath poured forth dispassion unto all, O Master [cf. Col. 2:14; John 19:34].[^8771]

*Another Canon, to the Most Holy Mother of God. By Joseph.*[^8772]
*Tone 7. The same Heirmos.*[^8773]

*Troparion 1:* Preserve thy people who hymn thy greatness with love, O Mother of God, and deliver them from harm; for thou art the protectress, pure guide, and establishment of all.[^8774]

*Troparion 2:* O Virgin who didst bear the Water of immortality: grant us waters of healing, washing away all death-bearing passions of our souls and bodies.[^8775]

*Troparion 3:* An honorable dwelling unto the Master wast thou revealed, O woman full of the grace of God, of Him Who honored the nature of our forefather; therefore we pray thee, O Pure One: deliver us from all the dishonor of passions.[^8776]

*Troparion 4:* Having borne the Sun of righteousness unto the world, drive away the darkness from those who hymn thee with faith, O Maiden, in this thy radiant and holy temple, O All-immaculate One [cf. Mal. 4:2].[^8777]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* O Lord and Savior, Who in the beginning didst establish the heavens by Thy powerful Word, and all their host by the all-effecting and divine Spirit: establish me upon the immovable rock of Thy confession [cf. LXX Ps. 32:6].[^8778]

*Troparion 1:* O Thou Who didst stretch out the heaven like a curtain: Thou didst spread out Thy hands upon the tree, O Jesus, healing the intemperance of Adam's hand in sin, as Thou art merciful, and snatching all from the hand of the deceitful warrior [cf. LXX Ps. 103:2].[^8779]

*Troparion 2:* With thorns do the lawless people crown Thee, O King, Who hast crowned men with glory...[^8780]

...and rooted out the thorns of Adam's disobedience, O Christ, and planted for all the sprouting forth of the knowledge of God [cf. Matt. 27:29; Gen. 3:18].[^8781]

*For the Martyrs:* Being mortal by nature, ye chose to meditate therein on immortality, O all-wise Martyrs; therefore wounds and afflictions, persecutions, beatings, and the severing of limbs did ye all endure with rejoicing.[^8782]

*For the Martyrs:* With steadfast resolve, all ye Sufferers humbled the deceitful enemy who threatened to destroy all things, having humbled yourselves in Christ; and ye were exalted by divine power, having armed yourselves against him.[^8783]

*Theotokion:* Beholding the Lamb Who came willingly unto His passion to be slain, the Mother and Ewe-lamb shed fountains of tears, saying: What is this, O sacred Child? How art Thou put to death, providing to quicken all the dead?[^8784]

*Canon to the Mother of God. The same Heirmos.*[^8785]

*Troparion 1:* Grant unto thy servants, O Pure One, saving petitions in this thy divine temple, wherein thou hast shown forth a fount of wonders, ever pouring forth by divine visitation, and deliver them from eternal torment.[^8786]

*Troparion 2:* O Pure One, who beyond cause and word didst bear the Abyss of mercies, Christ, the Giver of all good things: by thy divine overshadowing thou truly showest this thy divine temple to be a cleansing of all manner of passions.[^8787]

*Troparion 3:* Blessed is the fruit of thy womb, O Virgin Mother of God, joy of all: for thou hast truly borne joy and gladness unto the whole world, the Lover of mankind, having dispersed the sorrow of sin, O Bride of God [cf. Luke 1:42].[^8788]

*Troparion 4:* O noetic Ark, who truly didst bear the incorruptible Manna unto the world: come into the midst of us in this hour, O Virgin, and change us from all defilement, who purely call thee blessed [cf. Heb. 9:4].[^8789]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Leaving not the Father's bosom, and descending unto earth, O Christ God: I have heard the mystery of Thy dispensation, and I glorified Thee, O only Lover of mankind.[^8790]

*Troparion 1:* Having appeared as man upon earth, Thou didst make men heavenly; for being lifted up upon the tree, O Master, Thou didst raise up together with Thyself all who hymn Thy sufferings [cf. John 12:32].[^8791]

*Troparion 2:* For mortals Thou diest, O Life; for the unrighteous, O my righteous Jesus, Thou endurest a reproachful passion. We hymn, O Long-suffering One, Thy boundless tender compassion [cf. 1 Pet. 3:18].[^8792]

*For the Martyrs:* The onrushes of wild beasts, fiery burnings, severing of hands and feet, tearing out of limbs, and every other torment did ye endure, O Martyrs, which mediated divine delight.[^8793]

*For the Martyrs:* From the ends of the flesh, O Sufferers, crying unto Christ the God of all, ye were heard; and ye were lifted up upon the rock of divine consolation.[^8794]

*Theotokion:* Make supplication for us unto Him Who was incarnate of thee, O Mother of God, that we who glorify His passions may find help in time of affliction.[^8795]

*Canon to the Mother of God. The same Heirmos.*[^8796]

*Troparion 1:* Thee Habakkuk proclaimed of old as the mountain overshadowed by virtues, O most pure Mother of God, which overshadows all, O Sovereign Lady, from the burning heat of the malice of the primal warrior [cf. Hab. 3:3].[^8797]

*Troparion 2:* Offer supplication unto the Lord Who was incarnate of thy most pure blood, O Sovereign Lady: to save thy people from all harm, who bless thee as is meet.[^8798]

*Troparion 3:* This thy most glorious church full of God's grace hath come to be for the healing of souls and bodies; for it stills every disease, and cleanses from passions those who faithfully bless thee.[^8799]

*Troparion 4:* By thy fervent and honorable visitation, O Virgin, save all who with love flee unto thy defense, O Sovereign Lady: visit them and grant petitions that lead unto life.[^8800]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* O Lord my God, rising early from the night unto Thee I pray: grant me forgiveness of my transgressions, and direct my paths unto the light of Thy commandments, I pray [cf. Isa. 26:9].[^8801]

*Troparion 1:* Thou didst accept crucifixion for the taking away of evils, O Word of God; and Thou didst taste of gall, destroying the bitter harm of the sweet food of old: Glory to Thy great tender compassion! [cf. Matt. 27:34; Gen. 3:6].[^8802]

*Troparion 2:* O Thou Who by Thy lifting up upon the Cross didst shake the whole earth...[^8803]

...by Thy divine power, and Thou healest their afflictions, O Master; and dost establish wavering hearts in knowledge of Thee.[^8804]

*For the Martyrs:* Beliar spread malignant snares on every side, yet he ensnared not Christ's Martyrs; for having received fiery wings, they reached the divine abodes.[^8805]

*For the Martyrs:* Being deified by the unfailing nod of God, O Passion-bearers, ye feared not at all painful torments; as if another were suffering, so did ye yourselves abide, O all-wise ones.[^8806]

*Theotokion:* With what eyes shall I gaze upon the Eye that dries up all abysses, O my Son, extinguished as Thou hangest upon the Cross? the pure Virgin cried aloud, troubling herself with lamentations.[^8807]

*Canon to the Mother of God. Ode 5. Heirmos:* Night without light is unto the unbelieving, O Christ, but enlightenment unto the faithful in the sweetness of Thy words; therefore early in the morning do I rise unto Thee, and hymn Thy Godhead [cf. Isa. 26:9].[^8808]

*Troparion 1:* Making watchful those weighed down by the sleep of sin, O Pure One, yet in thy most honorable temple, through thy watchful and divine prayer, have tender mercy upon us, O Bride of God.[^8809]

*Troparion 2:* Grant unto all us who flee unto thee a helping hand, O Pure One, and wash away the defilement of every malice, and cleanse diseases by thy prayers.[^8810]

*Troparion 3:* O most pure Mother of God, who didst conceive God in thy virginal womb and bear Him: deliver from all eternal condemnation those who hymn thee.[^8811]

*Troparion 4:* The souls of those coming with faith into thy temple are renewed, which were waxed old through sins; and all glorify thee as is meet, O All-immaculate One.[^8812]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Sailing in the surge of worldly cares, overwhelmed with the ship by sins, and cast forth unto the soul-corrupting beast, like Jonah I cry unto Thee, O Christ: Bring me up from the death-bearing abyss! [cf. Jonah 2:3–7].[^8813]

*Troparion 1:* Being the only Redeemer, Thou didst give Thy saving blood as a ransom, and didst redeem us who were captive, O All-good One, and brought us unto Thy Father, having slain the tormentor by the Cross, O Christ [cf. 1 Tim. 2:6].[^8814]

*Troparion 2:* By intemperance of old I brought about a grievous fall; but Christ...[^8815]

...being lifted up upon the tree, spreading out His hands, raised up me that was fallen; and having willingly been wounded, manifestly healed all my wounds [cf. Isa. 53:5].[^8816]

*For the Martyrs:* By no means slumbering with the slumber of delusion, ye put to sleep every onslaught of the tormentors, O Martyrs; and having fallen asleep in the slumber meet for the righteous, O blessed ones, ye became watchful intercessors for all.[^8817]

*For the Martyrs:* Established upon the rock of Christ's divine commandments, ye remained immovable against the wiles of the enemy, O all-glorious Martyrs; and having trampled him underfoot in godly wisdom, ye journeyed unto God in a manner worthy of God.[^8818]

*Theotokion:* A young Child thou bearest, O Mother of God, Who before the ages was timelessly co-understood with the Father, Who by the Cross renews mankind, which was waxed old through sins by the counsel of the author of evil, O All-immaculate One [cf. Isa. 9:6].[^8819]

*Canon to the Mother of God. Ode 6. Heirmos:* Jonah cried out of the belly of Hades: Bring up my life from corruption; and we cry unto Thee: O all-powerful Savior, have mercy on us! [cf. Jonah 2:2, 6].[^8820]

*Troparion 1:* Thou alone didst pour forth for us the water of salvation; thou alone, O Pure One, having dried up the burning heat of delusion, dost bedew the true understanding of thy servants.[^8821]

*Troparion 2:* O animate city of our God: deliver thy flock from godless barbarians, and from earthquake, and constraint, and every temptation [cf. LXX Ps. 86:3].[^8822]

*Troparion 3:* Drive away the wounds of our souls, O Mother of God, and bodily infirmities, by the sprinkling of thy prayers, that with divine voices we may hymn thee.[^8823]

*Troparion 4:* O Virgin Maiden, who through thy most pure childbearing didst loose Eve from sorrows: loose the sorrowful passions of my soul and body [cf. Gen. 3:16].[^8824]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* Of old the Children showed forth the fiery furnace as dropping dew, hymning the one God and saying: Exalted above all is the God of our fathers, and supremely glorified [cf. Dan. 3:52].[^8825]

*Troparion 1:* Our forefather found a life free of pain, when Thou willingly didst suffer pain upon the Cross, and wast dead, and didst put the serpent to death, O Jesus Christ, abundant in mercy.[^8826]

*Troparion 2:* We all have been delivered from the curse of the law; for the Lawgiver was lifted up upon the Cross, pouring forth an ever-flowing blessing, and grace, and mercy, and the destruction of corruption [cf. Gal. 3:13].[^8827]

*For the Martyrs:* Approaching torments with voluntary impulse, the Martyrs imaged Him Who suffered willingly; and having been crowned by Him, they now dance with the Angels.[^8828]

*For the Martyrs:* Having surrendered your bodies unto various torments, O all-praised Martyrs, by the shedding of your blood ye drowned the bodiless enemies, and ye pour forth fountains of healings.[^8829]

*Theotokion:* How shall I not weep, O sweetest Child, beholding Thee upon the Cross? How shall I not wail, seeing Thee suffering unjustly, O most righteous Judge? the Virgin Mother spoke.[^8830]

*Canon to the Mother of God. The same Heirmos.*[^8831]

*Troparion 1:* O Virgin who didst bear the divine Fire: consume the brushwood passions of those who hymn thee, O All-praised One; and illuminate all with the light of repentance, we pray.[^8832]

*Troparion 2:* O incorrupt Maiden, deliver us from corruption; O animate palace of God, show us forth to be temples of the Divine Spirit, who in thy church unceasingly hymn thee with faith [cf. 1 Cor. 6:19].[^8833]

*Troparion 3:* Thou pourest forth a stream of healings unto all; for thou didst bear the Fount of life: and by thy fervent prayer, O Mother of God, cleanse this thy flock from defilement, O Bride of God.[^8834]

*Troparion 4:* Having seen all the images of thy divine childbearing, the divine Prophets sang with joy, crying aloud: Blessed art Thou, O supremely praised God of our fathers! [cf. Dan. 3:52].[^8835]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* The bush on Sinai that communed with the fire without being burned revealed God unto Moses slow of speech and halting of tongue; and the zeal of God showed the three Children in the fire to be unconquerable singers: All ye works of the Lord, hymn the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages! [cf. Exod. 3:2; Dan. 3:57].[^8836]

*Troparion 1:* The blood and water that flowed from Thy side, O Savior, renewed the whole world and poured forth incorruption; and the abominable sacrifices and shedding of defiled blood ceased. Therefore all ye works hymn Thee as Lord, and supremely exalt Thee unto all ages! [cf. John 19:34].[^8837]

*Troparion 2:* With a crown of thorns Thou wast encircled, O Savior, and didst taste of vinegar and gall...[^8838]

...and wast spat upon, and beaten, and lifted up upon the Cross, receiving nails; whereby being saved, I cry unto Thee: All ye works of the Lord, hymn the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages! [cf. Matt. 27:30, 34].[^8839]

*For the Martyrs:* Having been most radiantly enriched from the inexhaustible treasures of the truth, O Sufferers, ye spat upon all idolatrous poverty and were shown forth as enrichers of the poor, crying aloud: All ye works of the Lord, hymn the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages![^8840]

*For the Martyrs:* Having disregarded the ordinances of the impious, like the Children, O Martyrs, ye were cast into burning fire, wherein ye found the dew of God cooling you, crying aloud: All ye works of the Lord, hymn the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages! [cf. Dan. 3:21, 50].[^8841]

*Theotokion:* The legal ark of old prefigured thee, O Pure One, bearing within not the tablets, but Christ the Lawgiver, Whom the lawless people nailed unto the Cross, Who saves us that cry aloud: All ye works of the Lord, hymn the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages! [cf. Heb. 9:4].[^8842]

*Canon to the Mother of God. Ode 8. Heirmos:* The only unoriginate King of glory, Whom the heavenly hosts bless, and the ranks of Angels tremble before: praise Him, ye priests, and supremely exalt Him, ye people, unto the ages! [cf. Dan. 3:57].[^8843]

*Troparion 1:* O thou who pourest forth an abyss of wonders unto the world, All-praised One: dry up the stream of our passions, and pour forth for us the dew of forgiveness, who honor thee with untroubled faith, O Virgin.[^8844]

*Troparion 2:* From thee the righteous Sun shone forth upon those sitting in the darkness of passions, and made thee light, O all-praised and pure Maiden! Therefore we hymn thee as is meet, O Mother of God [cf. Mal. 4:2; Luke 1:79].[^8845]

*Troparion 3:* Having become the all-radiant church of God, O Pure One: show forth thy servants who assemble in thy holy temple to be dwellings of the Spirit, who glorify thee unto all ages [cf. 1 Cor. 3:16].[^8846]
