*Troparion 4:* By the light of thy childbearing thou didst marvelously enlighten the universe, O Mother of God, having borne in thine arms the true God, Who ever enlightens those who call out with faith: All ye works of the Lord, hymn the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto the ages![^8847]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* Mother of God, and Virgin who didst bear and again remain a virgin, not a work of nature...[^8848]

...was this, but the condescension of God; therefore as the only one accounted worthy of God's wonders, we ever magnify Thee.[^8849]

*Troparion 1:* The enemy's power utterly failed, when Thou didst cry out mightily upon the tree unto Thy supremely unoriginate Father, O Mighty One, and didst gather Thy scattered sheep unto knowledge of Thee, O Master Christ [cf. Matt. 27:46, 50; John 11:52].[^8850]

*Troparion 2:* As a Lamb Thou wast lifted up willingly upon the Cross, O Master, snatching Thy noetic sheep from the wolf, O Savior; and enclosing them in the fold of Thy commandments, who hymn Thee [cf. John 1:29; 10:11–12].[^8851]

*For the Martyrs:* Manifested as imitators of Thy sufferings, the gloriously renowned passion-bearers endured manifold kinds of torments, O Lover of mankind; whence they obtained crowns of glory and were accounted worthy of Thy kingdom, O Christ.[^8852]

*For the Martyrs:* Solar graces flash forth, enlightening the faithful: the praiseworthy festival of the honorable passion-bearers; wherefrom we who ever celebrate it are delivered from the darkness of grievous transgressions.[^8853]

*Theotokion:* Thy Son shone forth as noetic Light upon the Cross, O Most-pure One, and reproved the rulers of darkness, O All-immaculate One: He darkened the sun's light and enlightened the assembly of the faithful, O Pure Maiden [cf. Eph. 6:12; Luke 23:44–45].[^8854]

*Canon to the Mother of God. Ode 9. Heirmos:* O Mother who knewest not man, Virgin Mother of God, who didst bear without experience of corruption and lend flesh unto the all-skillful Word: receptacle of the Unbearable One, dwelling-place of the Uncontainable One, thy Creator: thee do we magnify.[^8855]

*Troparion 1:* More sacred than the hosts on high wast thou revealed, having brought forth the Creator of all, O Mother unwedded, holy Mother of God; therefore with the Angels we glorify thee, unceasingly hymning thy great deeds [cf. Luke 1:49].[^8856]

*Troparion 2:* As one enlightened by the beams of the Divine Spirit, the most sacred Prophet of old beheld thee, O Virgin, as the overshadowed mountain, O Mother of God; therefore we of the earth with the Angels call thee blessed, as thou didst foretell, O God-blessed one [cf. Hab. 3:3; Luke 1:48].[^8857]

*Troparion 3:* A jar wast thou shown to be, bearing the divine Manna; a ladder wast thou seen, leading men up from earth, O only unwedded Virgin Mother of God [cf. Heb. 9:4; Gen. 28:12]:...[^8858]

...therefore we the faithful, having come together, glorify thee as is meet, O woman full of the grace of God.[^8859]

*Troparion 4:* O Maiden who becamest the dwelling-place of the Godhead: me, who have become a den of soul-corrupting thieves and a workshop of all lawlessness, wash clean with the bath of tears, and show forth as a dwelling-place of the Divine Spirit [cf. Matt. 21:13; 1 Cor. 6:19].[^8860]

*Troparion 5:* O thou who alone didst bear the pre-eternal Light: deliver me from eternal darkness, O Sovereign Lady, and enlighten my soul, O Virgin, which is wholly darkened by the passions of this life, that with love I may ever glorify thee.[^8861]

*It is truly meet: Litany, Photogogicon, and the customary Psalms.*[^8862]

*At the Aposticha, Stichera of the Cross, Tone 7.*[^8863]

*Sticheron 1:* Being Master, Lover of mankind, and Giver of life, Thou didst redeem the universe by Thy Cross: O Lord, glory to Thee![^8864]

*Verse: We were filled in the morning with Thy mercy, O Lord, and we rejoiced: - - - And look upon Thy servants, and upon Thy works, and guide their sons [LXX Ps. 89:14, 16].*[^8865]

*Sticheron 2:* The true Vine was nailed upon the Cross, and the nations embraced paradise with the thief. This is the glory of the Church; this is the wealth of the kingdom: O Lord Who didst suffer for our sake, glory to Thee! [cf. John 15:1; Luke 23:43].[^8866]

*Verse: And let the brightness of the Lord our God be upon us: and direct Thou the works of our hands over us, yea, the work of our hands direct Thou [LXX Ps. 89:17].*[^8867]

*For the Martyrs:* Luminaries were ye revealed unto the universe, O all-praised passion-bearers, crying unto Christ: O Lord, glory to Thee![^8868]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:* When the All-immaculate One beheld Thee willingly nailed upon the tree, weeping she sang praise unto Thy dominion.[^8869]

*It is good to give praise: Trisagion. After Our Father: Troparia, Litany, and the rest.*[^8870]

*On Wednesday, the Beatitudes, Tone 7.*[^8871]

*Troparion 1:* Comely was the fruit and good for food that put me to death; but Christ is the tree of life, whereof eating I die not, but cry aloud with the thief: Remember me, O Lord, in Thy kingdom [cf. Gen. 3:6; Luke 23:42].[^8872]

*Troparion 2:* Thou didst spread out Thy palms upon the Cross, rectifying the stumbling of Adam's outstretched hands toward the tree of food, O Compassionate One, by reason of Thy manifold goodness; therefore we glorify Thee, O Lord Benefactor.[^8873]

*Troparion 3:* On the Place of the Skull the Jewish assembly, O Christ King, cru-...[^8874]

...cified Thee Who crushest the destructive head of the deceitful one, and pourest forth for us from Thy holy ribs rivers of forgiveness [cf. John 19:34; Gen. 3:15].[^8875]

*For the Martyrs:* Having been made conformable unto Christ's honorable and saving passions, O all-praised Martyrs, ye endured manifold torments and passed together unto immortality; therefore ye are called blessed.[^8876]

*Glory... Trinity:* Preserve Thy servants who hymn Thee, O Holy Trinity; strengthen them by the power of the Cross, and direct us to walk toward the city on high, where having arrived we may find mercy [cf. Heb. 11:10].[^8877]

*Both now... Theotokion:* Seeing Him Who was born of thee being crucified, shedding tears, troubled in her womb, the pure Mother cried aloud: How dost Thou suffer these things willingly, O Son, desiring to deliver mankind from passions?[^8878]

## Thursday: The Holy Apostles and Saint Nicholas[^8879]

*On Wednesday Evening.*[^8880]
*On "Lord, I have cried," Stichera to the Holy Apostles, Tone 7.*[^8881]
*Tone 7, Podoben: "No longer are we forbidden" (Не ктомꙋ̀ возбраня́еми є҆смы̀):*[^8882]

*Sticheron 1:* Having cultivated the whole earth with the plow of the knowledge of God, O glorious Apostles, ye raised up a multitude of the faithful.[^8883]

*Sticheron 2:* Dispel the winter of my passions, and make the calm of pure pouring forth to dawn upon me, O blessed Apostles.[^8884]

*Sticheron 3:* As disciples of the Word, ye brought the assemblies of the nations from senselessness unto the understanding of God.[^8885]

*Other Stichera to the Holy Wonderworker Nicholas, sung if there is no Menaion.*[^8886]
*Tone and Podoben the same.*[^8887]

*Sticheron 1:* As of old thou didst deliver the generals that were about to die, O Father, through thy prayers, O Nicholas: so now also save us.[^8888]

*Sticheron 2:* Deliver from sorrows all those who call upon thee and hymn thee with faith and love, O all-blessed hierarch Nicholas.[^8889]

*Sticheron 3:* Deliver thy servants from famine and destruction, from earthquake and afflictions, and from every constraint, O all-wise hierarch Nicholas.[^8890]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Offer the supplication of thy servants unto thy Son, O All-pure One, that He may save all whom He created.[^8891]

*O Gladsome Light: Prokeimenon of the day.*[^8892]

*At the Aposticha, Stichera to the Apostles, Tone 7.*[^8893]

*Sticheron 1:* Glorious Apostles, pillars of the Church, heralds of truth, radiant luminaries: by spiritual fire ye burned up all delusion, and enlightened mankind by faith. Therefore we pray you: entreat our Savior and God to give peace unto the world, and to save our souls [cf. Gal. 2:9].[^8894]

*Verse: Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes, O Thou that dwellest in heaven: - - - So our eyes look unto the Lord our God, until He have mercy on us [LXX Ps. 122:1–2].*[^8895]

*Sticheron 2:* Apostles of Christ, laborers of the Savior, carrying the Cross upon your shoulders like a plow: having cleansed the earth that was overcome by the delusion of idols, ye sowed the word of faith. Ye are worthily honored, O holy Apostles of Christ.[^8896]

*Verse: Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us: - - - For our soul hath been exceedingly filled with the reproach of them that are at ease, and with the contempt of the proud [LXX Ps. 122:3–4].*[^8897]

*For the Martyrs:* O all-praised Martyrs, spiritual sheep, rational whole-burnt offerings, acceptable sacrifices, well-pleasing unto God: neither did the earth hide you, but heaven received you; ye became partakers with the Angels. Together with them entreat—we pray you—our Savior and God to grant peace unto the world, and to save our souls [cf. Rom. 12:1].[^8898]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Let us hymn the Virgin who alone is most pure after childbearing, as Mother of God the Word, saying: Glory to thee![^8899]

*Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart: Trisagion. After Our Father: Troparion.*[^8900]

*On Wednesday at Small Compline.*[^8901]
*Canon to the Most Holy Mother of God, Tone 7.*[^8902]
*Ode 1. Heirmos:* By Thy nod the water's nature, before easily molded, was transformed into an earthy form, O Lord; therefore Israel, having journeyed on foot dryshod, sings unto Thee a song of victory [cf. Exod. 14:21–22, 29].[^8903]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Mother of God, save us.*[^8904]

*Troparion 1:* With living waters, O Virgin, refresh my soul that is wasted by the burning heat of sin, that I may bring forth a fruitful ear of divine compunction, O Pure One [cf. John 4:10].[^8905]

*Troparion 2:* Rain down upon me drops of compunction, O Pure One, with drops of tender mercies, wherewith I may wash away, O Mai-...[^8906]

...den, all the defilement of the soul that hath come upon it through heedlessness.[^8907]

*Glory:* Having received thy Creator in thy womb, thou didst bear Him without corruption, an infant Who was God; a strange wonder! thou art handmaid and Mother, O unwedded Sovereign Lady [cf. Luke 1:38, 43].[^8908]

*Both now:* Thou didst bear for us the Redeemer of mankind, both Deliverer and Savior, Christ, O most pure Virgin Mother; therefore manifestly knowing thee to be the mediatress of eternal life, we glorify thee.[^8909]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* O Lord and Savior, Who in the beginning didst establish the heavens by Thy powerful Word, and all their host by the all-effecting and divine Spirit: establish me upon the immovable rock of Thy confession [cf. LXX Ps. 32:6].[^8910]

*Troparion 1:* Having dyed the bodily purple from thy most pure blood, the King of all, and having worn it without corruption by nature, being both God and man, came forth from thee, O Virgin, possessing a kingdom immutable.[^8911]

*Troparion 2:* As a treasury of good and ineffable things given unto all from God, O Virgin, thou impartest unto us the ungrudging grace of marvelous gifts in temptations, afflictions, and infirmities, who pray unto thee.[^8912]

*Glory:* The marvelous Daniel foresaw thee as the mountain, O Pure One, wherefrom was cut the stone, Christ, Who crushed the demonic idols; therefore we hymn thee as the cause of all good things, being redeemed by thy childbearing [cf. Dan. 2:34, 45].[^8913]

*Both now:* The God-proclaiming one of old foresaw thee as the scroll wherein the Word was written, O Pure One, by the Father's finger; therefore I pray thee: tear up the evil record of my sins, and write me in the book of life [cf. Ezek. 2:9–10; Rev. 20:12].[^8914]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Leaving not the Father's bosom, and descending unto earth, O Christ God: I have heard the mystery of Thy dispensation, and I glorified Thee, O only Lover of mankind.[^8915]

*Troparion 1:* Offer supplication unto Him Who was born of thee, O Mother of God, that we who glorify His sufferings may find help in time of circumstance.[^8916]

*Troparion 2:* The Creator of nature, having been poured forth as Myrrh from thee, O all-holy and pure Maiden, filled all things with fragrance by divine understandings, and drove away the foul-smelling delusion [cf. Song 1:3].[^8917]

*Glory:* Christ God, having set thee as a noetic ladder for those upon earth, O Pure One, by thee leads up the faithful unto divine heavenly deeds, as He is merciful [cf. Gen. 28:12].[^8918]

*Both now:* Weighed down with many a burden of sins, O Good One, I cry unto thee: Appear as cleansing unto me, O thou who didst bear God Who takes away the sins of the world [cf. John 1:29].[^8919]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* O Lord my God, rising early from the night unto Thee I pray: grant me forgiveness of my transgressions, and direct my paths unto the light of Thy commandments, I pray [cf. Isa. 26:9].[^8920]

*Troparion 1:* O Mother of the Lover of mankind and God, all-hymned Mother of God: unto me who ever praise thee with faith and love in hymns, send down by thy supplications cleansing of my transgressions.[^8921]

*Troparion 2:* O most pure Mother of God, who art the receptacle of the immutable Light and the temple of the divine radiance: enlighten my darkened soul with thy light, I pray.[^8922]

*Glory:* O Lord my God, Who wast pleased to become man, I pray Thee: be pleased to save me, the prodigal, for the sake of the Virgin who purely bare Thee, and the pure Maiden.[^8923]

*Both now:* O Sovereign Lady Mother of God: grant forgiveness of my transgressions, O all-immaculate one who didst bear the hypostatic Life unto the world.[^8924]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Sailing in the surge of worldly cares, overwhelmed with the ship by sins, and cast forth unto the soul-corrupting beast, like Jonah I cry unto Thee, O Christ: Bring me up from the death-bearing abyss! [cf. Jonah 2:3–7].[^8925]

*Troparion 1:* From the corruption of death and from the sin that was of Adam were we delivered, O Mother of God, by thy seedless childbearing; for God and man together, one and the same, being the Son, bestows upon the faithful redemption of sins [cf. Rom. 5:12–19].[^8926]

*Troparion 2:* Tear up the handwriting of my transgressions, O Sovereign Lady, having written me in the book of the saved by thy divine prayers, that in praise I may hymn thee who art holier than the Cherubim [cf. Col. 2:14; Rev. 20:12].[^8927]

*Glory:* Them that of old were cast away from Thy presence by Thy righteous judgment, O Christ, Thou didst call back through Thy bodily coming, as...[^8928]

...compassionate; and now, O Good One, receive us who have sinned, and change us from evils.[^8929]

*Both now:* O most pure Virgin, who didst bear the Cleansing and the Benefactor of sinners, I pray thee: loose the guilt of my boundless transgressions by thy maternal prayer; for as a Mother unto the Son thou hast the power [cf. John 2:1–11].[^8930]

*Then, Lord, have mercy (40). Glory... Both now...*[^8931]

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 7:* Under thy protection all we earthborn flee: deliver our souls from the eternal fire, O Good One.[^8932]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* Of old the youths showed forth the fiery furnace to be dropping dew, praising the one God, and saying: O God of the fathers, supremely exalted and exceedingly glorious [cf. Dan. 3:49–50].[^8933]

*Troparion 1:* Raise up my mind, weighed down by sleep, O all-immaculate one, unto good deeds, that I may call thee blessed who art more honorable than all creation.[^8934]

*Troparion 2:* The three young children in the midst of the furnace prefigured thy childbearing; for it was not burned, O Pure One who didst bear the Fire; therefore burn up the material passions of my heart [cf. Dan. 3:49–50].[^8935]

*Glory:* Having perceived thee, beautiful and good among women, the most comely Lord was incarnate of thee; Him therefore implore, O most holy Maiden, to save me [cf. Song 1:8, 15].[^8936]

*Both now:* The divine dew, having gone forth from thee, O all-immaculate one, bedews them that are languishing in the burning heat of sin; therefore I pray thee: bedew my languishing soul.[^8937]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* The bush that held communion with the fire on Sinai without being consumed made God known unto Moses, slow of tongue and of an impediment in his speech; and the zeal of God showed forth the three children in the fire to be unconquerable singers: Praise the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages [cf. Exod. 3:2; 4:10; Dan. 3:57].[^8938]

*Troparion 1:* The bush on Sinai that communed with the fire without being burned showed forth thy strange childbearing unto Moses the God-seer; therefore, having marveled at thy strange childbearing, O Pure One, he cried aloud: Rejoice! Praise the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto the ages [cf. Exod. 3:2].[^8939]

*Troparion 2:* My mind that is mortified by sin, O pure Virgin who didst bear the Life, quicken...[^8940]

...and save me through thy maternal supplications unto God, who ever sing: Praise the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto the ages.[^8941]

*Glory:* Having thee, O Pure One, as an unceasing guardian by night and by day against noetic and visible enemies, I am in no wise afraid of any evil and deceitful assault, as I sing unto Christ: Praise the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^8942]

*Both now:* Being inclined by His love for mankind, O Mother of God, thy Son, as He is good, gave thee as help against enemies and as healing of passions unto them that call with faith: Praise the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^8943]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* Thee who without trial of corruption didst give birth, and didst lend flesh unto the Word, the all-craftsman, O Mother unwedded, Virgin Mother of God: receptacle of the Uncontainable, dwelling-place of thine uncontainable Creator, we magnify thee.[^8944]

*Troparion 1:* The unstolen wealth of thy virginity, O Mother in the flesh, thou wast revealed as the Mother of the Almighty, O unwedded Bride, Virgin Mother of God: having nurtured as an infant Him Who nurtures and sanctifies the whole world as Creator.[^8945]

*Troparion 2:* Having borne the most mankind-loving Word, O Virgin, Who takes away the sins of the world: Him implore to send down forgiveness of sins unto us who bless thee as is meet with undoubting faith [cf. John 1:29].[^8946]

*Glory:* Being a lover of sin, I tremble and fear lest the end of life suddenly overtake me, O pure Sovereign Lady, protectress of all the wronged: be thou now entreated to strengthen me in patterns of repentance.[^8947]

*Both now:* That Thou send me not into the fire prepared for me, the Virgin who bare Thee implores Thee, and the divine incorporeal Choir, the Apostles, Prophets, Martyrs, and Hierarchs, and the spirits of the righteous, O all-bountiful and most merciful One [cf. Matt. 25:41].[^8948]

*It is truly meet. Trisagion. After Our Father: the troparion and the rest according to the custom, and the dismissal.*[^8949]

*On Thursday at Matins.*[^8950]
*After the first Kathisma reading, Sessional Hymns of the Apostles, Tone 7:*[^8951]
*Sessional Hymn 1:* Thou didst show forth Thine Apostles, O Word, as laborers of Thy field, cutting...[^8952]

...down the idols; therefore having preached Thee as Master among the nations, they magnified Thee in godly manner.[^8953]

*Verse:* Their sound is gone out into all the earth, and their words unto the ends of the world [LXX Ps. 18:5].[^8954]
*Sessional Hymn 2:* Ye loved not earthly glory upon earth, O glorious ones; having proclaimed the heavenly God unto men, ye brought all unto Him.[^8955]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* The Fruit of thy womb, O most pure one, is the fulfillment of the Prophets and the Law; therefore, glorifying thee in knowledge as the Mother of God, we magnify thee in godly manner [cf. Matt. 5:17].[^8956]

*After the second Kathisma reading, Sessional Hymns, Tone 7:*[^8957]
*Sessional Hymn 1:* As the all-wise pilots of our salvation, let us duly honor with chants and hymns the Apostles of Christ God; for having driven away the ungodliness of delusion, they saved the world, having become eyewitnesses, servants, friends, and brethren of the Word [cf. Luke 1:2; John 15:14–15; 20:17].[^8958]

*Verse:* The heavens declare the glory of God, and the firmament proclaims the work of His hands [LXX Ps. 18:2].[^8959]
*Sessional Hymn 2:* The Prophets preached, the Apostles taught, the Martyrs confessed, and we have believed that Thou art Christ, the Son of God, the Redeemer of the world [cf. John 11:27].[^8960]

*Verse:* God is wonderful in His saints, the God of Israel [LXX Ps. 67:36].*[^8961]
*For the Martyrs:* Thy Martyrs, O Lord, overcame the enemy and put to shame the delusion of idols, having armed themselves with the power of Thy Cross; therefore together with the Angels singing praise unto Thee, they cry aloud the triumphal hymn, glorifying Thee, O Christ: by their prayers grant cleansing unto our souls, and great mercy.[^8962]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Rejoice, from whom the Word unchangeably became flesh and dwelt among us; rejoice, pure one, the rejoicing of the Apostles and Martyrs, and the salvation of us the faithful; rejoice, Mother of Christ God [cf. John 1:14].[^8963]

*After the third Kathisma reading, Sessional Hymns, Tone 7:*[^8964]
*Tone 7, Podoben: "Fruit of thy womb" (Пло́дъ чре́ва):*[^8965]
*Sessional Hymn 1:* All-praised disciples of the Lord, who, like radiant luminaries, have enlightened all that is under the sun: pray that I, who am in the darkness of passions and transgressions, may be enlightened.[^8966]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* Being the luminaries of the world, O Apostles of the Lord, ye ever cause the Word to shine upon the faithful, driving away exceedingly the mist of delusion; therefore ye also enlighten the nations through baptism, as...[^8967]

...preachers of the Trinity as ye are, ever revered.[^8968]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* O Virgin, the joy of the world, unceasingly implore thy Son together with the Apostles to grant forgiveness of our transgressions and amendment of life.[^8969]

*Canon to the holy, glorious, and all-praised Apostles. A creation of Theophanes.*[^8970]
*Tone 7. Ode 1. Heirmos:* By Thy nod the water's nature, before easily molded, was transformed into an earthy form, O Lord; therefore Israel, having journeyed on foot dryshod, sings unto Thee a song of victory [cf. Exod. 14:21–22, 29].[^8971]

*Refrain: Holy Apostles, pray to God for us.*[^8972]

*Troparion 1:* Having become godlike by a nod toward the divine, ye crushed the gods of the godless, and raised up all who have believed in God unto divine love, O all-honored ones.[^8973]

*Troparion 2:* Having become the salt of all the earth, O God-proclaiming Apostles of the Lord, stay all the rot of my heart, and heal that which has become bitterly foolish [cf. Matt. 5:13].[^8974]

*Troparion 3:* When the most righteous Judge of all is about to sit with you at the second coming, O divine Apostles: then deliver us from all condemnation [cf. Matt. 19:28].[^8975]

*Theotokion:* O most pure Mother of God, together with the incorporeal Angels, and the Prophets, and the Martyrs, and the Apostles, pray that we may receive forgiveness of transgressions, O Virgin, and rich mercy.[^8976]

*Second Canon to the holy wonderworker Nicholas. (Whose acrostic is: Accept, Nicholas, the seventh supplication of Joseph.)*[^8977]
*Tone 7. Ode 1. Heirmos:* Unto God Who aided Moses in Egypt to bring forth Israel, unto Him alone let us sing, for He hath been glorified [cf. Exod. 15:1].[^8978]

*Troparion 1:* Having passed through a glorified life upon earth, O Nicholas, make them that glorify thee partakers of the glory that is there.[^8979]

*Troparion 2:* Sailing in the abyss of cruel things, we are compassed about by the waves of worldly temptations, O all-blessed one: deliver us from them.[^8980]

*Troparion 3:* Extend unto me now a helpful hand of aid, O Father Nicholas: deliver me from enemies visible and invisible.[^8981]

*Theotokion:* He Who made the immaterial servants was born of thee, O Mother of God, because of ineffable mercy, and became visible unto men.[^8982]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* O Lord and Savior, Who in the beginning didst establish the heavens by Thy powerful Word, and all their host by the all-effecting and divine Spirit: establish me upon the immovable rock of Thy confession [cf. LXX Ps. 32:6].[^8983]

*Troparion 1:* As the heavens declare the glory of God, as said the prophet, ye proclaim it, O divine disciples, explaining His divine incarnation and sufferings openly, O wise ones, whereby ye deliver all from passions, death, and corruption [cf. LXX Ps. 18:2].[^8984]

*Troparion 2:* Ye were shown forth as sharpened arrows of Christ the Mighty One, O wise disciples of the Lord; deliver me from the arrows of the deceitful one, and heal my heart that is sorely wounded by the weapon of sin [cf. LXX Ps. 44:6; 119:4; Eph. 6:16].[^8985]

*Troparion 3:* Me who have been bitterly deceived and stripped of the divine vesture, O disciples of Christ, hasten to enlighten with divine garments; for ye stripped bare all the wiles of the enemy, and clothed him with shame [cf. Gen. 3:7; Ps. 34:26; Eph. 6:11].[^8986]

*Theotokion:* O Word, Who didst establish the heavenly hosts, Who gavest grace unto the Prophets, and the Disciples, and unto all the Martyrs: by their prayers, O God, and those of Thy most pure Mother, save all, and show bounty, as Thou art compassionate [cf. LXX Ps. 32:6].[^8987]

*Canon to Nicholas. Ode 3. Heirmos:* O Lover of mankind, Who didst establish the heavens by a word, and didst establish the foundation of the earth upon many waters: establish my mind in Thy will [cf. LXX Ps. 32:6; 103:2, 5].[^8988]

*Troparion 1:* Offering supplications for the world, save us from every necessity and countless afflictions, holy Nicholas.[^8989]

*Troparion 2:* As thou didst deliver from prison them that were inextricably bound, so having torn asunder the bonds of my evils, reconcile me unto God by thy prayers, holy Nicholas.[^8990]

*Troparion 3:* By night and by day we call thee protector; offer our prayers unto the Lord, ever preserving us, holy Nicholas.[^8991]

*Theotokion:* O Tongs, who didst receive the divine Coal within thy divine womb without being burned in any wise: burn up our transgressions, O Pure One [cf. Isa. 6:6].[^8992]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Leaving not the Father's bosom, and descending unto earth, O Christ God: I have heard the mystery of Thy dispensation, and I glorified Thee, O only Lover of mankind.[^8993]

*Troparion 1:* The Son Who is co-enthroned with the Father, having become incarnate upon earth as...[^8994]

...man, chose you as disciples, to preach His Divinity unto all nations.[^8995]

*Troparion 2:* Having fallen in a mind-damaging way unto the creators of all passions, wretched that I am, I was wounded in heart; therefore I pray: heal me, O physicians of the sick, Apostles.[^8996]

*Troparion 3:* Deliver us from passions, and from cruel misfortunes and sorrows, O glorious Apostles, and from every assault and all-painful torment.[^8997]

*Theotokion:* Chosen Mother of God, together with the holy Angels, and Martyrs, and Prophets, pray that we may be delivered from misfortunes, and afflictions, and transgressions.[^8998]

*Canon to Nicholas. Ode 4. Heirmos:* The heavens have been covered by Thy dispensation, O Christ God, with the virtue of Thine ineffable wisdom, O Lover of mankind [cf. Hab. 3:3].[^8999]

*Troparion 1:* By thy wisdom having blackened the mind of Arius that was darkened by heresy, O Nicholas, thou didst enlighten them that were deceived by him.[^9000]

*Troparion 2:* Heal my many wounds by thy God-acceptable prayers, O God-blessed Father Nicholas, and enlighten my heart.[^9001]

*Troparion 3:* Having put to death passionate wantonness, and having quickened me who was mortified thereby through thy prayers, O all-blessed one, make me new.[^9002]

*Theotokion:* Thou didst bear in the flesh the Timeless One; implore Him, O all-immaculate one, to deliver us who sing of thee from evils of many years.[^9003]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* O Lord my God, rising early from the night unto Thee I pray: grant me forgiveness of my transgressions, and direct my paths unto the light of Thy commandments, I pray [cf. Isa. 26:9].[^9004]

*Troparion 1:* O Lord my God, Who of old didst bestow peace upon Thine Apostles: by their prayers grant peace unto all, and forgiveness of transgressions [cf. John 14:27; 20:19, 21].[^9005]

*Troparion 2:* O Lord my God, Who knowest the sins of my soul, and the uncorrected disposition of my heart: show bounty and save me, through the supplications of Thine Apostles.[^9006]

*Troparion 3:* O Lord my God, Who for Thy great goodness didst save the thief and the harlot who had sinned: by the prayers of Thine Apostles show bounty also unto me, the prodigal [cf. Luke 7:37–50; 23:39–43].[^9007]

*Theotokion:* O Lord my God, Who wast born of the unwedded Virgin: by her...[^9008]

...prayers, and those of Thine Apostles, grant me cleansing of transgressions, and deliver me from future torment.[^9009]

*Canon to Nicholas. Ode 5. Heirmos:* My spirit rises early unto Thee, O God, for Thou art light, and Thy commandments have become healings unto Thy servants, O Lover of mankind [cf. Isa. 26:9].[^9010]

*Troparion 1:* The counsels of undisciplined men that are ever stirred up against us, O father Nicholas, make of no effect by thy prayers.[^9011]

*Troparion 2:* Tear asunder the bonds of our evils by thy prayers, O all-holy Nicholas, who didst bind the malice of the soul-destroying serpent [cf. Gen. 3:1; Rev. 20:2].[^9012]

*Troparion 3:* Ever sinning in mind, and held fast by a multitude of cruel things, O father, we warmly call upon thee, Nicholas, as our protector.[^9013]

*Theotokion:* Mary, Lady of all creation, completely set free my humble heart from the enemy who shamelessly seeks to dominate it, O Mother of God.[^9014]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Sailing in the surge of worldly cares, overwhelmed with the ship by sins, and cast forth unto the soul-corrupting beast, like Jonah I cry unto Thee, O Christ: Bring me up from the death-bearing abyss! [cf. Jonah 2:3–7].[^9015]

*Troparion 1:* Having become divine disciples of Wisdom Itself, they made foolish the Greek philosophy, and destroyed the sophist of malice; and them that were wandering in ignorance, the all-wise ones enlightened with the light of piety [cf. 1 Cor. 1:20].[^9016]

*Troparion 2:* Thou Who of old didst cleanse Peter's transgression through his tears: by his prayers cleanse the innumerable transgressions of my soul, for the sake of Thy boundless tender compassion and Thine abundant goodness, O Christ [cf. Matt. 26:75].[^9017]

*Troparion 3:* Thou Who of old didst show bounty unto the Ninevites who repented: by Thine accustomed tender mercy have mercy also upon me, for the sake of Thine Apostles; and because of the multitude of transgressions, O Redeemer, do not add a multitude of torments [cf. Jonah 3:5–10].[^9018]

*Theotokion:* O thou who didst bear the Light: enlighten my soul that is darkened by passions, and pray with the Apostles, and with the Prophets and Martyrs, that I may be delivered from every sin, every harm, and all malice of the enemy.[^9019]

*Canon to Nicholas. Ode 6. Heirmos:* Jonah cried aloud from the belly of hades: Bring up my life from corruption; and we cry aloud unto Thee: O all-powerful Savior, have mercy upon us [cf. Jonah 2:3, 7].[^9020]

*Troparion 1:* Thou didst destroy the unjust sentence of death, and by thy mercy, O Father Nicholas, didst save them that were about to die, as a fervent helper unto them that call upon thee [cf. the deliverance of the three innocent men from execution].[^9021]

*Troparion 2:* Still the infirmities of our souls, and shut the mouths that are ever opened against them that love thee, O most holy pastor.[^9022]

*Troparion 3:* Thou didst destroy the bitter pasture of Arius's impiety, O Nicholas, by the tempering of thy words, O sacred initiate; thou wast an establishment unto the Orthodox.[^9023]

*Theotokion:* Heal my wretched soul that is incurably sick with worldly deceptions, O all-immaculate one, and the besetment of many sins.[^9024]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* Of old the youths showed forth the fiery furnace to be dropping dew, praising the one God, and saying: O God of the fathers, supremely exalted and exceedingly glorious [cf. Dan. 3:49–50].[^9025]

*Troparion 1:* Of old ye quenched the furnace of bitter godlessness with the dew of divine preaching, O glorious Apostles, crying aloud: O God of the fathers, supremely exalted and exceedingly glorious.[^9026]

*Troparion 2:* Snatch me, O Christ, from cruel sin, and from torment in hades, and from pains in Gehenna, and save me, I pray, by the supplications of Thine Apostles, O Word.[^9027]

*Troparion 3:* Having caught men with the net of the Word from the abyss of ignorance, O disciples of Christ, save me who am overwhelmed by boundless transgressions and tempest-tossed [cf. Matt. 4:19].[^9028]

*Theotokion:* With the Angels, with the Martyrs, with the Venerable, and the Apostles, O all-immaculate one, implore thy Son and Lord, that Thy servants may be saved from all manner of assaults and afflictions.[^9029]

*Canon to Nicholas. Ode 7. Heirmos:* Thou didst bedew the burning furnace, O Savior, and didst save the children who sang and said: Blessed art Thou unto the ages, O Lord God of our fathers [cf. Dan. 3:49–50, 52].[^9030]

*Troparion 1:* Having become the beauty of the Church, O wise Nicholas, deliver me from all the ugliness of disgraceful passions, ever praying unto the Benefactor of the whole world, O hierarch.[^9031]

*Troparion 2:* With the showers of thy prayers, O wise Nicholas, water the hearts of us all, that we may bring forth fruits worthy of repentance, O hierarch Nicholas [cf. Matt. 3:8].[^9032]

*Troparion 3:* Enlighten with thy prayers the thoughts of us who rise early with faith and glorify God, O hierarch Nicholas, who by thy supplications didst destroy the temple of Artemis.[^9033]

*Theotokion:* Every tongue glorifies thee with faith, O Pure One, who art the glory and boast of our race, and the guide of the wandering, O all-blessed Mother of God.[^9034]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* The bush that held communion with the fire on Sinai without being consumed made God known unto Moses, slow of tongue and of an impediment in his speech; and the zeal of God showed forth the three children in the fire to be unconquerable singers: Praise the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages [cf. Exod. 3:2; 4:10; Dan. 3:57].[^9035]

*Troparion 1:* Having been kindled as coals with the noetic fire, the disciples of Christ burned up all the idol delusion like reed, and enlightened the souls of the faithful who cry aloud: Praise the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^9036]

*Troparion 2:* Enlighten now my soul darkened by sin, and my heart darkened by disgraceful passions, O Apostles of Christ, partakers of the ever-existing Light, that I may unceasingly cry aloud: Praise the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^9037]

*Troparion 3:* Heal my soul that is wounded by the venomous bites of the proud one, O Thou Who didst receive wounds in the flesh, through the prayers of Thy sacred disciples, and save them that sing: Praise the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^9038]

*Theotokion:* Having unburningly borne the Fire, thou wast preserved, O most pure Virgin; therefore implore Him Whom thou didst bear, together with the incorporeal Choirs and the Apostles, that they who sing with faith may be saved: Praise the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^9039]

*Canon to Nicholas. Ode 8. Heirmos:* Imitating the children in the furnace, having received the spiritual dew, we cry aloud in faith: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord [cf. Dan. 3:49–50, 57].[^9040]

*Troparion 1:* In Myra thy body, fragrant with myrrh, O all-holy Nicholas, lying in the tomb, pours forth myrrh unto them that draw near, and stays infirmities in men.[^9041]

*Troparion 2:* The Creator and Lord of the world showed thee forth as an intercessor for the world; therefore unto them that call upon thee in...[^9042]

...necessities now, O Nicholas, thou art found to be a most ready deliverer.[^9043]

*Troparion 3:* There is none who calls upon thee in afflictions, and does not quickly find consolation; therefore we pray thee: lighten all our infirmities, Nicholas.[^9044]

*Theotokion:* Every tongue sings of thee and glorifies thee, O Virgin Bride of God; for thou didst bear the all-hymned God; Him unceasingly implore, that our souls may be saved.[^9045]

*My soul magnifies the Lord:*[^9046]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* Thee who without trial of corruption didst give birth, and didst lend flesh unto the Word, the all-craftsman, O Mother unwedded, Virgin Mother of God: receptacle of the Uncontainable, dwelling-place of thine uncontainable Creator, we magnify thee.[^9047]

*Troparion 1:* The Word of God the Father showed you forth as sons of the light and of the day; having loved Him, O divine Apostles, lights of the universe, destroyers of demons, guides of the wandering, ye were shown forth as firm foundations of the Church [cf. 1 Thess. 5:5; Eph. 2:20].[^9048]

*Troparion 2:* Ye plows of the Word, who bore His easiest yoke upon your necks: renew now my soul that has grown barren through passions, and make it fruitful with the seed of repentance [cf. Matt. 11:30].[^9049]

*Troparion 3:* Me who have been heedless of the divine commandments, and who have become a dwelling-place of demons and a workshop of every sin: show bounty unto me, O Word, through the prayers of Thy glorious Apostles, as Thou art the Lover of mankind.[^9050]

*Troparion 4:* The earth is sanctified by the divine relics of the all-glorious Apostles through the Holy Spirit; and the Church of the firstborn on high is unceasingly brightened by their spirits: for their sake, O Savior, show bounty unto all [cf. Heb. 12:23].[^9051]

*Theotokion:* That Thou send me not into the fire prepared for me, the Virgin who bare Thee implores Thee, and the divine incorporeal Choirs, the Apostles, Prophets, and Martyrs, Hierarchs, and the spirits of the righteous, O all-bountiful, Most Merciful One [cf. Matt. 25:41].[^9052]

*Canon to Nicholas. Ode 9. Heirmos:* Mother beyond nature, and Virgin according to nature, the only blessed one among women: with hymns we the faithful magnify the Mother of God [cf. Luke 1:28, 42].[^9053]

*Troparion 1:* Having followed the manners of the holy Apostles, thou didst inherit their thrones, as a venerable hierarch, praiseworthy Nicholas.[^9054]

*Troparion 2:* As a divine zealot and a most great helper throughout all the world unto them that warmly call upon thee, O blessed one, the Creator showed thee forth.[^9055]

*Troparion 3:* With a contrite heart we cry aloud unto thee, O Father: be thou consolation unto us in afflictions, ever driving away griefs from our souls, O Nicholas [cf. LXX Ps. 50:19].[^9056]

*Troparion 4:* The felling of death, like an axe unto a tree, lies before thee, O soul; be not slothful therefore, but strive earnestly to show forth fruits of repentance unto God [cf. Matt. 3:8, 10].[^9057]

*Both now... Theotokion:* Thou dost bear Him Who bears all things with thy holy hands: Him implore, O Pure One, that we may be saved unharmed from the malice of the stranger [cf. Heb. 1:3].[^9058]

*It is truly meet. Litany, Exapostilarion, and the customary Psalms.*[^9059]

*At the Aposticha, Stichera of the Apostles, Tone 7:*[^9060]
*Sticheron 1:* Glorious angels, pillars of the Church, preachers of truth, being bright luminaries: by the spiritual fire ye burned up all delusion, and enlightened the human race [cf. Gal. 2:9; Phil. 2:15]. Therefore we pray you: implore the Savior and our God to bring peace to the world, and to save our souls.[^9061]

*Verse:* We were filled in the morning with Thy mercy, O Lord, and we rejoiced... And look upon Thy servants, and upon Thy works, and guide their sons [LXX Ps. 89:14, 16].[^9062]

*Sticheron 2:* Apostles of Christ, laborers of the Savior! Bearing the Cross like a plow upon your shoulders, having cleansed the earth possessed by idol delusion, ye sowed the word of faith [cf. Luke 9:62; Mark 4:14]: truly worthily are ye named the holy Apostles of Christ.[^9063]

*Verse:* And let the brightness of the Lord our God be upon us, and direct Thou the works of our hands over us; yea, the work of our hands do Thou direct [LXX Ps. 89:17].[^9064]

*For the Martyrs:* Breathing as one and looking unto one thing, O passion-bearing Martyrs, having found one way of life, which is death for Christ, being zealous for one another's end, O what a marvel! anticipating tortures like treasures, they said one to another: For if we die not now, we shall die in any case, and we shall pay what is due to existence; let us make necessity a point of honor, let that which is common be reckoned as our own; by death let us purchase life. By their prayers, O God, have mercy on us.[^9065]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Thou wast born ineffably of the Virgin, O Christ, and Thou hast enlightened them that are in darkness, who cry aloud: Lord, glory to Thee [cf. Isa. 9:2; Luke 1:79].[^9066]

*It is good to give praise. Trisagion. After Our Father: the troparia, litany, and First Hour, and the Dismissal.*[^9067]

*On Thursday, The Beatitudes, Tone 7:*[^9068]
*Troparion 1:* Fair to look upon and good for food was the fruit that put me to death [cf. Gen. 3:6]; Christ is the tree of life, whereof eating I die not [cf. Gen. 2:9; Rev. 2:7], but cry aloud with the thief: Remember me, O Lord, in Thy kingdom [cf. Luke 23:42].[^9069]

*Troparion 2:* Out of the depth of evil belief ye drew the flocks of the Gentiles, O all-wise ones, unto divinely beautiful faith and unto the spiritual banquet, having brought them as a precious tribute, O glorious Apostles.[^9070]

*Troparion 3:* Having driven away the darkening of cruel delusion by the light of preaching, O Apostles of the Lord, ye manifestly enlightened the hearts of the pious; therefore with divine songs we call you blessed.[^9071]

*For the Martyrs:* Though cut limb by limb with the sword, O divine sufferers, ye remained uncut from the love of the Redeemer [cf. Rom. 8:35]; ascending unto Whom, ye now live in joy, all illumined with heavenly glory.[^9072]

*Glory:* The most wicked corrupter of the human race holds me fast; through the prayers of Thy preachers, O almighty Trinity, snatch me from his throat, that I may magnify Thy boundless mercy.[^9073]

*Both now... Theotokion:* The [Son] co-enthroned with the Father, O pure Virgin, thou didst bring forth from thy pure blood, bearing mortal flesh, that He might make human nature immortal; therefore as is due we all call thee blessed.[^9074]

## Friday: The Precious and Life-Giving Cross[^9075]

*On Thursday Evening.*[^9076]
*On "Lord, I have cried," Stichera of the Cross, Tone 7.*[^9077]
*Tone 7, Podoben: "No longer are we forbidden" (Не ктомꙋ̀ возбрана́еми є҆смы̀):*[^9078]

*Sticheron 1:* That Thou mightest make man God, O Christ, Thou becamest man, O all-good Christ, and Thou wast crucified. Glory to Thy power![^9079]

*Sticheron 2:* When the Jewish council condemned Thee to the Cross, O Jesus, the earth trembled and the light of the sun hid itself [cf. Matt. 27:45, 51].[^9080]

*Sticheron 3:* With thorns the lawless gathering crowned Thee, O Immortal One, Who cuttest out the thorns of delusion from the root, O Holy King [cf. Matt. 27:29].[^9081]

*Other Stichera, to the Most Holy Theotokos, Tone 7.*[^9082]
*Tone 7, Podoben: "Today Judas watches" (Дне́сь бди́тъ І҆ю́да):*[^9083]

*Sticheron 1:* Seeing the Lamb outstretched upon the Tree between two thieves, the spotless Ewe-lamb and Virgin cried aloud: Woe is me, my sweetest Child! What is this strange and most glorious mystery? How has the lawless assembly raised Thee upon the Cross, and given Thee gall to drink, Who didst satisfy men with manna [cf. Matt. 27:34, 38; Exod. 16:15]?[^9084]

*Sticheron 2:* When the Virgin and Thy Mother saw Thee nailed to the Cross by the judgment of the lawless Jews upon the Place of the Skull, O my Christ, she cried aloud: Woe is me, my most beloved Son! What is this strange sight? How do the senseless Jewish children nail Thee, the Lord of all, unto the Cross [cf. Matt. 27:33]?[^9085]

*Sticheron 3:* Knowing thee alone, O all-holy Sovereign Lady, to be revealed as a Virgin after childbearing, seeing Him Whom thou didst bear without seed willingly nailing His hands unto the Tree, the all-pure one weeping cried aloud: Willingly, O Long-suffering One, Thou diest, delivering from death all them that sing praise unto Thee.[^9086]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:* I am wounded with the sword of grief, seeing Thee upon the Cross Who suspended the earth upon the waters and didst stretch out the heaven like a skin [cf. Luke 2:35; Job 26:7; LXX Ps. 103:2]; for I see Thee hanging naked and outstretched, and I choose rather to die first, O my most beloved Son! Tremble, O earth, and be shaken! said the all-pure one weeping.[^9087]

*O Gladsome Light. Prokeimenon of the day. Vouchsafe, O Lord.*[^9088]

*At the Aposticha, Stichera of the Cross, Tone 7:*[^9089]
*Sticheron 1:* No longer are we forbidden from the tree of life, having Thy Cross as our hope: Lord, glory to Thee [cf. Gen. 3:24].[^9090]

*Verse:* Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes, unto Thee that dwellest in heaven... So our eyes look unto the Lord our God, until He take pity on us [LXX Ps. 122:1–2].[^9091]

*Sticheron 2:* Being suspended upon the Tree, O Immortal One, Thou didst crush all the snares of the devil: Lord, glory to Thee [cf. LXX Ps. 123:7].[^9092]

*Verse:* Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us... For exceedingly was our soul filled with the reproaches of the prosperous, and with the contempt of the proud [LXX Ps. 122:3–4].[^9093]

*For the Martyrs:* Glory to Thee, O Christ God, the boast of Apostles and the rejoicing...

...of Martyrs, whose preaching is the consubstantial Trinity.[^9094]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:* Joy-making words I heard of old at Thy birth, O Child, wherewith I expected to be filled with joy and to make glad afflicted souls [cf. Luke 2:10–14]; but these things have manifestly been turned into sorrow by Thy crucifixion, and grief tears me apart, and I choose to die, sorrow having filled my heart.[^9095]

*Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart, and the rest.*[^9096]

*On Thursday at Compline.*[^9097]
*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos, Tone 7.*[^9098]
*Ode 1. Heirmos:* Unto God Who shook Pharaoh off into the Red Sea, let us sing a song of victory, for He hath been glorified [cf. Exod. 14:27; 15:1].[^9099]

*Refrain: Most Holy Sovereign Lady Mother of God, save us.*[^9100]

*Troparion 1:* Foreseeing the end of my life that has come, O Sovereign Lady, I cry aloud: do thou grant me cleansing of sins, O Bride of God.[^9101]

*Troparion 2:* Having fled unto the deep of thy goodness, I cry aloud: O good Sovereign Lady, deliver me from all torment.[^9102]

*Glory:* O Sovereign Lady, accept us who flee unto thee with faith, and grant deliverance from assaults and afflictions.[^9103]

*Both now:* Thou didst prophetically raise up the fallen tabernacle of Adam, having carried the Savior and God in thy womb, O all-pure one [cf. Amos 9:11; Acts 15:16].[^9104]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* The Church of Christ was established by faith; for unceasingly with songs she cries aloud, singing: Holy art Thou, O Lord, and my spirit sings praise unto Thee [cf. 1 Sam. 2:2].[^9105]

*Troparion 1:* O thou who ineffably didst conceive God and the Master, deliver me who am held fast by the tempest of sin from these things, and save me, O all-pure one.[^9106]

*Troparion 2:* Illumine me with the rays of repentance, who am held fast by the night of unseemly sins, O Maiden, granting me ever to do bright deeds [cf. Rom. 13:12].[^9107]

*Glory:* O Mother of God who didst bear the heavenly Bread, Who nourishes the hearts of all the faithful: satisfy my passionate, hungering soul [cf. John 6:33, 35].[^9108]

*Both now:* I was established, having understood thy divine childbearing, and I am enriched by thy protection, O Virgin, crying: Holy art Thou, O Lord, Who savest our souls.[^9109]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* I heard report of Thee, and was afraid; I considered Thy works, and was astonished, O Lord [LXX Hab. 3:2].[^9110]

*Troparion 1:* Despise me not, neither put me to shame, who groan before thee, O Sovereign Lady, and diligently cry aloud from my mind.[^9111]

*Troparion 2:* Burn up the thorns of carnal pleasures that have pierced my wretched soul, O Sovereign Lady who didst bear the divine Fire [cf. Heb. 6:8; 12:29].[^9112]

*Glory:* Grant me remission of sins, O all-blessed Pure One, and deliver me from eternal and fearful torment.[^9113]

*Both now:* The Word that dwelt within thee, O all-pure one, fashioned anew my nature that was fallen through transgressions.[^9114]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* My spirit seeks Thee early in the morning, O God, for Thou art light, and Thy commandments have become healings unto Thy servants, O Lover of mankind [cf. LXX Isa. 26:9].[^9115]

*Troparion 1:* I, most wretched, have become like irrational beasts, serving my wretched flesh through irrational passions [cf. LXX Ps. 48:13, 21]: do thou, O Mother of God, grant me revival.[^9116]

*Troparion 2:* Have mercy on me who have sinned more than all men, and who flee unto thy warm protection, O Virgin Mother of God, and snatch me from torment.[^9117]

*Glory:* May they that confess thee as Mother of God, O Virgin Mother, be vouchsafed through thee, O Mother of God, to obtain the unending kingdom and delight.[^9118]

*Both now:* O blessed and most glorious Mother unwedded, quicken my soul that is mortified by sins and bent down by unrestrained passions.[^9119]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Jonah cried out of the belly of Hades: Lead up my life from corruption! But we cry aloud unto Thee: O almighty Savior, have mercy on us [cf. Jonah 2:3, 7].[^9120]

*Troparion 1:* The tempest of sin holds me fast, O Virgin, and leads me to the corruption of despair; but stretch forth unto me a firm and all-powerful hand of help.[^9121]

*Troparion 2:* Pour out upon me, O Sovereign Lady, the oil of the warm mercy that is in thee, and deliver me from sins, and snatch me from the eternal fire [cf. Luke 10:34].[^9122]

*Glory:* O Virgin who didst release Eve from pangs by thy most pure childbearing: release for me, O Maiden, the passionate sicknesses of my soul and body [cf. Gen. 3:16].[^9123]

*Both now:* Thou hymnest God incarnate of thee, and thou weepest...

...looking upon Him lifted up upon the Cross, O Virgin Mother, pure and all-immaculate.[^9124]

*Then, Lord, have mercy (40). Glory... Both now...*[^9125]

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 7:* The Fruit of thy womb, O Pure One, planted the Cross in the ends of the earth, and delivered the world from corruption; therefore as most glorious we magnify thee.[^9126]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* Cast into the fiery furnace, the venerable children transformed the fire into dew, crying aloud in song: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God of our fathers [cf. Dan. 3:21, 49–50, 52].[^9127]

*Troparion 1:* The threat of my transgressions has multiplied, worthy of judgment; O Pure One, I fall down and cry unto thee: before the end grant me cleansing, and tears of compunction, and amendment of ways.[^9128]

*Troparion 2:* Having fallen grievously sick with defiled transgressions, O Pure One, and going toward the grave of despair: as thou didst bear the Life of all, O all-pure one, quicken me by divine deeds.[^9129]

*Glory:* O good Mother of God, delivering them that call with faith upon thy ever-living grace from spiritual death, vouchsafe them the kingdom through thy most pure prayers.[^9130]

*Both now:* We glorify the incomprehensible childbearing of the Virgin, whereby we were delivered from death; on Whose account being born into incorruption, we cry: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God of our fathers.[^9131]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* The one unoriginate King of glory, Whom the heavenly powers bless, and the angelic hosts tremble before: praise Him, ye priests; supremely exalt Him, ye people, unto the ages [cf. Dan. 3:57–58].[^9132]

*Troparion 1:* The furnace of passions burns me, O Virgin, and subjects me to the fire of pleasures; do thou make haste and quench it with thy merciful dew, granting strong cooling unto my soul.[^9133]

*Troparion 2:* I have defiled my soul, and corrupted my flesh with pleasures, wallowing in them, and I have become a reproach unto men and a laughingstock unto enemies [cf. LXX Ps. 30:12]: O Sovereign Lady Mother of God, be thou a helper unto me.[^9134]

*Glory:* Implore God Whom thou didst bear, O Virgin, to send down salvation unto them that honor thee with faith, and release from perils, and swift change from cruel infirmities, and eternal grace.[^9135]

*Both now:* Him Who created Adam according to His image, and Who came forth from his mingled nature, and loosed the first curse: praise the Lord, ye priests, and supremely exalt Him unto the ages [cf. Gen. 1:26–27; 3:17; Dan. 3:57].[^9136]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* O all-hymned one, higher than the heavens, who without seed didst conceive the unoriginate Word, and didst bear incarnate God unto men: therefore we all magnify thee.[^9137]

*Troparion 1:* O all-radiant Virgin Mother, Mother of God, guide brightly into the fear of God my wretched soul, grievously darkened by carnal passions, by the light of thy prayers.[^9138]

*Troparion 2:* Having despaired of salvation because of the multitude of my transgressions, and being found in perplexity, O all-holy Sovereign Lady: send down upon me thy beneficence and thy mercy.[^9139]

*Glory:* Thy childbearing beyond nature, O Mother of God, astonishes the Angels and strikes men with awe; for unto all it is ineffable and incomprehensible; singing thereof, we piously glorify thee.[^9140]

*Both now:* Strip me, O Virgin, of the garments of mortification and corruption, who didst bear the garment of salvation, the incarnate Word, unto men [cf. Isa. 61:10]; therefore we all ever magnify thee.[^9141]

*It is truly meet. Trisagion. After Our Father: the troparia and the rest.*[^9142]

*On Friday at Matins.*[^9143]
*After the first Kathisma reading, Sessional Hymns of the Cross, Tone 7:*[^9144]
*Sessional Hymn 1:* The Church cries aloud unto Thee, O Christ God, in the pine, the cedar, and the cypress, worshiping Thee [cf. Isa. 60:13]: grant victories unto our Emperor (Name)[^9145] for the sake of the Mother of God, and have mercy on us.[^9146]

*Verse:* Exalt ye the Lord our God, and worship the footstool of His feet, for He is holy [LXX Ps. 98:5].[^9147]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* O Thou Who for my sake didst endure nailing to the Cross, receive my vigilant praise, O Christ God, and save me, as Thou art the Lover of mankind.[^9148]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:* Preserved by the Cross of thy Son, O Virgin, we escape the assaults of demons; therefore as is due we praise and glorify thee, O all-hymned Mother of God.[^9149]

*After the second Kathisma reading, Sessional Hymns, Tone 7:*[^9150]
*Sessional Hymn 1:* Thou didst show forth the tree of Thy Cross brighter than fire and more active than flame, O Christ...

...burning up the sins of men, and enlightening the hearts of them that sing of Thy voluntary crucifixion: O Christ God, glory to Thee.[^9151]

*Verse:* But God is our King before the ages, He hath wrought salvation in the midst of the earth [LXX Ps. 73:12].[^9152]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* Seeing Thy Cross ever exalted, O Lord, we approach with songs and hymns in faith, kissing it with fear and joy: sanctify Thy servants and bring peace to Thy world by its manifestation, O only Most Merciful One.[^9153]

*Verse:* God is wonderful in His saints, the God of Israel [LXX Ps. 67:36].[^9154]

*For the Martyrs:* Holy Martyrs, pray that remission of our transgressions may be given unto us, and that we may be delivered from the torments that are expected, and from bitter death, we pray.[^9155]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:* O pure Mother of God, Ever-Virgin, seeing thy Son hanging upon the Cross, weeping as a Mother, thou didst magnify His awesome condescension, O unwedded Sovereign Lady.[^9156]

*After the third Kathisma reading, Sessional Hymns, Tone 7:*[^9157]
*Sessional Hymn 1:* Thy precious Cross, O Christ, is the weapon of the world and unconquerable victory: whereby casting down invisible enemies, we thankfully sing praise unto Thee.[^9158]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* O Thou Who by the Tree of the Cross didst heal the condemnation of Adam: heal our contrite hearts, and save us [cf. LXX Ps. 146:3].[^9159]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:* The Fruit of thy womb, O all-pure one, planted the Cross in the ends of the earth, and delivered the world from corruption; therefore, O most glorious one, we magnify thee.[^9160]

*Canon to the Precious and Life-Giving Cross of the Lord. (Whose acrostic is: Christ on the Tree hath put an end to the ancient harm.) A creation of Joseph.*[^9161]
*Tone 7. Ode 1. Heirmos:* Unto God Who shook Pharaoh off into the Red Sea, let us sing a song of victory, for He hath been glorified [cf. Exod. 14:27; 15:1].[^9162]

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy Cross and Crucifixion.*[^9163]

*Troparion 1:* Christ, being lifted up upon the Cross, drew all men unto Himself, and cast down the enemy who had cast down all [cf. John 12:32].[^9164]

*Troparion 2:* Water for my life, O Jesus, Thou didst pour forth from Thy life-bearing side, and Thou didst put the enemy to death, O Master, by becoming dead [cf. John 19:34].[^9165]

*For the Martyrs:* Offer diligent prayer unto Christ for us, O passion-bearers, that we all may be delivered from the awesome judgment.[^9166]

*For the Martyrs:* Having humbled yourselves for the sake of Christ, O all-wise Martyrs, ye cast down the proud enemy by divine grace [cf. Matt. 23:12; 1 Pet. 5:5].[^9167]

*Both now... Theotokion:* Seeing Him Who shone forth from thee in His exceeding tender compassion hanging upon the Cross, O Sovereign Lady, thou didst weep, glorifying Him.[^9168]

*Second Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos. A creation of Joseph. Tone 7. Heirmos the same.*[^9169]
*Troparion 1:* Thou didst renew our forefather Adam who had fallen into the corruption of disobedience, O all-pure one, having borne God in the flesh [cf. Rom. 5:19].[^9170]

*Troparion 2:* With gladness we cry aloud unto thee the voice of Gabriel, O Pure One: Rejoice, O all-pure hope of all; rejoice, Bride of God [cf. Luke 1:28].[^9171]

*Troparion 3:* Quicken my mortified mind by the life-giving energy of Him Who was truly born of thee, O all-pure Virgin.[^9172]

*Troparion 4:* They that bless thee in faith are manifestly blessed by God; for thou didst bear the fountain of blessing, O only All-Hymned One [cf. Gen. 12:3; Gal. 3:9].[^9173]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* The Church of Christ was established by faith; for unceasingly with songs she cries aloud, singing: Holy art Thou, O Lord, and my spirit sings praise unto Thee [cf. 1 Sam. 2:2].[^9174]

*Troparion 1:* Being passionless in essence, how dost Thou endure passion, Who breathest life into me? How dost Thou die upon the Tree? Great is Thy mercy and long-suffering, O Savior![^9175]

*Troparion 2:* Upon the Cross between thieves, O Word, Thou wast unjustly lifted up, and Thou didst justify him who by faith recognized Thee as the Creator of all creation, willingly suffering [cf. Luke 23:39–43].[^9176]

*For the Martyrs:* Enduring the scraping of their flesh, and the cutting off of hands, feet, and all their limbs, the passion-bearers were vouchsafed glory, praying for us.[^9177]

*For the Martyrs:* Being enlightened in God by exceedingly many torments, O wise ones, ye have now inherited exceedingly great glory, ever praying for our souls.[^9178]

*Both now... Stavrotheotokion:* Grant remission of our debts, O pure Virgin, by thy maternal prayers, who didst bear God the Word Who became man, nailed to the Cross.[^9179]

*Canon to the Theotokos. Heirmos the same.*[^9180]
*Troparion 1:* Thee, O Virgin, typically as the all-holy tabernacle, the lawgiver on...

...the mountain God foretold [cf. Exod. 25:8–9, 40]; for thou becamest the dwelling-place of Him Who sanctifies all.[^9181]

*Troparion 2:* We all understand thee to be the holy land, O Virgin, which without seed brought forth for us the beautiful Ear of wheat, Jesus Christ, nourishing them that bless thee with faith and love.[^9182]

*Troparion 3:* The goings of God the Most High were seen, when He was ineffably incarnate in thee, O Virgin [cf. LXX Ps. 67:25]; for thou wast the Mother of Him Who reigns over all.[^9183]

*Troparion 4:* I was established, having understood thy divine childbearing, and I am enriched by thy protection, O Virgin, crying: Holy art Thou, O Lord, save our souls.[^9184]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* I heard report of Thee, and was afraid; I considered Thy works, and was astonished, O Lord [LXX Hab. 3:2].[^9185]

*Troparion 1:* As a most fair cluster of grapes Thou wast lifted up upon the Cross, O Master, and didst distil the wine of gladness, O Lover of mankind [cf. Song 1:13; 7:8; LXX Ps. 103:15].[^9186]

*Troparion 2:* Truly Thou didst calm the many-painful passions of men, O Master, willingly receiving passions upon Thy flesh.[^9187]

*For the Martyrs:* While being wounded, the passion-bearers wounded the adversary, and were revealed as physicians of our souls.[^9188]

*For the Martyrs:* The passion-bearers set themselves in array against the tormentors, and having conquered, were crowned with crowns of victory [cf. 2 Tim. 4:7–8].[^9189]

*Both now... Theotokion:* Without seed, O Virgin, thou didst bear the Word, Who by His goodness destroyed corruption upon the Cross.[^9190]

*Canon to the Theotokos. Heirmos the same.*[^9191]
*Troparion 1:* Habakkuk heard of thee, O all-pure one, as a mountain shadowed by the Spirit, being enlightened; for from thee God was revealed, being incarnate [cf. LXX Hab. 3:3].[^9192]

*Troparion 2:* The assembly of the Orthodox is set aright, singing of thee, the all-holy one, as Mother of God, crying unto thee with the Archangel: Rejoice [cf. Luke 1:28]![^9193]

*Troparion 3:* Smitten is the face of Nestorius, who would not profess thee to be the pure Mother of God; for truly, O all-pure one, thou didst bear God.[^9194]

*Troparion 4:* Finding thee fair and radiant in beauties, the Bridegroom made His abode in thee, and made thee the Mother of God, O all-pure one [cf. LXX Ps. 44:3; Song 1:15–16].[^9195]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* My spirit seeks Thee early in the morning, O God, for Thou art light, and Thy commandments... [cf. LXX Isa. 26:9].[^9196]

...have become healing unto Thy servants, O Lover of mankind.[^9197]

*Troparion 1:* Being lifted up upon the Tree in the flesh, O Master, Thou didst draw all creation forth from the pit of grievous ignorance unto the knowledge of Thyself, O Lover of mankind.[^9198]

*Troparion 2:* At the Place of the Skull, the lawless people demanded to crucify Thee, O Jesus, Who crushest the ruinous head of the serpent.[^9199]

*For the Martyrs:* Ye stayed the flow of impiety, O martyrs of Christ, by your divine blood, and in it ye drowned the tormentor Pharaoh.[^9200]

*Another for the Martyrs:* Being scraped mercilessly with iron claws, O passion-bearers, by your refutations ye tore asunder the vain-minded hearts, and became victors.[^9201]

*Glory... Both now: Stavrotheotokion:* God hath sanctified Thy womb, O holy and pure one, and made His abode therein; and being lifted up upon the Cross, He co-elevated creation with Himself.[^9202]

*Canon to the Theotokos. The Heirmos the same:*[^9203]

*Troparion 1:* Having loved Thy God-befitting, comely, most sweet, and good beauty, O Lady, the Master took up His abode in Thee, O Virgin.[^9204]

*Troparion 2:* O all-holy and light-bearing portal of grace, having illumined the universe with Thy most bright light, O Maiden, enlighten those who sing of Thee.[^9205]

*Troparion 3:* May those who confess Thee to be the Theotokos, O Virgin Mother, be counted worthy through Thee, O Mother of God, to attain unto the unfading kingdom and delight.[^9206]

*Troparion 4:* A temple of the All-seeing and Almighty One hast Thou been shown to be; for finding Thy womb more honorable than the heavens, He took up His dwelling therein, O all-pure Theotokos.[^9207]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Jonah cried out from the belly of Hades: Lead my life up from corruption! And we cry aloud unto Thee: O almighty Savior, have mercy upon us![^9208]

*Troparion 1:* Having appeared by reason of the tender compassion of Thy mercy, O Physician of the diseased, Thou didst heal ailing human nature by Thy Cross and Passion.[^9209]

*Troparion 2:* By the tree of old Adam was condemned; but by the Tree of the Cross he hath now been justified, receiving entrance into Paradise and the reception of its sweetness.[^9210]

*Troparion 3:* We sing of Thee Who wast crucified in the flesh; we glorify Thee Who wast crowned with thorns and hast crowned mankind with glory, O most good King.[^9211]

*For the Martyrs:* Having cast away the destructive harm of idolatry, O sufferers, having partaken of torments and died, ye reign with Christ.[^9212]

*Another for the Martyrs:* Neither persecution, nor famine, nor nakedness, nor perils, nor death at all separated the divine passion-bearers from the love of Christ.[^9213]

*Glory... Both now: Stavrotheotokion:* Thou dost hymn in song Him Who was incarnate of Thee, and dost weep, beholding Him lifted up upon the Cross, O holy and all-immaculate Virgin Mother.[^9214]

*Canon to the Theotokos. The Heirmos the same:*[^9215]

*Troparion 1:* In God-manly manner, Thou didst bear Him Who dwelt among mankind, O Theotokos Virgin, the only Benefactor, Who grants unto men their very being.[^9216]

*Troparion 2:* O Theotokos, Thou didst give birth unto Emmanuel, twofold in essence, Who was aforetime the perfect Word, God incarnate, Who grants redemption unto us.[^9217]

*Troparion 3:* The ark that received the tables of the law prefigured Thee, O Theotokos, as having received in Thy womb the Word of God incarnate for our sake.[^9218]

*Troparion 4:* Though filled with angelic tongues, they cannot proclaim Thy praises, O pure one. Yet we now, having received it as servants, offer Gabriel's "Rejoice!"[^9219]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* The children of old showed the fiery furnace to be dew-dropping, hymning the one God and saying: The highly exalted and most glorious God of our fathers.[^9220]

*Troparion 1:* Lightening the burden of my lawlessness, Thou wast lifted up upon the Cross in the midst of the lawless, O most good Jesus: the highly exalted and most good God of our fathers.[^9221]

*Troparion 2:* Being pierced with a spear in Thy divine side, Thou didst right the stumbling of the rib, O Savior, and Thou commandest the fiery sword ever to give me entrance into Paradise.[^9222]

*For the Martyrs:* Having become stars upon the height of the founded Church, O martyrs, ye illuminate creation with the brightness of your sufferings and with the radiance of healings.[^9223]

*Another for the Martyrs:* The book of the living ever records the Lord's martyrs who have zealously preserved the written ordinances of God and suffered valiantly.[^9224]

*Glory... Both now: Stavrotheotokion:* The all-holy one, beholding the all-holy Word Who shone forth from her lifted up upon the holy Cross and sanctifying those upon earth, wept aloud.[^9225]

*Canon to the Theotokos. The Heirmos the same:*[^9226]

*Troparion 1:* Thy Child indeed saved those in the fiery furnace of old; and now save us, O all-immaculate one, who cry aloud unto His coming which is beyond word: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^9227]

*Troparion 2:* David the Forefather of God, indeed, inscribs Thy glory, O Virgin, and clearly prophesies Thee to be the Queen of creatures, standing at the right hand, O all-immaculate one, of the God of our fathers.[^9228]

*Troparion 3:* Thou didst renew the nature of earthborn men, O pure one, by the renewal of Thy childbearing. Therefore we cry aloud unto Thee: Blessed unto all ages, O Lady, is the fruit of Thy womb.[^9229]

*Troparion 4:* Having had mercy through Thee upon Adam who was condemned and held fast by death, O all-pure one, the second Adam called him, who cried: Blessed art Thou Who wast born and hast renewed me.[^9230]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* The only unoriginate King of glory, Whom the heavenly hosts bless and the angelic ranks tremble before: praise Him, ye priests; ye people, supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^9231]

*Troparion 1:* The tree of knowledge showed me dead; yet Thou, O my Christ, having died upon the Tree, didst make me alive and enlighten me to sing: Praise Him, ye priests; ye people, supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^9232]

*Troparion 2:* The law-transgressing council crowned Thee with thorns, O King, Who uprootest the thorns of disobedience of the first-created Adam, and lifted Thee up upon the Cross, Who didst draw all men out of the abyss of deceit.[^9233]

*Troparion 3:* The senseless people stretched Thee upon the Tree, Who by understanding didst stretch out the heavens, Who healest our passions by Thy Passion, O Savior, and stayest our sicknesses by the pangs of the nails.[^9234]

*For the Martyrs:* The relics of the martyrs emit the fragrance of miracles unto those who draw near with undoubting heart, and ever drive away the stench of passions, and grant health in God unto all.[^9235]

*Glory... Both now: Stavrotheotokion:* The ranks of the saints pray unto the Master Who came forth from Thy womb, O Virgin, Who showed them upon the Cross the path of suffering, O pure one; and they hymn Thee in glory as Queen of all.[^9236]

*Canon to the Theotokos. The Heirmos the same:*[^9237]

*Troparion 1:* The incomprehensible Master of all, Whom the heavens cannot contain, O Theotokos, is contained within Thy womb, O Virgin; therefore, we faithful supremely exalt Thee with love unto the ages.[^9238]

*Troparion 2:* Him Who is higher than the intellect of all creation, O Maiden, Whom created nature cannot behold, hast Thou borne in Thy most pure arms; therefore, we faithful supremely exalt Thee with love unto all ages.[^9239]

*Troparion 3:* Entreat God, Whom Thou didst bear, O Virgin, to send down salvation unto those who faithfully honor Thee, and deliverance from perils, and swift relief from grievous infirmities, and eternal grace.[^9240]

*Troparion 4:* Knowing Thee to be adorned with diverse virtues and an illuminated temple of the Most High, O all-holy Virgin, we piously sing of Thee and hymn Thee in glory unto all ages.[^9241]

*My soul magnifies the Lord:*[^9242]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* O all-hymned one, higher than the heavens, who didst conceive without seed the unoriginate Word and bear incarnate God unto mankind: therefore do we all magnify Thee.[^9243]

*Troparion 1:* When the sun beheld Thee lifted up upon the Cross, O King of the ages, it was darkened, and the earth was shaken, and the veil of the temple was rent in its brightness, O Jesus all-powerful.[^9244]

*Troparion 2:* The lawless dug into Thy hands and Thy feet, and pierced Thy life-bearing side with a spear, and gave Thee gall with vinegar to drink, O my Christ, the Sweetness of all and true God.[^9245]

*For the Martyrs:* Crushed by every form of torment, they broke the snares of the author of evil; and having become crown-bearers in victory, the valiant sufferers are called blessed.[^9246]

*Another for the Martyrs:* The passion-bearers sanctified all the earth by the deposition of their relics, and quenched the foul stench of idol-sacrifices, commingling with fire and therein being mystically offered as whole burnt-offerings.[^9247]

*Glory... Both now: Stavrotheotokion:* O lover of goodness, holier than the Cherubim, who didst bear in the flesh God the Word of God...[^9248]

...Who was lifted up voluntarily upon the Cross: diligently entreat Him on behalf of all.[^9249]

*Canon to the Theotokos. The Heirmos the same:*[^9250]

*Troparion 1:* Humanity was all-perfectly assumed from Thee, O all-pure Maiden, the Word having united Himself unto ensouled flesh adorned with a rational soul; therefore do all we faithful magnify Thee.[^9251]

*Troparion 2:* Let the babbling of orators be silent, and let the apostolic trumpet sound forth, praising Thee with true voices, O Virgin, and proclaiming Thee to be truly the Theotokos.[^9252]

*Troparion 3:* By Thy sake hath humanity found mercy, being truly united hypostatically unto the Word, and by recompense made divine, O Virgin; therefore do we all ever magnify Thee.[^9253]

*Troparion 4:* Through prophetic keenness of sight the mystery of Thy childbearing was foreshown, O Maiden, in that Thou didst bear incarnate God unto mankind, Who delivers us from tribulations by Thy prayers.[^9254]

*It is truly meet: Litany, Exapostilarion, and the customary psalms.*[^9255]

*At the Aposticha, Stichera of the Cross, Tone 7:*[^9256]

*Sticheron 1:* Being Master, Lover of mankind, and Giver of life, Thou hast redeemed the universe by Thy Cross. O Lord, glory to Thee.[^9257]

*Verse:* We were filled in the morning with Thy mercy, O Lord, and we rejoiced: [and in all our days we were glad. Let us be glad for the days wherein Thou hast humbled us, for the years wherein we have seen evils.] And look upon Thy servants, and [upon Thy works,] and guide their sons.[^9258]

*Sticheron 2:* The true Vine was nailed to the Cross; the Gentiles took possession of Paradise with the thief. This is the glory of the Church! This is the wealth of the Kingdom! O Lord Who didst suffer for our sake, glory to Thee.[^9259]

*Verse:* And let the brightness of the Lord our God be upon us: [and the works of our hands do Thou direct upon us;] and the work of our hands do Thou direct.[^9260]

*For the Martyrs:* Celebrating the memory of Thy holy passion-bearers, we sing of Thee, O Christ, crying aloud: O Lord, glory to Thee.[^9261]

*Glory... Both now: Stavrotheotokion:* Crucifixion and slaughter as an evildoer didst Thou submit to, O Long-suffering One, that Thou mightest raise us up again by Thy Tree of the Cross, who were fallen by reason of the tree. But the Virgin who bore Thee was struck with dread, beholding Thee dead, and groaning cried aloud: O unrighteous fury! How do the lawless slay the Righteous One?[^9262]

*It is good to give praise: Trisagion, and after Our Father: Troparion, Litany, First Hour, and Dismissal.*[^9263]

*On Friday, The Beatitudes, Tone 7:*[^9264]

*Troparion 1:* Comely was the fruit and good for food that put me to death; but Christ is the Tree of Life, whereof eating I die not, but cry aloud with the thief: Remember me, O Lord, in Thy Kingdom.[^9265]

*Troparion 2:* Healing Adam's ailment, which was inflicted by the enemy's counsel, Thou wast lifted up upon the Tree, O King of all, and didst endure afflictions, being nailed in hands and feet; therefore do we glorify Thy long-suffering, O Word.[^9266]

*Troparion 3:* Between the two lawless ones the Jewish gathering crucified Thee, the only Lawgiver and Redeemer, Who deliverest mankind from every lawlessness, O Christ; therefore we sing of Thee.[^9267]

*For the Martyrs:* Bearing the wounds of many torments as comely adornments, O valiant sufferers, ye were recognized as a radiant adornment of the Church, ever praying for our souls.[^9268]

*Glory: Triadikon:* O Holy Trinity, deliver from torments Thy faithful servants who have believed in Thee, Who art in one Godhead unceasingly glorified with piety, and grant us Thine eternal Kingdom.[^9269]

*Both now: Stavrotheotokion:* Having neither form nor comeliness, beholding Thee, O Christ, lifted up in the flesh upon the Tree of the Cross, the all-pure Virgin cried out with pain: Woe is me! How have the lawless wounded Thee, O Child?[^9270]

## Saturday: All Saints and the Departed[^9271]

*On Friday Evening.*[^9272]

*On "Lord, I have cried," Stichera to the Holy Martyrs, Hierarchs, and Venerables, Tone 7:*[^9273]

*Tone 7, Podoben: "No longer are we forbidden" (Не ктомѹ̀ возбранѧ́еми є҆смы̀):*[^9274]

*Sticheron 1:* The martyrs drove away the darkness of godlessness, having shown forth the light of the knowledge of God unto all mankind.[^9275]

*Sticheron 2:* By Orthodox teachings, as with divine rays, ye illuminated the Church of the Lord, O all-wise shepherds.[^9276]

*Sticheron 3:* Ye shattered the snares of the demons, dwelling ever in trackless wildernesses, O venerable fathers.[^9277]

*Other Stichera, to the Martyrs, in the same Tone:*[^9278]

*Sticheron 4:* Put me not to shame, O Savior, when Thou comest to judge the whole world, for I have wrought shameful deeds.[^9279]

*Sticheron 5:* Glory to Thee, O Christ God, the boast of the Apostles and the joy of the Martyrs, whose proclamation was the consubstantial Trinity.[^9280]

*Sticheron 6:* O holy martyrs, who have fought the good fight and been crowned: pray unto the Lord that our souls may be saved.[^9281]

*Glory... Both now: Theotokion (Dogmatikon):* A Mother indeed wast Thou known to be beyond nature, O Theotokos, yet Thou didst remain a virgin beyond word and understanding; and the miracle of Thy childbearing tongue cannot express. For the conception being most glorious, O pure one, incomprehensible is the manner of Thy giving birth; for wherever God wills, the order of nature is overcome. Therefore, knowing Thee to be the Mother of God, we all earnestly beseech Thee: Pray that our souls may be saved.[^9282]

*O Gladsome Light: Prokeimenon of the day.*[^9283]

*At the Aposticha, Stichera to the Martyrs, Tone 7:*[^9284]

*Sticheron 1:* Taking no thought for any earthly things, O holy martyrs, and having manly preached Christ before the judgment seat, ye received from Him the recompense for your torments. But as ye have boldness, entreat Him as almighty God, we pray you, to save our souls who flee unto you.[^9285]

*Verse:* Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes, unto Thee that dwellest in heaven. [Behold, as the eyes of servants look unto the hands of their masters, as the eyes of the maiden unto the hands of her mistress,] so do our eyes look unto the Lord our God, until He take pity on us.[^9286]

*Sticheron 2:* O all-praised martyrs, spiritual sheep, rational whole burnt-offerings, a sacrifice acceptable and well-pleasing unto God: neither did the earth conceal you, but heaven received you. Ye became companions of the angels; with them pray, we beseech you, our Savior and God, to pacify the world and save our souls.[^9287]

*Verse:* Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us, for we are greatly filled with contempt; exceeding much hath our soul been filled with the reproach of them that are at ease, and with the contempt of the proud.[^9288]

*Sticheron 3 (Hymn for the Departed (Поко́инъ)):* In Thine own image and likeness having fashioned man in the beginning, O Lord, Thou didst place him in Paradise to rule over Thy creations; but deceived by the envy of the devil, he partook of the food, becoming a transgressor of Thy commandments. Therefore back into the earth...[^9289]

...whence he was taken, didst Thou condemn him to return, O Lord, and to ask for rest.[^9290]
