[^8283]: Physical leaf p773. Troparion 1 of Ode 1: contrasting the sublime angelic praise of the Mother of God with the humble, compunctionate prayer offered from human clay lips.
[^8284]: Physical leaf p773. Troparion 2 of Ode 1: petition unto the Mother of Life to put to death the living lusts of the flesh and grant deliverance from sinful habits.
[^8285]: Physical leaf p773. Glory... Troparion 3 of Ode 1: the eternal Word as "Sun from the Sun" (Со́лнце ѿ Со́лнца) shining forth in time from the Virgin to illumine and instruct human souls.
[^8286]: Physical leaf p773. Both now... Theotokion of Ode 1: perpetual praise due unto the Theotokos as the sure refuge of mortals in worldly tribulation.
[^8287]: Physical leaf p773. Ode 3. Heirmos: the creation and establishment of the heavens by the Word and the Holy Spirit; petition to be grounded upon the unshakable rock of the confession of Christ.
[^8288]: Physical leaf p773. Troparion 1 of Ode 3: rendering grateful homage unto the Mother of the Word and praying for deliverance from worldly afflictions.
[^8289]: Physical leaf p773. Troparion 2 of Ode 3: the restoration of fallen Adam to eternal life through the birth of the Life-originating Word; petition for deliverance from deadly sin.
[^8290]: Physical leaf p774 (printed fol. 774, ѱѻ҃д). Glory... Troparion 3 of Ode 3: the internal warfare between the carnal law of passions and the spiritual law of God (cf. Rom. 7:22–23); petition for the peace of dispassion (apatheia).
[^8291]: Physical leaf p774. Both now... Theotokion of Ode 3: the conception of Christ freeing mortals from the subterranean gates of hades; prayer to rend the ropes of corruption.
[^8292]: Physical leaf p774. Ode 4. Heirmos: the prophetic vision of Christ's incarnation without separation from the Father's bosom (Habakkuk 3:2).
[^8293]: Physical leaf p774. Troparion 1 of Ode 4: spiritual struggle against nocturnal darkness and sorrow; petition unto the "Door of Light" for illumination.
[^8294]: Physical leaf p774. Troparion 2 of Ode 4: the soul bound in chains of multiple sins and trials calling upon the Virgin's unfailing protection.
[^8295]: Physical leaf p774. Glory... Troparion 3 of Ode 4: petition for the confounding of wicked, envious, and deceitful conspiracies against the servant of God.
[^8296]: Physical leaf p774. Both now... Theotokion of Ode 4: prayer to cut off slanderous and deceitful tongues with the sharp sword of the Theotokos's intercessions.
[^8297]: Physical leaf p774. Ode 5. Heirmos: night of blindness for the unbelieving, but dawn and sweetness of the divine words for the faithful; early morning hymnody of Christ's Godhead.
[^8298]: Physical leaf p774. Troparion 1 of Ode 5: the Virgin as an impregnable rampart and protective shelter against grievous tribulations.
[^8299]: Physical leaf p774. Troparion 2 of Ode 5: lamentation over debilitating spiritual and physical weakness, seeking total restoration through the prayers of the Mother of God.
[^8300]: Physical leaf p774. Glory... Troparion 3 of Ode 5: seeking refuge in the ocean of divine tender-heartedness against unjust oppression.
[^8301]: Physical leaf p774. Both now... Theotokion of Ode 5 (opens on p774, concluding on p775): extolling the universal power of the Virgin's intercession to rescue the soul from the storms of life.
[^8302]: Physical leaf p775 (printed fol. 775, ѱѻ҃є). Conclusion of Theotokion of Ode 5 from p774: "...deliver me from every assault of this present life" (мѧ всѧ́кагѡ и҆зба́ви жите́йскагѡ настоѧ́нїа).
[^8303]: Physical leaf p775. Ode 6. Heirmos: the allegorical storm of worldly anxieties and shipwreck of sin; invoking the prayer of Jonah from the belly of the sea monster (Jonah 2:3–10).
[^8304]: Physical leaf p775. Troparion 1 of Ode 6: the universal debt of human transgression blotted out by Christ's nativity; the Mother of God receiving the hymns of her suppliants.
[^8305]: Physical leaf p775. Troparion 2 of Ode 6: the soul wounded by the sharp briers of carnal passions, seeking the divine medicine of healing from the Theotokos.
[^8306]: Physical leaf p775. Glory... Troparion 3 of Ode 6: human nature elevated unto the heavenly realm through Mary who became the living palace of the King of Glory.
[^8307]: Physical leaf p775. Both now... Theotokion of Ode 6: the supernatural incarnation of the Word freeing mankind from irrationality (alogia); glorified in ceaseless divine songs.
[^8308]: Physical leaf p775. Rubric: threefold Kyrie eleison, Glory... Both now... preceding the Sessional Hymn.
[^8309]: Physical leaf p775. Sessional Hymn at Compline (Tone 7): dramatic plea acknowledging that human transgressions have provoked the righteous wrath of the Son, beseeching the Mother of God to intercede and turn His anger into mercy.
[^8310]: Physical leaf p775. Ode 7. Heirmos: the Three Holy Youths in the Babylonian furnace turning the raging flame into refreshing dew through their continuous hymnody (Dan. 3:49–52).
[^8311]: Physical leaf p775. Troparion 1 of Ode 7: the Maiden adorned with the fringes of purity (cf. Ps. 44:14 LXX) transforming the defilement of the soul into divine beauty.
[^8312]: Physical leaf p775. Troparion 2 of Ode 7: consuming the ferocious fires of demonic temptation through the bright rays of Mary's compassionate intercession.
[^8313]: Physical leaf p776 (printed fol. 776, ѱѻ҃ѕ). Glory... Troparion 3 of Ode 7: ceaseless maternal intercession before Christ to extricate His servants from human suffering and calamities.
[^8314]: Physical leaf p776. Both now... Theotokion of Ode 7: the transcendent Son of God assuming human flesh immutably (непрело́жно) from the Virgin womb for the redemption of mankind.
[^8315]: Physical leaf p776. Ode 8. Heirmos: the bedewed Children in the furnace summoning all created things to praise the Lord Who framed the universe by His Word (Song of the Three Children / Dan. 3:57ff.).
[^8316]: Physical leaf p776. Troparion 1 of Ode 8: deliverance from the seductive pastures of worldly passions and the manifold snares of temptation.
[^8317]: Physical leaf p776. Troparion 2 of Ode 8: prayer for the mighty right hand of the Sovereign Lady to avert anticipated human misfortunes.
[^8318]: Physical leaf p776. Glory... Troparion 3 of Ode 8: destruction of malicious snares and traps laid by wicked and treacherous men.
[^8319]: Physical leaf p776. Both now... Theotokion of Ode 8: the Virgin hailed as the sole divine haven (приста́нище) for the faithful tossed upon the tempestuous sea of life.
[^8320]: Physical leaf p776. Ode 9. Heirmos: festive Marian acclamations: radiant bride, mother of the Light-giver, dwelling of the Uncontainable One, and more honorable than the Cherubim.
[^8321]: Physical leaf p776. Troparion 1 of Ode 9: penitential confession of having strayed like an irrational beast from God's commandments, begging to be restrained by the bridle of the Virgin's prayers.
[^8322]: Physical leaf p776. Troparion 2 of Ode 9: warning against spiritual degradation and relapse into habitual sin, seeking deliverance from worse corruption.
[^8323]: Physical leaf p776. Glory... Troparion 3 of Ode 9: Mary revealed as the primal remedy of the human race, healing the ancient rupture of Adam and Eve.
[^8324]: Physical leaf p776. Both now... Theotokion of Ode 9: petition for the spiritual virtues of a chaste mind (цѣломꙋ́дренъ оу҆́мъ), a pure heart, and watchful vigilance to observe God's commandments.
[^8325]: Physical leaf p776. Rubric: the hymn to the Theotokos "It is truly meet" (Досто́йно є҆́сть / Axion Estin), Trisagion prayers, and concluding dismissal of Compline.
[^8326]: Physical leaf p777 (printed fol. 777, ѱѻ҃є). Rubric: Monday Matins (Въ Понедѣ́лникъ Оу҆́трѧ), opening the morning office dedicated to repentance and the Holy Angels.
[^8327]: Physical leaf p777. Rubric: Penitential Sessional Hymns in Tone 7 following the First Kathisma / Stichologia of the Psalter.
[^8328]: Physical leaf p777. Sessional Hymn 1: the soul approaching Christ the Physician with the medicine of repentance, seeking to be numbered among the forgiven sinners (harlot, thief, and publican).
[^8329]: Physical leaf p777. Scriptural verse: Psalm 6:2 (LXX versification; Masoretic Ps. 6:1): "O Lord, rebuke me not in Thine anger, nor chasten me in Thy wrath."
[^8330]: Physical leaf p777. Sessional Hymn 2: Christ the Lover of mankind Who accepted Peter's bitter tears of repentance (Matt. 26:75) and justified the publican for his humble sighing (Luke 18:13).
[^8331]: Physical leaf p777. Glory... Both now... Theotokion: the Virgin exalted above the celestial hosts as the living temple who gave birth to Christ the Savior.
[^8332]: Physical leaf p777. Rubric: Penitential Sessional Hymns in Tone 7 following the Second Kathisma / Stichologia.
[^8333]: Physical leaf p777. Sessional Hymn 1: the conscience lamenting its spiritual blindness and failure to imitate the contrition of the publican or the tears of the sinful woman.
[^8334]: Physical leaf p777. Scriptural verse: Psalm 6:2 (LXX versification).
[^8335]: Physical leaf p777. Sessional Hymn 2: the soul engulfed by the waves of lawlessness, crying for Christ's outstretched hand even as Peter was rescued from sinking in the Sea of Galilee (Matt. 14:30–31).
[^8336]: Physical leaf p777. Scriptural verse: Psalm 67:36 (LXX versification; Masoretic Ps. 68:35): "God is marvelous in His Saints, the God of Israel."
[^8337]: Physical leaf p777. Martyrion: intercession of the Holy Martyrs for remission of sins and rescue from eternal, bitter death.

[^8978]: Canon to St Nicholas, Tone 7, Ode 1. Heirmos ('Поспѣ́шествовавшемꙋ Бг҃ꙋ въ Є҆гѵ́птѣ Мѡѷсе́ови...').
[^8979]: Canon to St Nicholas, Ode 1, Troparion 1 ('Просла́влено на землѝ преше́дъ житїѐ...').
[^8980]: Canon to St Nicholas, Ode 1, Troparion 2 ('Въ пꙋчи́нѣ лю́тыхъ пла́вающе...').
[^8981]: Canon to St Nicholas, Ode 1, Troparion 3 ('Рꙋ́кꙋ мнѣ̀ ны́нѣ пода́тельнꙋю по́мощи пода́ждь...').
[^8982]: Canon to St Nicholas, Ode 1, Theotokion ('И҆́же невеще́ственныѧ слꙋги̑ сотвори́въ...').

[^8984]: Canon to the Apostles, Ode 3, Troparion 1 ('Ꙗ҆́кѡ небеса̀ сла́вꙋ Бж҃їю, ꙗ҆́кѡже речѐ проро́къ...').

[^8986]: Canon to the Apostles, Ode 3, Troparion 3 ('Прельще́наго мѧ̀ лю́тѣ, и҆ ѻ҆де́жды ѡ҆бнаже́наго бж҃е́ственныѧ...').

[^8996]: Canon to the Apostles, Ode 4, Troparion 2 ('Къ созида́телемъ всѣ́хъ страсте́й, впа́дся ᲂу҆мовре́днѡ ѻ҆каѧ́нный а҆́зъ...').
[^8997]: Canon to the Apostles, Ode 4, Troparion 3 ('Ѿ страсте́й и҆зба́вите на́съ, и҆ ѿ бѣ́дъ лю́тыхъ и҆ скорбе́й...').

[^9000]: Canon to St Nicholas, Ode 4, Troparion 1 ('Премꙋ́дростїю твое́ю, ᲂу҆черни́въ А҆́рїевъ ᲂу҆́мъ є҆ресїю помраче́ный...').
[^9001]: Canon to St Nicholas, Ode 4, Troparion 2 ('Оу҆врачꙋ́й моѧ̑ мнѡ́гїа стрꙋ́пы бг҃опрїя́тными твои́ми моли́твами...').
[^9002]: Canon to St Nicholas, Ode 4, Troparion 3 ('Оу҆мертви́въ стра̑стнаѧ взыгра̑нїа...').
[^9003]: Canon to St Nicholas, Ode 4, Theotokion ('Родила̀ є҆сѝ пло́тїю Безлѣ́тнаго...').
[^9004]: Canon to the Apostles, Ode 5. Heirmos ('Гдⷭ҇и Бж҃е мо́й, ѿ но́щи ᲂу҆́треневавъ тебѣ̀ молю́ся...').
[^9005]: Canon to the Apostles, Ode 5, Troparion 1 ('Гдⷭ҇и Бж҃е мо́й, А҆́пⷭ҇толѡмъ твои̑мъ ми́ръ дре́вле дарова́вый...').
[^9006]: Canon to the Apostles, Ode 5, Troparion 2 ('Гдⷭ҇и Бж҃е мо́й, свѣ́дый согрѣше́нїа дꙋшѝ моеѧ̀...').

[^9010]: Canon to St Nicholas, Ode 5. Heirmos ('Оу҆́тренюетъ дꙋ́хъ мо́й къ Бг҃ꙋ тебѣ́ же, занѐ свѣ́тъ є҆сѝ...').
[^9011]: Canon to St Nicholas, Ode 5, Troparion 1 ('Совѣ́ты ѻ҆́тче подвиза́ємыѧ при́снѡ на ны̀...').
[^9012]: Canon to St Nicholas, Ode 5, Troparion 2 ('Сою́зы ѕѡ́лъ на́шихъ расто́ргни твои́ми моли́твами...').

[^9014]: Canon to St Nicholas, Ode 5, Theotokion ('Марі́е, Госпожѐ всеѧ̀ тва́ри, ѿ врага̀ безстꙋ́днѡ ѡ҆блада́ти и҆́щꙋщагѡ...').

[^9020]: Canon to St Nicholas, Ode 6. Heirmos ('І҆ѡ́на и҆зъ чре́ва а҆́дова вопія́ше...').
[^9021]: Canon to St Nicholas, Ode 6, Troparion 1; Physical Leaf p828 = Printed Folio 818 ('Разори́лъ є҆сѝ сме́рти непра́ведный сꙋ́дъ, и҆ спа́слъ є҆сѝ ми́лостїю твое́ю, О҆́тче Нїко́лае...').
[^9022]: Canon to St Nicholas, Ode 6, Troparion 2 ('Недꙋ́ги дꙋ́шъ на́шихъ ᲂу҆ти́ши...').
[^9023]: Canon to St Nicholas, Ode 6, Troparion 3 ('Па́жить го́рькꙋю ѕлоче́стїа А҆́рїева потреби́лъ є҆сѝ Нїко́лае...').
[^9024]: Canon to St Nicholas, Ode 6, Theotokion ('Оу҆врачꙋ́й Всенепоро́чнаѧ, неисцѣ́льнѡ болѧ́щꙋю ѻ҆каѧ́ннꙋю дꙋ́шꙋ мою̀...').

[^9030]: Canon to St Nicholas, Ode 7. Heirmos ('Пе́щь горѧ́щꙋю ѡ҆роси́лъ є҆сѝ Спа́се...').

[^9034]: Canon to St Nicholas, Ode 7, Theotokion ('Вся́къ ꙗ҆зы́къ вѣ́рою сла́витъ тѧ̀ Чи́стаѧ...').
[^9035]: Canon to the Apostles, Ode 8. Heirmos ('Неѡпа́льнаѧ ѻ҆гню̀ въ Сїна́и прича́щшаѧся кꙋпина̀...').

[^9329]: *Ode 8. Heirmos:* Based on the Song of the Three Holy Children (Dan. 3:57–88).
[^9330]: *Troparion 1:* Supplication to the Most Holy Theotokos for cleansing of sins, salvation at death, and rest after demise.
[^9331]: *Troparion 2:* Contemplation of the fearful examination, the inexorable Judge, and endless torments.
[^9332]: *Glory:* Troparion 3 of Ode 8, addressing the Mother of God as the cause of salvation for those born of earth.
[^9333]: *Both now:* Troparion 4 of Ode 8, offering supplications as a gift to the Master through the Theotokos.
[^9334]: *Ode 9. Heirmos:* Professing the mystery of divine condescension in the virginal childbearing of the Mother of God.
[^9335]: *Troparion 1:* Fleeing beneath the protection of the Virgin from the storm of sins.
[^9336]: *Troparion 2:* Entreaty to the Creator incarnate of the Virgin's pure blood; text continues across the leaf boundary onto physical leaf p852 (printed fol. 845).
[^9337]: Conclusion of Troparion 2 of Ode 9 of the Friday Compline Canon, continued from physical leaf p851 (printed fol. 844).
[^9338]: *Glory:* Troparion 3 of Ode 9, petitioning deliverance from the unquenchable fire, outer darkness, and eternal weeping.
[^9339]: *Both now:* Troparion 4 of Ode 9, professing protection beneath the grace of the Mother of God.
[^9340]: Closing rubrics of Small Compline on Friday evening: It is truly meet, Trisagion, and after the Our Father, the troparia and the rest as usual.
[^9341]: Opening rubric for Saturday Matins in Tone 7. Printed text: 'Въ Сꙋббѡ́тѹ Оу҆́трѧ'.
[^9342]: Rubric for the Sessional Hymns to the Martyrs after the first Kathisma reading at Saturday Matins, Tone 7.
[^9343]: *Sessional Hymn 1:* Supplication to the holy martyrs for remission of sins and deliverance from bitter death.
[^9344]: Scriptural verse for the martyrs: Psalm 67:36 (LXX).
[^9345]: *Sessional Hymn 2:* Commemorating the martyrs who struggled on earth, overcame the enemy, and abolished the deceit of idols; text spans columns on physical leaf p852 (printed fol. 845).
[^9346]: *Glory... Both now: Theotokion:* Addressing the Virgin as the treasury of our resurrection and ever-virgin before, in, and after childbearing.
[^9347]: Rubric for the Sessional Hymns after the second Kathisma reading at Saturday Matins, Tone 7.
[^9348]: *Sessional Hymn 1:* Exhortation to the righteous and heavens to rejoice at the victory of the holy martyrs.
[^9349]: Scriptural verse for the martyrs: Psalm 67:36 (LXX).
[^9350]: *Sessional Hymn 2:* To the holy martyrs armed with the power of the Cross; text continues across the leaf boundary onto physical leaf p853 (printed fol. 846).
[^9351]: Conclusion of Sessional Hymn 2 after the second Kathisma reading, continued from physical leaf p852 (printed fol. 845).
[^9352]: Scriptural verse for the righteous: Psalm 33:20 (LXX).
[^9353]: *Sessional Hymn 3 (Hymn for the Departed (Поко́инъ)):* Commemorating Christ's victory over Hades and praying for the departed.
[^9354]: *Glory... Both now: Theotokion:* Supplication to the unwedded Maiden together with the Prophets, Hierarchs, and Martyrs.
[^9355]: Heading for the First Canon at Saturday Matins, Tone 7, dedicated to the Holy Martyrs, Hierarchs, Venerables, and the Departed.
[^9356]: Acrostic heading in the original Greek: 'Съ ли́ки воспѣва́ю Па́стырей и҆ Мч҃никъ, а҆́зъ І҆ѡ́сифъ' (With choirs I sing of shepherds and martyrs, I, Joseph), indicating the hymnographic authorship of St. Joseph the Hymnographer.
[^9357]: *Tone 7. Ode 1. Heirmos:* Recalling God's assistance unto Moses in Egypt to lead Israel forth across the Red Sea.
[^9358]: Refrain to the Holy Martyrs: 'Ст҃іи Мч҃ници, моли́те Бг҃а ѡ҆ на́съ'.
[^9359]: *Troparion 1 (To the Martyrs):* The choir of guileless martyrs overcoming all the deceptions of the adversary.
[^9360]: Refrain to the Holy Hierarchs: 'Святи́телїе Хрⷭ҇тѡ́ви, моли́те Бг҃а ѡ҆ на́съ'.
[^9361]: *Troparion 2 (To the Hierarchs and Venerables):* Commemorating the Hierarchs and Venerables who attained eternal delight by divine grace.
[^9362]: Refrain to All the Saints: 'Всѝ Ст҃іи, моли́те Бг҃а ѡ҆ на́съ'.
[^9363]: *Troparion 3 (To All Saints):* Petition for deliverance from wrath through the prayers of the Prophets, Venerables, and holy women.
[^9364]: Refrain for the Departed: 'Оу҆поко́й Гдⷭ҇и дѹ́ши ѹ҆со́пшихъ рабѡ́въ твои́хъ'.
[^9365]: *Troparion 4 (For the Departed):* Prayer unto the divine Word Who fashioned man from the earth to grant rest to the departed faithful.
[^9366]: *Theotokion:* Addressing the all-pure Mother who bore the most holy Word of God.
[^9367]: Heading for the Second Canon at Saturday Matins, Tone 7, dedicated to the Departed.
[^9368]: Attributed in the title rubric to St. Theophanes the Branded ('Творе́ніе Ѳеофа́ново').
[^9369]: *Tone 7. Ode 1. Heirmos:* Recalling the dry-shod passage of Israel across the sea; text continues across the leaf boundary onto physical leaf p854 (printed fol. 847).
[^9370]: Conclusion of Ode 1 Heirmos of the Second Canon (to the Departed), continued from physical leaf p853 (printed fol. 846).
[^9371]: Scriptural verse for the martyrs: Psalm 67:36 (LXX).
[^9372]: *Troparion 1 (To the Martyrs):* The martyrs offered unto Christ as a dedicated offering of virtue and piety.
[^9373]: Liturgical verse for the departed: 'Give rest, O Lord, to the souls of Thy departed servants.'
[^9374]: *Troparion 2 (For the Departed):* Supplication for the departed to attain the unapproachable, thrice-sunned dawn where sickness and sorrow are banished.
[^9375]: *Glory:* Troparion 3 for the departed, addressing Christ as hypostatic Life Who raised up mortal human nature.
[^9376]: *Both now: Theotokion:* The Mother of God setting upright the ancestral fall by bearing the Lord of life.
[^9377]: *Ode 3. Heirmos:* Thou Who didst establish the heavens by Thy word and the earth upon the waters.
[^9378]: Refrain to the Holy Martyrs.
[^9379]: *Troparion 1 (To the Martyrs):* The martyrs crushed with stones yet unbroken in mind.
[^9380]: Refrain to the Holy Hierarchs.
[^9381]: *Troparion 2 (To the Hierarchs):* The Hierarchs dispelling the mist of heresies by the radiance of divine dogmas.
[^9382]: Refrain to All the Saints.
[^9383]: *Troparion 3 (To the Venerables):* The venerable fathers who died to the world and inherited heavenly life.
[^9384]: Refrain for the Departed.
[^9385]: *Troparion 4 (For the Departed):* Intercession for those translated in faith and hope at the hour of judgment.
[^9386]: *Glory... Both now: Theotokion:* The Beginningless Word held as an infant in the Virgin's womb without departing from the Father's bosom.
[^9387]: *Canon to the Departed. Ode 3. Heirmos:* In the beginning having established the heavens by Thy all-powerful Word.
[^9388]: *Troparion 1 (To the Martyrs):* The youthful and manly endurance of the choirs of martyrs.
[^9389]: *Troparion 2 (For the Departed):* Prayer to settle the departed in Abraham's bosom and number them with Lazarus.
[^9390]: *Glory:* Intercession for those translated in piety to partake of the immaterial light and divine glory.
[^9391]: *Both now: Theotokion:* The dissolution of nature's laws in the conception of the incomprehensible Word; text spans columns on physical leaf p855 (printed fol. 848).
[^9392]: *Ode 4. Heirmos:* Prophet Habakkuk perceiving the Lord's coming in the flesh (Hab. 3:2).
[^9393]: *Troparion 1 (To the Martyrs):* The martyrs enfeebling the enemy's power through divine love.
[^9394]: *Troparion 2 (To the Hierarchs):* Hierarchs presiding as rational sheep over Christ's rational flock.
[^9395]: *Troparion 3 (To the Venerables):* Venerable fathers shining as brilliant stars before the assembly of the faithful.
[^9396]: *Glory:* The valiant women and the assembly of holy Prophets attaining heavenly blessings.
[^9397]: *Both now: Theotokion:* Petition to the Mother of God to deliver her flock from temptations and tribulations.
[^9398]: *Canon to the Departed. Ode 4. Heirmos:* Not leaving the Father's bosom and descending to earth; text continues across the leaf boundary onto physical leaf p856 (printed fol. 849).
[^9399]: Conclusion of Ode 4 Heirmos of the Second Canon (to the Departed), continued from physical leaf p855 (printed fol. 848).
[^9400]: *Troparion 1 (To the Martyrs):* Martyrs adorned with crowns of righteousness through their patient suffering.
[^9401]: *Troparion 2 (For the Departed):* Prayer for the departed to be counted worthy of supernatural brilliance at Christ's coming with the angels.
[^9402]: *Glory:* Supplication to grant the departed delight in the thrice-radiant dawn and the single shining of the Godhead.
[^9403]: *Both now: Theotokion:* Christ dwelling in the virginal womb and delivering from death those who bless His Mother.
[^9404]: *Ode 5. Heirmos:* Rising early unto God Whose light and commandments bring healing unto His servants (cf. Isa. 26:9).
[^9405]: *Troparion 1 (To the Martyrs):* Valiant sufferers healing the passions of all through their own bodily wounds; text spans columns on physical leaf p856 (printed fol. 849).
[^9406]: *Troparion 2 (To the Hierarchs):* Hierarchs exercising the power to bind and loose upon earth (Matt. 18:18).
[^9407]: *Troparion 3 (To the Ascetics, Prophets, and Righteous):* Choirs of ascetics, prophets, righteous, and holy women rejoicing with indestructible gladness.
[^9408]: *Glory: Hymn for the Departed (Поко́инъ):* Supplication for the departed to be made citizens of Paradise.
[^9409]: *Both now: Theotokion:* Addressing the Virgin as the indestructible bridal chamber of God.
[^9410]: *Canon to the Departed. Ode 5. Heirmos:* Rising early from the night to seek remission of sins and guidance toward the divine light.
[^9411]: *Troparion 1 (To the Martyrs):* Martyrs clothed in life-bearing death interceding for the departed; text continues across the leaf boundary onto physical leaf p857 (printed fol. 850).
[^9412]: Conclusion of Troparion 1 of Ode 5 of the Second Canon (to the Departed), continued from physical leaf p856 (printed fol. 849).
[^9413]: *Troparion 2 (For the Departed):* Beseeching the Savior to settle the departed with His firstborn in heavenly mansions.
[^9414]: *Glory:* Prayer for the departed servants who have put off bodily heaviness and passed to the life on high.
[^9415]: *Both now: Theotokion:* Supplication to the Mother of God, who bore the hypostatic Life, for pardon of transgressions.
[^9416]: *Ode 6. Heirmos:* Jonah crying from the belly of Hades (Jonah 2:3).
[^9417]: *Troparion 1 (To the Martyrs):* Martyrs casting down demonic pride through their sufferings and becoming citizens of heaven.
[^9418]: *Troparion 2 (To the Hierarchs):* Hierarchs dispelling the winter of heresies and leading the faithful into the spiritual spring; text spans columns on physical leaf p857 (printed fol. 850).
[^9419]: *Troparion 3 (To the Venerables, Prophets, and Holy Women):* Saints justified by Christ's strength and delighting in the unwaning light.
[^9420]: *Glory: Hymn for the Departed (Поко́инъ):* Intercession for those translated from temporal life to partake of eternal joy.
[^9421]: *Both now: Theotokion:* The most holy Virgin sanctifying her servants by bearing the most holy Word.
[^9422]: *Canon to the Departed. Ode 6. Heirmos:* The soul tossed on the sea of worldly cares crying with Jonah to be delivered from the deep.
[^9423]: *Troparion 1 (To the Martyrs):* Martyrs inheriting painless delight and crowns of righteousness; prescribed to be chanted twice ('Два́жды').
[^9424]: *Glory:* Prayer to rank the departed among the Righteous and angelic choirs; text continues across the leaf boundary onto physical leaf p858 (printed fol. 851).
[^9425]: Conclusion of the Glory Troparion of Ode 6 of the Second Canon (to the Departed), continued from physical leaf p857 (printed fol. 850).
[^9426]: *Both now: Theotokion:* Christ proceeding from the Virgin's womb by divine will to enlighten those in death's shadow.
[^9427]: *Ode 7. Heirmos:* The children of Abraham rejoicing in the Chaldean furnace with the Angel (Dan. 3:28).
[^9428]: *Troparion 1 (To the Martyrs):* Passion-bearers quenching the flame of impiety by their streams of blood.
[^9429]: *Troparion 2 (To the Hierarchs):* Praise of the Hierarchs as enlighteners of the inhabited world.
[^9430]: *Troparion 3 (To the Prophets and Venerables):* Honoring the holy assembly of Prophets and Venerable fathers.
[^9431]: *Glory: Hymn for the Departed (Поко́инъ):* Deliverance from Gehenna for the departed faithful servants.
[^9432]: *Both now: Theotokion:* Praising the Theotokos as more honorable than the Angels.
[^9433]: *Canon to the Departed. Ode 7. Heirmos:* The children showing the fiery furnace of old to be dew-dropping.
[^9434]: *Troparion 1 (To the Martyrs):* Crowned martyrs standing in spirit around Christ the King with the angelic hosts.
[^9435]: *Troparion 2 (For the Departed):* Entreating Christ Who holds authority over life and death to grant divine delight to the departed.
[^9436]: *Glory:* Prayer to settle the departed in heavenly tabernacles in the light of immaterial brilliance.
[^9437]: *Both now: Theotokion:* Christ born motherless of the Father and fatherless of the Virgin; text continues across the leaf boundary onto physical leaf p859 (printed fol. 852).
[^9438]: Conclusion of the Theotokion of Ode 7 of the Second Canon (to the Departed), continued from physical leaf p858 (printed fol. 851).
[^9439]: *Ode 8. Heirmos:* Imitating the youths in the furnace and blessing the Lord (Dan. 3:57).
[^9440]: *Troparion 1 (To the Martyrs):* Overthrow of idolatrous temples and becoming temples of the Holy Spirit.
[^9441]: *Troparion 2 (To the Hierarchs):* Hierarchs blossoming as sweet-scented flowers of pious doctrine.
[^9442]: *Troparion 3 (To the Venerables):* Ascetics living as strangers upon earth and seeking the heavenly heritage.
[^9443]: *Glory: Hymn for the Departed (Поко́инъ):* Supplication unto Christ, Lord of living and dead, to unite the departed with the choir of the saved.
[^9444]: *Both now: Theotokion:* The assembly of holy women who sought the Lord by fasting and martyrdom praying to the Mother of God.
[^9445]: *Canon to the Departed. Ode 8. Heirmos:* Hymning the only beginningless King of glory Whom the hosts of heaven praise.
[^9446]: *Troparion 1 (To the Martyrs):* Martyrs disregarding earthly glory in contemplation of Christ's heavenly kingdom.
[^9447]: *Troparion 2 (For the Departed):* Prayer for Christ Who dissolved the earthly tabernacle to grant a heavenly dwelling to the departed.
[^9448]: *Glory:* Supplication to the fountain of life to refresh the departed with rivers of delight.
[^9449]: *Both now: Theotokion:* The Virgin receiving the unapproachable Light and illuminating those in worldly darkness.
[^9450]: Magnificat incipit ('Вели́читъ дѹша̀ моѧ̀ Гдⷭ҇а:') and *Ode 9. Heirmos:* Magnifying the Theotokos as Mother beyond nature and pure Virgin according to nature.
[^9451]: *Troparion 1 (To All Saints):* Petition for mercy through the prayers of the Holy Martyrs, Prophets, and the Righteous of all ages.
[^9452]: *Troparion 2 (To the Hierarchs):* Sacred ministers joined with the heavenly hosts interceding for us.
[^9453]: *Troparion 3 (To the Holy Ascetics and Women):* Honoring the monastic ranks and holy women who finished the blessed contest.
[^9454]: *Glory: Hymn for the Departed (Поко́инъ):* Intercession for the departed Orthodox Christians to share the glory of the saints.
[^9455]: *Both now: Theotokion:* Trembling before the judgment seat and imploring the Mother of God for preservation without condemnation.
[^9456]: *Canon to the Departed. Ode 9. Heirmos:* The mystery of the divine condescension in the virginal birth of Christ.
[^9457]: *Troparion 1 (To the Martyrs):* Valiant martyrs shining as pillars of faith and bulwarks of the Church; text spans columns on physical leaf p860 (printed fol. 853).
[^9458]: *Troparion 2 (For the Departed):* Supplication to Christ Who tore up the bond of sins with the spear of His side to deliver the departed from eternal fire.
[^9459]: *Glory:* Beseeching the good and compassionate Savior to grant incorruptible sweetness to the departed.
[^9460]: *Both now: Theotokion:* The fulfillment of the Law and the Prophets in Christ, born in two natures of the Ever-Virgin.
[^9461]: Closing rubrics of Saturday Matins: It is truly meet, litany, Photogogikon (Exapostilarion), and the customary psalms.
[^9462]: Rubric for the Aposticha Stichera to the Martyrs at Saturday Matins, Tone 7.
[^9463]: *Sticheron 1:* Celebrating the memory of the holy passion-bearers; text continues across the cohort boundary onto physical leaf p861 (printed fol. 854).
[^9464]: Concluding phrase of the first Sticheron to the Martyrs at the Aposticha of Saturday Matins, Tone 7, completed from physical leaf p860.
[^9465]: Second Sticheron to the Martyrs at the Aposticha of Saturday Matins, Tone 7.
[^9466]: Third Sticheron to the Martyrs at the Aposticha of Saturday Matins, Tone 7.
[^9467]: Fourth Sticheron to the Martyrs at the Aposticha of Saturday Matins, Tone 7.
[^9468]: Hymn for the Departed (Поко́инъ) (Sticheron to the Departed), Tone 7.
[^9469]: Doxastikon (Glory) to the Departed at the Aposticha of Saturday Matins, Tone 7.
[^9470]: Theotokion (Both now) at the Aposticha of Saturday Matins, Tone 7.

[^9472]: First Sticheron to the Departed in Tone 7.
[^9473]: Psalm verse: LXX Ps. 64:5 (*Блаже́ни, ꙗ҆́же и҆збра́лъ, и҆ прїѧ́лъ є҆сѝ Гдⷭ҇и* / *Blessed are they whom Thou hast chosen and taken to Thyself, O Lord*).
[^9474]: Second Sticheron to the Departed in Tone 7, opening on physical leaf p861 and continuing onto physical leaf p862.
[^9475]: Conclusion of the second Sticheron to the Departed, completed from physical leaf p861.
[^9476]: Psalm verse: LXX Ps. 24:13 (*Дꙋ́ши и҆́хъ во благи́хъ водворѧ́тсѧ* / *Their souls shall dwell amid good things*).
[^9477]: Third Sticheron to the Departed in Tone 7.
[^9478]: Theotokion (Glory... both now...) concluding the Aposticha to the Departed, Tone 7.
[^9479]: Rubric for the Beatitudes (Blazhenna) on Saturday in Tone 7 (*Въ Сꙋббѡ́тꙋ Блаже́нна, Гла́съ з҃*).
[^9480]: First Troparion of the Saturday Beatitudes, Tone 7.
[^9481]: Second Troparion of the Saturday Beatitudes, Tone 7 (to the holy Martyrs).
[^9482]: Third Troparion of the Saturday Beatitudes, Tone 7 (to the Hierarchs, Venerable, and Righteous).
[^9483]: Fourth Troparion of the Saturday Beatitudes, Tone 7 (for the Departed).

[^9485]: Conclusion of the Triadikon (Glory) of the Saturday Beatitudes, completed from physical leaf p862.
[^9486]: Theotokion (Both now) of the Saturday Beatitudes in Tone 7.
[^9487]: Codicological note: An engraved ornamental baroque vignette with floral scrollwork and arabesques marks the formal conclusion of the liturgical cycle of Tone 7 on physical leaf p863 (printed fol. 856).
[^9488]: Codicological note: An engraved ornamental baroque headpiece frieze with medallions depicting the Annunciation, the Crucifixion of Christ, and the Mother of God with the Christ Child, amidst ornate arabesques and foliate scrollwork, marks the formal opening of Tone 8 on physical leaf p864 (printed fol. 856 verso).
